We propose a diffusion based multi-class normality framework in which a single class-conditional ControlNet is trained exclusively on authentic CDPs from multiple P\&D classes and detects counterfeits through reconstruction error under authentic-class conditioning. We further introduce dual template masking, which hides complementary regions of the input template and scores only withheld pixels, reducing reliance on visible binary structure. On the Indigo 1 x 1 Base dataset, the proposed method outperforms traditional and adapted generative baselines under multi-class authentic-versus-counterfeit evaluation, without using counterfeit samples for training or threshold calibration.

</details>


### [71] [Identifying Latent Concepts and Structures for Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00620)
*Boyang Dai,Chaoqi Chen,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Compositional Primitive Fields (CPF-GCD)，一个即插即用的表示学习框架，通过在backbone与分类头之间插入低秩组合原语场机制，将高秩纠缠的token表示重写为可复用视觉原语的组合与空间布局，从而为广义类别发现提供结构化、可分离的特征空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GCD方法主要聚焦于聚类目标设计，却忽视了标准视觉backbone产生的高秩、纠缠的token表示这一关键瓶颈——这类表示本质上不适合无监督地发现潜在概念与结构。作者的核心假设是：所有类别（已知或未知）都可表达为有限可学习视觉原语的组合与空间排列。

Method: 提出Compositional Primitive Fields (CPF)，作为插入backbone与分类头之间的空间场机制。CPF通过低秩原语混合重写噪声patch token，将图像分解为可复用的原子部件及其空间布局。通过显式建模原语的空间分布，使新类别自然涌现为共享词汇表上的新激活模式，从而将表示学习的重心从全局嵌入划分转向构建结构化、可分离的原语场。

Result: 大量实验表明，CPF作为通用即插即用模块，在多种GCD基线方法上均能持续提升性能，验证了识别和利用低秩组合结构是开放世界识别中关键的归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 低秩组合结构是广义类别发现的重要归纳偏置，CPF通过对特征空间施加组合性几何约束，使潜在类别结构可被辨识，为开放世界识别提供了新的表示学习范式。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to recognize known classes while autonomously discovering novel ones in open-world settings. However, current approaches primarily focus on designing clustering objectives, often overlooking a critical bottleneck: standard vision backbones yield high-rank, entangled token representations that are ill-suited for unsupervised discovery of latent concepts and structures. In this paper, we propose Compositional Primitive Fields (CPF-GCD), a novel representation learning framework that reshapes the feature space to make such latent structure identifiable by enforcing a low-rank compositional organization. Our core hypothesis is that all categories, whether known or novel, can be expressed as compositions and spatial arrangements of a finite set of learnable visual primitives that capture reusable concepts. CPF instantiates this geometric constraint via a spatial field mechanism. Inserted between the backbone and the head, it rewrites noisy patch tokens through low-rank primitive mixtures, effectively decomposing images into reusable atomic parts and their spatial layouts. By explicitly modeling the spatial distribution of primitives, CPF enables novel categories to emerge naturally as new activation patterns over a shared vocabulary. This shifts the focus of representation from merely partitioning global embeddings to constructing a structured and separable primitive field. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CPF serves as a generic, plug-and-play module that consistently boosts performance across diverse GCD baselines, validating that identifying and leveraging low-rank compositional structure is a crucial inductive bias for open-world recognition.

</details>


### [72] [Learning to Watch: Active Video Anomaly Understanding via Interleaved Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00622)
*Mengjingcheng Mo,Jiaxu Leng,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Anom-π，一个将视频异常理解重新定义为主动序贯决策的闭环框架，通过时间原子操作实现感知主动性，并设计了iDPO算法在弱监督下进行轨迹级策略对齐，以2B参数在复杂场景中显著超越现有大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常理解（VAU）采用被动范式，面临观测混淆问题——静态采样无法区分语义上不同的事件，因为VAU依赖稀疏且上下文依赖的线索。

Method: 提出Anom-π闭环框架：1）将视频理解重构为动态环境中的主动序贯决策过程，融合内部认知推理与策略性证据获取的交错策略；2）引入时间原子操作（局部回溯、时间扩展、细粒度采样）赋予模型感知主动性；3）设计交互式直接偏好优化（iDPO）实现轨迹级策略对齐，以主动证据查询（AEI）效用函数平衡任务成功、信息性证据获取与交互成本。

Result: 仅使用2B参数的Anom-π框架在广泛实验中取得了极具竞争力的性能，在复杂场景中显著优于最先进的大规模VAU模型。

Conclusion: 通过闭环主动决策框架，小参数模型也能在视频异常理解任务中超越大规模被动模型，验证了主动证据获取策略在解决观测混淆问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) relies on sparse, context-dependent cues. However, existing passive paradigms suffer from observational aliasing, where static sampling fails to disambiguate semantically distinct events. To overcome this, we propose $Anom\text{-}π$, a closed-loop framework that reconceptualizes video understanding as an active sequential decision-making process within a dynamic environment. Inspired by human video-reviewing behavior, this framework unifies internal cognitive reasoning and strategic evidence acquisition into an interleaved policy, utilizing temporal atomic operators such as local backtracking, temporal expansion, and fine-grained sampling to endow the model with perceptual proactivity. To learn such complex interaction strategies under video-level weak supervision, we design Interactive Direct Preference Optimization (iDPO) to achieve trajectory-level policy alignment, guided by an Active Evidence Inquiry (AEI) utility that balances task success, informative evidence acquisition, and interaction cost. This approach enables the agent to learn to actively disambiguate hypotheses while suppressing redundant exploration. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework, with only 2B parameters, achieves highly competitive performance, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art large-scale VAU models in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [73] [Uncertainty-aware tree height change regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00638)
*Max Gaber,Dimitri Gominski,Jaime C. Revenga,Stefan Oehmcke,Rasmus Fensholt,Martin Brandt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Canopy Height Change数据集，提供3米分辨率的连续冠层高度差异及其空间不确定性，并引入不确定性感知的变化回归任务及相关评估指标，用于微调地理空间基础模型以改进冠层高度变化的连续估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法和数据集将冠层高度变化检测框定为二值变化检测问题，忽视了植被变化的连续性本质以及标签中的固有不确定性，亟需一种能够捕捉连续变化并量化不确定性的新范式。

Method: 构建了覆盖西班牙北部和西部10598 km²的CHC数据集，包含3米分辨率的连续冠层高度差异及空间分辨的不确定性，并配以PlanetScope卫星影像时间序列；在此基础上定义不确定性感知的变化回归任务，提出相应的评估指标和地理空间基础模型微调策略。

Result: 对当前最先进的地理空间基础模型进行了评估，揭示了在连续冠层高度变化估计方面的有前景方向和仍存在的挑战。

Conclusion: 连续且不确定性感知的冠层高度变化估计是遥感与深度学习结合的新方向，CHC数据集和新任务框架为该领域提供了基准，但仍有显著挑战待解决。

Abstract: Monitoring canopy height change is essential for understanding carbon sinks and forest dynamics. Remote sensing enables consistent, large-scale observations of such changes, increasingly integrated with deep learning architectures such as Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs). However, existing methods and datasets frame the problem as binary change detection, which overlooks both the continuous nature of change, especially for vegetation, and the inherent uncertainty in labels. We present the Canopy Height Change (CHC) dataset, providing 3 $\mathrm{m}$ resolution continuous canopy height differences and associated spatially resolved uncertainties across 10598 $\mathrm{km}^2$ of northern and western Spain. The dataset is paired with a co-located time series of PlanetScope satellite imagery. Based on the dataset, we introduce the task of uncertainty-aware change regression, associated metrics and strategies for fine-tuning GFMs. Furthermore, we evaluate state-of-the-art GFMs and highlight promising directions and remaining challenges for advancing continuous canopy height change estimation.

</details>


### [74] [Not All Prediction Targets Keep Training-Free Diffusion Guidance on the Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00647)
*Yunsung Lee,Hyeongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了训练无关引导（TFG）在早期高噪声采样阶段因干净图像估计不准确而导致样本偏离数据流形的根本问题，论证了x-prediction直接输出干净图像的优势，并提出Child FID指标来量化流形偏离程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ε-预测和v-预测扩散模型在执行训练无关引导时，需要从噪声状态估计干净图像x̂，这一估计在高噪声阶段精度不足，导致引导信号使生成样本偏离数据流形。x-prediction模型直接输出干净图像，从源头消除了这一误差，但缺乏对其在TFG场景下优势的理论和实证分析。

Method: 首先，对不同预测目标（ε-prediction、v-prediction、x-prediction）在高噪声下的干净图像估计精度进行理论分析；其次，提出Child FID（guided-class FID）指标，通过条件FID评估引导后样本是否仍保持在目标类别的数据流形上；最后，在新的细粒度鸟类生成基准和风格迁移任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明x-prediction在高噪声阶段具有最优的干净图像估计精度；Child FID成功暴露了标准评估指标无法检测的流形偏离问题；在细粒度鸟类基准和风格迁移实验中，x-prediction在保持样本处于数据流形方面表现最可靠，作为TFG的基础架构优势显著。

Conclusion: x-prediction是训练无关引导的最优基础架构，其直接预测干净图像的特性从根本上解决了高噪声阶段估计误差导致的流形偏离问题，为TFG的实际应用提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Training-free guidance (TFG) steers a pretrained diffusion model toward a desired attribute at inference. To be effective, this guidance must be applied from the earliest, high-noise steps of sampling. Because its objective (a classifier or energy) is defined on clean images, $ε$- and $v$-prediction models must first estimate the clean image $\hat{x}$ from the noisy state at each step, and the accuracy of that estimate determines how easily guidance drifts off the data manifold. $x$-prediction, a recent alternative, outputs the clean image directly, removing this source of error even at high noise. This is our motivation. We provide a theoretical analysis of how each prediction target shapes this accuracy, and introduce guided-class FID (Child FID), a metric that exposes the manifold damage standard evaluation misses. Experiments on a new fine-grained bird benchmark and on style transfer confirm that $x$-prediction keeps guided samples on the manifold most reliably, making it the strongest foundation for training-free guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/ManLuML/on-manifold-tfg

</details>


### [75] [Linguistic Relative Policy Optimization for Video Anomaly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00654)
*Jiaxu Leng,Jiankang Zheng,Mengjingcheng Mo,Zhanjie Wu,Haosheng Chen,Ji Gan,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LRPO 提出一种无需参数更新的语言化相对策略优化方法，通过从多条推理轨迹中蒸馏群体相对语义优势，形成语言表达的异常经验先验，注入上下文来引导模型输出分布，在多个 VAD 基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型用于视频异常检测仍依赖大规模标注或专家设计先验，难以在最少人工干预下获取异常知识。LRPO 旨在以无参数更新的方式，从推理轨迹中自动蒸馏异常经验，实现 tuning-free 的高效 VAD。

Method: 提出 LRPO 框架：1）构建两类互补经验表示——通用经验捕捉跨场景的可迁移异常偏好，场景经验建模上下文相关的异常规则；2）通过群体相对语义优势从多条推理轨迹中蒸馏经验先验；3）引入异常对齐奖励（anomaly alignment reward），引导轨迹优化以匹配人类风险偏好并强化时序推理；4）将经验先验注入上下文，在不更新模型参数的情况下调节输出分布。

Result: 在 XD-Violence、UCF-Crime 和 UBnormal 三个数据集上进行了广泛实验，LRPO 在 tuning-free 设定下显著超越现有 state-of-the-art 方法。

Conclusion: LRPO 通过语言化经验蒸馏和无参数适配机制，实现了在最少人工干预下的高效视频异常检测，验证了群体相对语义优势与异常对齐奖励在经验学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) with multimodal large language models has shown strong potential, yet most existing methods still depend on large-scale annotations or expert-designed priors, limiting their ability to acquire anomaly knowledge with as little human intervention as possible. To address this, we propose Linguistic Relative Policy Optimization (LRPO), which distills group-relative semantic advantages from multiple reasoning trajectories into a linguistically expressed anomaly experience prior, and adapts the model by injecting this prior into the context to steer its output distribution without any parameter updates. LRPO builds two complementary experience representations: general experience captures transferable anomaly preferences across scenarios, while scenario experience models context-dependent anomaly rules for targeted refinement. To further improve the learned experience, we introduce an anomaly alignment reward that guides trajectory optimization to match human risk preferences and reinforce temporally grounded reasoning. Extensive experiments on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and UBnormal demonstrate that LRPO significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods under tuning-free settings.

</details>


### [76] [DART: Difficulty-Adaptive Routing for Zero-Shot Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00672)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Mark He Huang,Zhigang Tu,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART 提出了一种难度自适应路由框架，利用 DPP 进行关键帧筛选和难度评估，将简单查询走快速路径直接预测，复杂查询走慢速路径（含时序标记提示）进行结构化推理，在零样本视频时序定位任务上取得了 SOTA 性能，且帧使用量减少 7 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本 VTG 方法依赖帧-查询特征匹配，对简单事件有效，但面对需要理解时序顺序和因果结构的多阶段复杂查询时力不从心——作者将此称为"推理鸿沟"（reasoning gap）。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出 DART（Difficulty-Adaptive Routing for Temporal Grounding）框架：(1) 使用查询条件化的行列式点过程（DPP）同时完成两项任务——筛选多样化且查询相关的关键帧作为时序证据，并利用谱熵作为难度指标；(2) 简单查询路由至 Fast 路径直接预测；(3) 复杂查询路由至 Slow 路径，采用时序标记提示（Temporal Markup Prompting），将定位任务分解为全局事件分析、逐帧时序角色标注和边界提取三个子步骤。

Result: 在 Charades-STA 和 ActivityNet Captions 上，DART 在同分布和多种分布外设定下均取得最先进的零样本性能，mIoU 较最强基线提升高达 3.5 个点，同时使用的帧数减少超过 7 倍。

Conclusion: DART 通过难度自适应路由与结构化推理，有效弥合了零样本 VTG 中的推理鸿沟，在保持高效（大幅减少帧使用量）的同时显著提升了复杂查询的定位精度。

Abstract: Zero-shot video temporal grounding (VTG) localizes events in untrimmed videos from natural language queries without task-specific training. Existing methods rely on frame-query feature matching, which suffices for simple events but struggles with complex multi-stage queries that require understanding temporal ordering and causal structure -- a disparity we call the reasoning gap. We propose DART (Difficulty-Adaptive Routing for Temporal Grounding), which bridges this gap by coupling difficulty-aware routing with structured reasoning in large vision-language models. A query-conditioned Determinantal Point Process (DPP) serves a dual role: selecting diverse, query-relevant keyframes as temporal evidence, and providing spectral entropy as a difficulty indicator. Simple queries are routed to a Fast path for direct prediction, while complex queries follow a Slow path with Temporal Markup Prompting, which decomposes localization into global event analysis, per-frame temporal role annotation, and boundary extraction. On Charades-STA and ActivityNet Captions, DART achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance across both identically distributed and multiple out-of-distribution settings, improving mIoU by up to 3.5 points over the strongest baseline while using over 7 times fewer frames. The project homepage is available at https://dart-vtg.github.io/.

</details>


### [77] [ABot-M0.5: Unified Mobility-and-Manipulation World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00678)
*Ronghan Chen,Yandan Yang,Zuojin Tang,Dongjie Huo,Tong Lin,Haoning Wu,Haoyun Liu,Yuzhi Chen,Lulu Zheng,Botai Yuan,Tianlun Li,Mingxin Wang,Dekang Qi,Bin Hu,Wei Mei,Yuze Xuan,Haolong Yang,Yanqing Zhu,Mu Xu,Zhiheng Ma,Xinyuan Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABot-M0.5 提出一种三层对齐的 World Action Model，通过中间潜在动作对齐时间粒度、双层级 MoT 架构解耦异构动作空间、Dream-Forcing 训练策略对齐训练-测试一致性，在移动操作的长时域任务成功率和精细控制精度上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLA 策略缺乏显式世界建模，现有 World Action Models 在移动操作中存在三重不对齐问题：(1) 时间粒度粗糙，操作在粗粒度视频块上；(2) 动作空间纠缠，导航与机械臂控制混在一起导致分布冲突；(3) 训练使用真实状态做逆动力学，但推理时依赖模型自回归预测，训练-测试不一致导致误差累积。

Method: 提出三层对齐方案：(1) 时间粒度对齐——引入中间潜在动作（intermediate latent actions），捕捉局部视觉状态转移，作为视频潜变量与具体控制信号之间的桥接动作空间；(2) 动作空间对齐——设计双层级 Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) 架构，解耦模态表征与异构动作子空间（底盘移动 vs 机械臂操作）；(3) 推理条件对齐——提出 Dream-Forcing 训练策略，在模型自预测视频上逐步训练逆动力学，提升自回归推理时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的移动操作和精细操控基准测试上，ABot-M0.5 在长时域任务成功率和精细控制精度两个维度均达到 SOTA 水平。

Conclusion: 时间粒度对齐、动作解耦和训练-推理一致性是构建高效 World Action Model 的三大关键要素，ABot-M0.5 验证了这一设计理念的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a key capability for general-purpose robots, yet remains challenging for current embodied learning methods. VLA policies are typically reactive and lack explicit world modeling, while existing World Action Models (WAMs) are still poorly aligned with the structure of mobile manipulation: they operate on coarse video chunks, model entangled navigation-manipulation actions, and train inverse dynamics under supervision that does not match autoregressive inference. As a result, they often miss fine-grained contact dynamics, suffer from action-distribution conflicts, and accumulate errors over long-horizon rollouts. We propose ABot-M0.5, a new WAM built on the insight that mobile manipulation requires alignment at three levels: temporal granularity, action space, and train-test consistency. To align temporal granularity, we introduce intermediate latent actions that capture local visual state transitions and serve as an bridging action space between video latents and embodiment-specific controls. To align action space, we design a dual-level Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that disentangles both modality representations and heterogeneous action subspaces such as base movement and arm manipulation. To align inference conditions, we propose the dream-forcing training strategy that progressively trains inverse dynamics on model-predicted videos, improving train-test alignment and robustness during autoregressive prediction. Experiments on challenging mobile and fine-grained manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that ABot-M0.5 achieves state-of-the-art performance in both long-horizon task success and finegrained control accuracy. These results highlight the critical importance of granularity-aligned, action-disentangled, and inference-consistent world-action modeling.

</details>


### [78] [LUMA: Benchmarking Segmentation via a Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00687)
*Tobias Christian Nauen,Anosh Billimoria,Federico Raue,Stanislav Frolov,Brian B. Moser,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LUMA，一个轻量级、骨干无关的掩码Transformer头，统一基准下对20种骨干和11种预训练方案进行公平对比，发现plain ViT在吞吐量上占据帕累托前沿，且预训练目标而非架构是决定分割质量的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像分割中不同Transformer骨干的对比被混淆因素（不同解码器、训练配方、预训练方案）所干扰，难以公平评估骨干本身的性能差异。需要一个统一的、骨干无关的解码头来实现公平的基准测试。

Method: 提出LUMA（Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter），一个轻量级的骨干无关掩码Transformer头，将任意骨干视为黑盒特征提取器，通过廉价的交叉注意力让一组可学习查询从骨干特征中读取信息。LUMA可不变地附加到各向同性、层次化、卷积和混合专家等各类骨干上。在此固定头下，对ADE20K和Cityscapes上的20种骨干、11种预训练方案及多种分辨率进行统一基准测试。

Result: LUMA以更低成本匹配当前最优高效ViT分割器EoMT的精度。基准测试发现：（1）所谓"高效"token mixer在高分辨率下并未实现真正的效率优势，plain ViT在所有分辨率下均占据吞吐量帕累托前沿；（2）预训练目标（而非架构）才是决定分割质量的最关键杠杆。

Conclusion: 统一解码头对于公平的骨干对比至关重要。plain ViT在效率-精度权衡上仍是最优选择，社区过度关注架构创新而忽视了预训练目标这一更关键的因素。

Abstract: Comparing transformer backbones for image segmentation is confounded: each is paired with a different decoder, recipe, and pretraining, so reported differences rarely reflect the backbone itself. We introduce the Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter (LUMA), a lightweight, backbone-agnostic mask-transformer head that treats any backbone as a black-box feature extractor, letting a set of queries read from its features through cheap cross-attention. LUMA matches the accuracy of EoMT, the state-of-the-art efficient ViT-segmenter, at lower cost, while attaching unchanged to isotropic, hierarchical, convolutional, and mixture-of-experts backbones alike. Holding this head fixed, we benchmark 20 backbones, 11 pretraining schemes and a range of resolutions on ADE20K and Cityscapes under one modern recipe. We find that ``efficient'' token mixers fail to deliver efficiency even at the high resolutions that motivate them, with plain ViT holding the throughput Pareto-front at every resolution. Additionally, the pretraining objective, not the architecture, the lever the field has tuned hardest, governs segmentation quality.

</details>


### [79] [Imprint: Online Memory Compression for Long-Horizon Egocentric QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00696)
*Kousik Das,Debaditya Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Imprint 提出一种以交互为中心的记忆框架，将长时程自我中心记忆建模为在线压缩问题而非摘要问题，通过复用性、近因性和独特性三种信号选择性保留和压缩交互记录，在 EgoLifeQA 七天基准上显著提升了 QA 准确率、证据 grounded 比例、记忆效率和检索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程自我中心 QA 方法将记忆构建为层级化文本摘要，虽能减少记忆体积，但摘要优化的是描述性压缩而非检索效果——重复交互被吸收进粗粒度文本描述，而非保留为显式的、可复现的记忆单元，导致长时程证据聚合困难。

Method: 提出 Imprint 框架：(1) 将传入观察表示为结构化的交互记录（Interaction Records）；(2) 持续将其组织为重复出现的交互模式；(3) 借鉴人类记忆巩固信号——复用性（recurrence）、近因性（recency）和独特性（distinctiveness），选择性保留和压缩交互，构建紧凑的检索导向记忆。

Result: 在 EgoLifeQA 七天基准上，使用相同 LLM，Imprint 将 QA 准确率从 31.0% 提升至 35.8%；相比 EgoRAG，证据 grounded 回答提升 6 倍，记忆占用减少 2.3 倍，检索延迟降低 11.8 倍。

Conclusion: 记忆压缩（而非摘要）为长时程自我中心问答提供了可扩展且检索高效的基础，Imprint 框架验证了这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon egocentric question answering involves answering about events that have occurred hours or days in the past. This requires memory representations that remain both retrieval-effective and scalable over days or weeks of recording. Existing long-horizon egocentric QA methods construct memory as hierarchical textual summaries of observations. While effective for reducing memory size, summarization optimizes for descriptive compression rather than retrieval: repeated interactions are absorbed into coarse textual descriptions instead of being preserved as explicit, recurring memory units, making long-horizon evidence aggregation difficult. We propose Imprint, an interaction-centric memory framework that formulates long-horizon egocentric memory as an online memory compression problem rather than summarization. Incoming observations are first represented as structured Interaction Records and continuously organized into recurring interaction patterns. Using human memory consolidation signals of recurrence, recency, and distinctiveness, Imprint selectively retains and compresses interactions into a compact retrieval-oriented memory. We evaluate Imprint on EgoLifeQA, a seven-day egocentric benchmark containing questions that require reasoning over interactions occurring hours to days before the query. With the same LLM, Imprint improves QA accuracy from 31.0% to 35.8%, increases evidence-grounded answers by $6\times$ compared with EgoRAG, reduces memory footprint by $2.3\times$, and decreases retrieval latency by $11.8\times$. These results demonstrate that memory compression provides a scalable and retrieval-effective foundation for long-horizon egocentric question answering.

</details>


### [80] [Creating Impactful Autonomous Driving Datasets: A Strategic Guide from Research Gap to Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00710)
*Richard Schwarzkopf,Jonas Merkert,Frank Bieder,Annika Bätz,Alexander Blumberg,Carlos Fernandez,Felix Hauser,Fabian Immel,Christian Kinzig,Hendrik Königshof,Fabian Konstantinidis,Martin Lauer,Willi Poh,Nils Rack,Kevin Rösch,Yinzhe Shen,Marlon Steiner,Gleb Stepanov,Dominik Strutz,Ömer Şahin Taş,Julian Truetsch,Kaiwen Wang,Royden Wagner,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶数据集设计的战略框架，核心思想是先诊断研究瓶颈是数据问题还是评估问题，再选择最小代价的数据操作符来弥合差距，仅在廉价手段不足时才录制新数据。作者以KITScenes数据集家族为案例，对该框架进行了实践论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献大多仅描述数据集包含什么内容，却鲜有探讨如何战略性地设计有影响力的数据集。这对资源有限的中小型实验室和初创公司尤为不利，因为它们无法承受资源错配的代价。因此，需要一套系统性的方法论来指导数据集的高效创建。

Method: 作者提出一个四阶段战略框架：（1）差距识别——判断研究问题是被数据问题还是评估问题所阻碍；（2）操作符选择——选择最小的数据操作符（如数据增强、合成数据、迁移学习等）来弥合差距，仅在更廉价的手段不足时才进行新数据采集；（3）传感器套件设计；（4）标注策略设计。作者通过该框架回顾分析了主流自动驾驶数据集的演进历程，并以自研的KITScenes数据集家族为贯穿案例进行实践验证。

Result: 分析表明，该框架能够有效解释现有自动驾驶数据集的演化路径，并为新数据集的设计提供系统性指导。KITScenes数据集家族的构建过程验证了"先诊断、后最小化干预"策略的可行性与经济性。

Conclusion: 有影响力的数据集创建应始于对研究瓶颈的精准诊断，并遵循最小成本原则选择数据操作符。该框架为资源受限团队提供了可操作的路线图，有助于提升数据集设计的效率与影响力。

Abstract: Well-designed autonomous driving datasets have fundamentally shaped research progress, yet existing literature primarily describes what datasets contain rather than how to strategically design impactful ones. This is especially limiting for small and medium-sized labs and startups that cannot afford to misallocate scarce resources. We argue that impactful dataset creation begins with a diagnosis: whether a research question is blocked by a data problem or an evaluation problem, and proceeds by selecting the minimal data operator(s) that closes the resulting gap, recording new data only when no cheaper operator(s) suffices. We analyze the evolution of major autonomous driving (AD) datasets through this lens and distill a strategic framework spanning gap identification, operator choice, sensor suite design, and annotation strategy. We ground the framework in a running case study of our KITScenes dataset family. The datasets are available at: https://kitscenes.com/

</details>


### [81] [Towards Memory-Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation via Instance-Specific Parametric Absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00712)
*Xiaomeng Fu,Jia Li,Yiming Hu,Yong Wang,Hayden Kwok-Hay So,Jiao Dai,Xiangxiang Chu,Jizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ISPA 提出了一种从"丢弃"转向"蒸馏"的 KV 缓存压缩范式：通过将部分全注意力层转换为局部注意力层，并利用闭式最小二乘解将历史上下文吸收进模型权重，实现最多 50% 的 KV 缓存削减且画质几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归流式视频生成模型中，KV 缓存随序列线性增长导致内存瓶颈，而现有的丢弃冗余 token 的压缩方法会破坏长程依赖，引发时间闪烁和身份丢失问题。

Method: ISPA 包含三个阶段：(1) 将部分 Full-Attention 层转换为 Local-Attention 层；(2) 在短暂预热阶段监控全局注意力与局部注意力之间的输出差异；(3) 在转换点求解闭式最小二乘问题，计算出实例特定的权重调制（weight modulation），将历史上下文"吸收"进模型参数以补偿缺失的全局信息。

Result: 在 1.3B 到 14B 规模的多种架构上，ISPA 能够移除高达 50% 的 KV 缓存，同时保持接近无损的视觉质量。

Conclusion: ISPA 为流式生成模型的记忆管理提供了新视角，即超越外部 token 级缓存管理，探索参数化记忆整合。该方向值得未来进一步研究。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) streaming models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for long video generation. However, the linearly growing Key-Value (KV) cache poses a significant bottleneck, leading to memory overload and degraded inference throughput. A common compression method is to drop redundant KV tokens, which often breaks long-range dependencies, resulting in temporal flickering and identity loss. In this paper, we propose Instance-Specific Parametric Absorption (ISPA), a novel framework that shifts the KV cache compression from discarding to distilling. The core idea is to transit a subset of layers from Full-Attention (F-Layers) to memory-efficient Local-Attention (L-Layers) by "absorbing" historical context into the model's weights. Specifically, during a brief warmup phase, ISPA monitors the output discrepancy between global and local attention. At the transition point, we solve a closed-form least-squares problem to compute an instance-specific weight modulation that compensates for the missing history. Experiments across architectures (1.3B to 14B) demonstrate that ISPA can remove up to 50\% of the KV cache with near-lossless visual quality. We hope this perspective encourages future work to explore parametric memory consolidation beyond external token-level cache management for streaming generative models.

</details>


### [82] [Partial Skeleton Visibility for Action Recognition: A Constrained Field-of-View Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00716)
*Yingjie Dai,Tianyang Xu,Yanglin Deng,Xiao-Jun Wu,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PartialVisGraph 是一种基于超图的骨架动作识别框架，通过可学习的虚拟超边和单头样本自适应Transformer，显式引入可见性先验来选择性门控信息流，解决受限视场（FoV）下关节遮挡导致的性能退化问题，在NTU RGB+D数据集上相较强基线提升最高68.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架动作识别方法普遍假设输入骨架完整且干净，但在自我中心视觉、拥挤监控、可穿戴设备等真实部署场景中，受限视场（FoV）常导致大量关节可见性丢失，现有模型对此缺乏应对能力，性能急剧退化。这一关键问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出PartialVisGraph框架：（1）构建高表达力超图，引入可学习的虚拟超边形成软关联矩阵，捕捉超越传统成对图的高阶依赖关系；（2）设计单头样本自适应Transformer（Single-Head Sample-Adaptive Transformer），自适应地将关节特征聚合到超边上，并显式融入可见性先验（visibility prior），通过选择性门控机制阻止被遮挡或视野外关节污染可靠特征传播；（3）在NTU RGB+D 60和120数据集上建立真实的FoV模拟评估基准。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60和120数据集上的大量实验表明，PartialVisGraph在部分可见性条件下持续达到最先进精度，在严重FoV限制的子集上相较近期强基线提升高达68.8%，同时在完全可见性设置下仍保持优势。

Conclusion: PartialVisGraph为无约束环境下可部署的骨架动作理解提供了一条有原则且实用的路径，有效弥合了受限视场下鲁棒骨架动作识别的关键空白。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition has achieved remarkable success by exploiting joint coordinates and their topological connections, yet prevailing methods overwhelmingly assume complete and clean skeleton inputs. In real-world deployments, such as egocentric vision, crowded surveillance, wearable devices, or edge robotics, limited field-of-view (FoV) frequently causes substantial joint visibility dropout, leading to severe performance degradation that existing models are largely unprepared to handle. To bridge this critical yet underexplored gap, we introduce PartialVisGraph, a novel hypergraph framework tailored for robust skeleton action recognition under constrained FoV. We first construct highly expressive hypergraphs by introducing learnable virtual hyperedges that form a soft incidence matrix, capturing flexible high-order dependencies beyond conventional pairwise graphs. We then propose the Single-Head Sample-Adaptive Transformer, which adaptively aggregates joint features onto hyperedges while explicitly incorporating a visibility prior. This prior selectively gates information flow, preventing occluded or out-of-view joints from corrupting reliable feature propagation. We further establish rigorous evaluation protocols with realistic FoV simulation benchmarks on NTU RGB+D 60 and 120. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PartialVisGraph consistently achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under partial visibility, with gains of up to 68.8\% on subsets with severe FoV restrictions compared to recent strong baselines, while remaining superior on full-visibility settings. Our approach offers a principled and practical pathway toward deployable skeleton-based action understanding in unconstrained environments.

</details>


### [83] [AV-SyncBench: Decoupled Benchmarking of Temporal and Semantic Audio-Visual Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00726)
*Tianhong Zhou,Mingyang Han,Boyu Li,Yuxuan Jiang,Jiaxin Ye,Dongxiao Wang,Haoxiang Shi,Kunpeng Wang,Jun Song,Cheng Yu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了AV-SyncBench，首个将音视频同步评估中的时间一致性与语义一致性完全解耦的基准，覆盖语音、音乐和声音三大类、10个场景和5个挑战任务，包含3269个视频和38390个样本，并评估了五种代表性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频特征提取模型的评估协议存在维度偏差（仅关注语义匹配或时间偏移检测），且数据构造耦合，无法独立评估时间一致性和语义一致性。

Method: 构建AV-SyncBench基准：从自然场景视频中自动筛选并人工验证，确保声源在屏幕内；将时间与语义评估完全分离，覆盖Voice、Music、Sound三大类、10个场景和5个挑战任务；在此基准上评估五种代表性模型的特征质量及下游任务表现。

Result: 构建了包含3269个视频和38390个样本的基准数据集，并对五种代表性模型进行了量化评估，展示了特征质量在对齐任务和下游任务中的表现差异。

Conclusion: AV-SyncBench有效填补了音视频同步评估中时间与语义解耦的空缺，为多模态特征提取模型的公平、细粒度评估提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Audio-visual feature extraction is a fundamental component of multimodal understanding and generation tasks. However, existing evaluation protocols for feature extraction models exhibit dimensional bias, typically focusing on either semantic matching or temporal offset detection. Moreover, their data construction remains coupled, preventing independent assessment of temporal and semantic consistency. We propose AV-SyncBench, the first benchmark to fully separate temporal and semantic evaluation for audio-visual synchronization. Built from in-the-wild videos, it spans Voice, Music, and Sound across 10 scenarios and 5 challenge tasks. Data are automatically filtered and manually verified to ensure on-screen sound sources. The benchmark contains 3,269 videos and 38,390 samples, and we evaluate five representative models to quantify feature quality for alignment and downstream tasks. The code and dataset are available at: https://fgt7t6g.github.io/AV-SyncBench.

</details>


### [84] [ConRTF: Edge-Constrained Boundary Distribution Refinement for Realtime TransFormer Table Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00734)
*Eliott Thomas,Tri-Cong Pham,Mickael Coustaty,Aurelie Joseph,Gaspar Deloin,Vincent Poulain d'Andecy,Jean-Marc Ogier,Antoine Doucet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EFL损失函数，将表格行列结构不对称性编码为几何先验，强调行元素重视水平边界、列元素重视垂直边界，在实时检测器D-FINE中实现训练期引导、推理无开销，方法ConRTF数据高效且带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于检测的TSR方法将表格元素视为通用对象，忽略了表格布局的根本特性：行与列在结构上扮演不同角色，其边界具有不对等的重要性。边界定位的微小误差会传播为错误的单元格分配和结构不一致。

Method: 提出Edge-constrained Fine-grained Localization loss (EFL)，将表格特有的几何先验形式化为训练目标：行类元素监督侧重于水平边界，列类元素侧重于垂直边界。方法在D-FINE检测器中实现，仅在训练阶段运作，不改变推理流程。整体方案称为ConRTF。

Result: 在PubTables-1M和两个私有数据集上，ConRTF相比优化基线及RT-DETRv2、YOLOv10-11等实时检测器取得一致提升，最高+1.6 GriTS点，且仅需2k-3k标注表格即可保持鲁棒精度。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模表格行列的结构不对称性，EFL在保持推理速度不变的前提下有效提升了表格结构识别的边界定位精度，并具备良好数据效率。

Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) aims to recover the row and column layout of tables from document images, a key step in document understanding pipelines. Accurate TSR depends on precise boundary localization: small errors in row or column boundaries can propagate into incorrect cell assignments and structural inconsistencies. Yet detection-based approaches treat table elements as generic objects, ignoring a fundamental property of table layout: rows and columns play structurally distinct roles and their boundaries carry unequal importance. We propose an Edge-constrained Fine-grained Localization loss (EFL) that formalizes this structural asymmetry by encoding table-specific geometric priors into the training objective: row-like elements are supervised with emphasis on their horizontal boundaries, while column-like elements prioritize vertical boundaries. Implemented within a real-time detector with distribution-based boundary refinement (D-FINE), EFL operates during training only and guides boundary refinement toward structurally meaningful adjustments with no change to the inference pipeline. The proposed approach, ConRTF, is also data-efficient, maintaining robust accuracy with as few as 2k--3k annotated tables. Experiments on PubTables-1M and two private datasets show consistent improvements over the optimized baseline and several real-time detectors including RT-DETRv2 and YOLOv10-11, with gains of up to +1.6 GriTS points at equal inference speed.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Robust Driving Perception: A Flexible Scale-Driven Family for Self-Supervised Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00736)
*Zhaowen Zhu,Li Zhang,Yujie Chen,Tian Zhang,Yingjie Wang,Mingxia Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexDepth 是一种面向具有挑战性道路场景的自监督单目深度估计模型家族，采用两阶段静动态解耦训练策略和尺度驱动解码器（SDD），在任意尺度上以极低计算开销实现最先进性能，最小模型 Flex-Nano 仅需 0.7 GFLOPs，在移动端可达 37.6 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督单目深度估计模型大多局限于单一尺度，在复杂驾驶环境中性能退化严重；处理动态交通参与者的网络过于复杂，难以部署到资源受限的车载边缘设备。因此需要一种灵活、轻量且能应对多尺度复杂场景的鲁棒深度估计方案。

Method: 提出 FlexDepth 模型家族，核心设计包括：(1) 两阶段静动态解耦训练策略，分别对静态背景和动态道路目标进行独立的置信度评估；(2) 精心设计的尺度驱动解码器（Scale-Driven Decoder, SDD），根据尺度大小动态选择组件，实现高效特征融合和高精度深度图输出。

Result: 在标准驾驶基准数据集上的大量实验表明，无需任何辅助信息，FlexDepth 在任意尺度上均达到最先进性能，同时计算开销极小。最小模型 Flex-Nano 仅需 0.7 GFLOPs，在移动平台上实现 37.6 FPS，具备可靠的实时感知能力和出色的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: FlexDepth 通过静动态解耦训练与尺度驱动解码器的协同设计，成功在资源受限边缘设备上实现了鲁棒、灵活、高性能的自监督单目深度估计，为自动驾驶实时感知提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) has garnered attention in recent years due to its independence from ground truth. However, most existing models are limited to a single scale and exhibit considerable performance degradation in complex driving environments. Networks specifically designed to handle dynamic traffic participants tend to be overly complex, hindering their deployment on resource-constrained automotive edge devices. To address these limitations and move towards robust driving perception, we propose FlexDepth, a scale-driven and flexible family of self-supervised MDE models tailored for challenging road scenarios. FlexDepth employs a two-stage static-dynamic decoupled training strategy, enabling the independent assessment of confidence for both static backgrounds and dynamic road objects. Furthermore, it introduces a meticulously designed Scale-Driven Decoder (SDD) to dynamically select components based on scale size, facilitating efficient feature fusion and the output of high-precision depth maps. Extensive experiments on standard driving benchmarks demonstrate that without any auxiliary information, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance across arbitrary scales with minimal computational overhead. Our smallest model, Flex-Nano, requires only 0.7 GFLOPs and achieves 37.6 FPS on mobile platforms, ensuring reliable real-time perception while maintaining excellent zero-shot generalization.Our source code is avalible: https://github.com/startnew/flexdepth

</details>


### [86] [Prototype Memory-Guided Training-Free Anomaly Classification and Localization in Prenatal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00744)
*Huanwen Liang,Yuhao Huang,Xiliang Zhu,Yuanji Zhang,Xuedong Deng,Xinru Gao,Guowei Tao,Yuhan Zhang,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的多类别产前超声异常分类与定位框架，仅需每类少量参考图像，通过多粒度原型记忆库、原型驱动软合并机制和类别感知优化策略，在9类共2357张图像的数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前超声异常诊断面临两大临床痛点：一是异常类别发病率低且异质性高，导致大规模标注数据集难以获取；二是现有少样本学习方法仍需针对新类别进行微调，在资源有限的临床环境中实用性不足。因此需要一种无需训练、仅依赖极少量参考图像即可完成多类别分类与定位的方法。

Method: 提出一种免训练（training-free）框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）多粒度原型记忆库，显式建模类别级语义和异常特征；（2）原型驱动的软合并机制，聚合判别性特征以检测异常区域；（3）类别感知优化策略，利用原型一致性提升类别预测精度。该框架仅需每类几张参考图像即可工作，是该设定下的首次探索。

Result: 在多中心产前超声数据集上进行了广泛验证，数据集包含1,149例病例、共计2,357张图像、覆盖9个类别，所提方法在分类与定位任务上均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 该训练-free框架有效解决了产前超声异常诊断中数据稀缺和微调负担的双重挑战，在仅需少量参考图像的条件下实现了优越的多类别分类与定位性能，为资源受限临床场景提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Prenatal anomaly classification and localization is of critical importance for fetal health and pregnancy management. Although ultrasound (US) is the primary modality for prenatal screening, accurate diagnosis remains challenging due to the low prevalence and high heterogeneity of anomalies. Existing deep learning methods for prenatal tasks rely on large-scale annotated datasets, which are difficult to obtain in practice. Although few-shot learning alleviates data scarcity, it typically requires fine-tuning for new categories, limiting its practicality in resource-limited clinical settings. To address these challenges, we propose a training-free framework for multi-class prenatal US anomaly classification and localization that operates with only a few reference images per class, representing the first exploration of this setting. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a memory bank with multi-granular prototypes that explicitly models both class-level semantics and anomaly characteristics; (2) a prototype-driven soft merging mechanism that aggregates discriminative features to detect the anomaly region; and (3) a class-aware refinement strategy that leverages prototype consistency to improve category prediction. Extensively validated on a multi-center prenatal US dataset containing 1,149 cases, with a total of 2,357 images and 9 categories, our proposed method outperforms the competitors.

</details>


### [87] [Foundation Model-driven Key Anatomy Frame Selection for Blind-sweep Ultrasound Fetal Birth Weight Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00745)
*Le Ou,Xiliang Zhu,Huanwen Liang,Wenxiong Pan,Yuhao Huang,Yuxiang Deng,Xuan Sheng,Hong Yin,Juhua Xiao,Xin Zhou,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于盲扫超声视频和视觉-语言基础模型的胎儿出生体重估计框架，通过解剖引导的帧选择和冗余感知特征压缩，在839例前瞻性数据上实现MAE 161.3 g，90.23%的案例误差在10%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中胎儿出生体重（FBW）估计依赖操作者经验，在资源匮乏地区尤其受限。盲扫超声视频无需标准切面约束，可降低对操作者技能的要求，但缺乏平面约束给准确回归带来挑战。本文旨在利用盲扫超声视频实现操作者无关的精准FBW估计。

Method: 提出一个三部分框架：(1) 首次将盲扫超声视频用于FBW估计；(2) 设计解剖引导的帧选择模块（Anatomy-Guided Frame Selection），借助视觉-语言基础模型从无约束扫查中自动筛选关键帧；(3) 设计冗余感知特征压缩模块（Redundancy-Aware Feature Compression），在压缩帧特征的同时保留任务相关信息，缓解时序冗余。以分娩后实际称重作为金标准，在产前48小时内采集视频进行回归训练与评估。

Result: 在前瞻性收集的839例患者数据上验证，方法达到平均绝对误差（MAE）161.3 g，90.23%的案例绝对百分比误差在10%以内，100%的案例在15%以内，优于典型的Hadlock公式估计及其他强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架首次证明了盲扫超声视频结合基础模型可实现精确、操作者无关的产前胎儿出生体重估计，有望在低资源场景下提升产前评估的可及性和可靠性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate fetal birth weight (FBW) estimation shortly before delivery is clinically valuable yet challenging due to its reliance on operator expertise, particularly in low-resource settings. To reduce this reliance, we study near-term birth-weight regression from blind-sweep ultrasound (US) videos acquired within 48 hours prior to delivery, with post-delivery weighing as ground truth. Accordingly, we propose a foundation model-driven key anatomy frame selection framework that enables accurate FBW regression despite the absence of plane constraints in blind sweeps. Our highlights are as follows: (1) We believe this is the first work to estimate FBW using blind-sweep US videos, enabling operator-independent assessment. (2) An Anatomy-Guided Frame Selection module equipped with a vision-language foundation model is proposed for keyframe collection in unconstrained sweeps. (3) A Redundancy-Aware Feature Compression module is designed to compress frame features while preserving task-relevant information, alleviating temporal redundancy. Extensively validated on prospectively collected data from 839 patients, our method achieves an MAE of 161.3 g, with 90.23% and 100% of cases falling within 10% and 15% absolute percentage error, outperforming typical Hadlock estimation and strong competitors. Codes are available at https://github.com/ouleoule/BlindSweep-EBW.

</details>


### [88] [GaussianFusion: Unified 3D Gaussian Representation for Multi-Modal Fusion Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00746)
*Xiao Zhao,Chang Liu,Mingxu Zhu,Zheyuan Zhang,Linna Song,Qingliang Luo,Chufan Guo,Kuifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianFusion 提出了一种基于 3D 高斯表示的多模态融合框架，取代传统的 BEV 离散网格，在统一的连续 3D 高斯空间中融合多传感器特征，显著提升了细节保留能力，在 nuScenes 数据集上的 3D 目标检测和语义占用预测任务中均取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BEV 表示采用离散网格结构，会导致显著的细节丢失，并限制了多模态融合中的特征对齐和跨模态信息交互。作者希望打破 BEV 范式，寻求一种连续、统一的多模态特征表示方式。

Method: 提出 GaussianFusion 框架：(1) 将多模态特征统一到共享的连续 3D 高斯空间中，有效保留边缘和细纹理细节；(2) 设计了基于前向投影的多模态高斯初始化模块；(3) 设计了共享的跨模态高斯编码器，基于注意力机制迭代更新高斯属性。该模型是任务无关的，统一的高斯表示可自然支持多种 3D 感知任务。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上，GaussianFusion 在 3D 目标检测中比 BEVFusion 基线提升 2.6 NDS；其变体在 3D 语义占用预测任务上比 GaussFormer 提升 1.55 mIoU，同时仅使用 30% 的高斯点，推理速度提升 450%。

Conclusion: GaussianFusion 验证了以 3D 高斯表示替代 BEV 网格进行多模态融合的可行性与优越性，在保持高效的同时实现了更强的细节保留和跨模态交互能力，为多模态 3D 感知提供了新的通用范式。

Abstract: The bird's-eye view (BEV) representation enables multi-sensor features to be fused within a unified space, serving as the primary approach for achieving comprehensive 3D perception. However, the discrete grid representation of BEV leads to significant detail loss and limits feature alignment and cross-modal information interaction in multimodal fusion perception. In this work, we break from the conventional BEV paradigm and propose a new universal framework for multi-modal fusion based on 3D Gaussian representation. This approach naturally unifies multi-modal features within a shared and continuous 3D Gaussian space, effectively preserving edge and fine texture details. To achieve this, we design a novel forward-projection-based multi-modal Gaussian initialization module and a shared cross-modal Gaussian encoder that iteratively updates Gaussian properties based on an attention mechanism. GaussianFusion is inherently a task-agnostic model, with its unified Gaussian representation naturally supporting various 3D perception tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate the generality and robustness of GaussianFusion. On the nuScenes dataset, it outperforms the 3D object detection baseline BEVFusion by 2.6 NDS. Its variant surpasses GaussFormer on 3D semantic occupancy with 1.55 mIoU improvement while using only 30% of the Gaussians and achieving a 450% speedup.

</details>


### [89] [Active Learning for Cascaded Object Detection: Balancing Coverage and Uncertainty in Table Extraction Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00747)
*Eliott Thomas,Mickael Coustaty,Aurelie Joseph,Gaspar Deloin,Vincent Poulain d'Andecy,Jean-Marc Ogier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将混合覆盖-不确定性采样方法 UHerding 适配到级联目标检测流水线中，并提出两种流水线感知扩展（RankFusion 和 CAPA），用于降低表格提取任务中表格检测和表格结构识别的标注成本。实验表明 CAPA 在四个数据集中的三个上表现最稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业文档表格提取依赖级联流水线（先表格检测 TD，后表格结构识别 TSR），构建训练集成本高昂，尤其是 TSR 需要细粒度结构标注。现有主动学习策略多针对单模型任务，未考虑级联架构中级间依赖关系。

Method: 将原用于图像分类的 UHerding（混合覆盖-不确定性采样）首次适配到级联目标检测流水线。提出两种流水线感知扩展：1) RankFusion：在检测和结构两个表示空间上施加双流形覆盖；2) CAPA：进一步引入阶段依赖门控和逐任务不确定性校准。

Result: 在两个公开数据集（PubTables-1M、FinTabNet）和两个私有数据集上，以 71 到 500 份文档的标注预算进行实验。UHerding 在表格提取任务上泛化良好，优于所有基线。RankFusion 预期收益更高但方差较大，CAPA 在四分之三的数据集上比标准 UHerding 更稳定一致。

Conclusion: UHerding 可有效推广到级联目标检测场景。流水线感知的 CAPA 策略通过阶段依赖建模和不确定性校准，在多数数据集上实现最稳定性能，为级联架构的主动学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Table extraction from business documents relies on a cascaded pipeline where Table Detection (TD) first localizes tables and Table Structure Recognition (TSR) then recovers their internal layout. Building task-specific training sets for this pipeline is costly, particularly for TSR which requires fine-grained structural annotations. Active learning (AL) can reduce this annotation burden, yet most AL strategies are designed for single-model tasks and do not account for inter-stage dependencies in cascaded architectures. In this work, we present the first adaptation of Uncertainty Herding (UHerding), a hybrid coverage-uncertainty sampling method originally proposed for image classification, to cascaded object detection pipelines. We propose two pipeline-aware extensions that exploit the TD-to-TSR dependency: RankFusion adds dual-manifold coverage over both detection and structure representation spaces, while CAPA further incorporates stage-dependent gating and per-task uncertainty calibration. Extensive experiments across two public (PubTables-1M and FinTabNet) and two private table extraction datasets, with various annotation budgets (from 71 to 500 documents) show that UHerding generalizes well to table extraction, outperforming each baseline. Among pipeline-aware variants, RankFusion achieves higher expected gains but at the cost of greater variance, while CAPA emerges as the most consistent strategy, outperforming standard UHerding on three out of four datasets.

</details>


### [90] [FrameONE: Hierarchical Motion Modeling for Universal Multi-View Echocardiographic Keyframe Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00748)
*Rusi Chen,Yuhao Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Chao Tian,Shunan Ji,Yuhan Zhang,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameONE 是一个统一的端到端多视图超声心动图关键帧检测框架，通过层级运动建模策略（视图内多任务学习 + 视图间通用运动学习），在四个标准视图上实现了最优的关键帧检测精度和跨视图泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声心动图关键帧（ES/ED）检测方法通常针对特定视图开发，依赖辅助标注或密集视觉建模，泛化性受限。多视图建模中，关键帧检测虽由共享的心脏运动驱动，但不同视图间存在显著的外观差异和运动模式差异，统一建模极具挑战。

Method: 提出 FrameONE 统一框架，核心是层级运动建模（Hierarchical Motion Modeling）：(1) 视图内多任务学习——在单一视图内减少外观偏差，促进运动聚焦的表征学习；(2) 视图间通用运动学习模块——将视图无关的动态特征与视图特定模式分离，实现跨视图共享且灵活的运动表征学习。整体为端到端框架。

Result: 在涵盖四个标准视图的 25,872 个视频数据集上进行大规模实验，FrameONE 取得了最先进的关键帧检测精度，并展现出强大的跨视图泛化能力。

Conclusion: FrameONE 通过层级运动建模有效解决了多视图超声心动图关键帧检测中外观差异与运动模式差异的难题，提供了一个统一、高精度且泛化性强的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate detection of end-systole (ES) and end-diastole (ED) frames is fundamental to echocardiographic assessment. Existing methods are typically developed in a view-specific manner, depend on auxiliary annotations or intensive visual modeling, which limits their generalizability. In multi-view modeling, keyframe detection is driven by shared cardiac motion, yet large appearance differences and motion patterns make unified modeling challenging. To address these issues, we propose FrameONE, a unified end-to-end framework for multi-view echocardiographic keyframe detection. FrameONE introduces a Hierarchical Motion Modeling strategy: an intra-view multi-task learning reduces appearance bias and promotes motion-focused representations within each view; an inter-view general motion learning module further separates view-agnostic dynamics from view-specific patterns, enabling shared yet flexible motion representation learning across views. Extensive experiments on 25,872 videos spanning four standard views demonstrate that FrameONE achieves state-of-the-art keyframe detection accuracy with strong cross-view generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/szuboy/FrameONE.

</details>


### [91] [LeVLJEPA: End-to-End Vision-Language Pretraining Without Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00784)
*Lukas Kuhn,Giuseppe Serra,Randall Balestriero,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeVLJEPA 是首个完全非对比式的端到端视觉-语言预训练方法，通过跨模态预测和分布正则化学习，无需负样本、温度参数或动量编码器，生成的冻结编码器在下游密集语义任务（如VQA和语义分割）上显著优于对比式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言预训练仍以对比学习为主，而纯视觉自监督学习已转向非对比方法；同时视觉-语言编码器的角色已从零样本分类器转变为冻结的视觉骨干网络，用于生成全图patch token网格。然而，尚无工作探索非对比式目标函数是否能为此类密集下游使用场景提供更优的视觉表征。

Method: 提出LeVLJEPA，核心机制包括：(1) 跨模态预测——使用停止梯度目标进行预测，无需负样本；(2) 逐模态分布正则化，防止表示坍缩；(3) 完全摒弃温度参数、动量编码器和teacher-student架构；(4) 端到端训练，在大规模数据上保持稳定。

Result: 在冻结VLM骨干设定下，LeVLJEPA在GQA、VQAv2和POPE上均优于所有对比编码器（测试两种语言模型）；在语义分割上超越对比基线；在线性探测等全局读出任务上与对比方法持平。

Conclusion: 非对比式预训练是生成密集语义视觉特征的有效途径，尤其适合需要全图patch token的现代视觉-语言模型和密集预测系统，为视觉-语言预训练范式提供了对比学习之外的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-language pretraining remains dominated by contrastive objectives, whereas vision-only self-supervised learning has largely adopted non-contrastive methods. At the same time, the role of vision-language encoders has shifted: they are increasingly deployed not as zero-shot classifiers but as the frozen visual backbone of vision-language models and dense prediction systems, which consume the full grid of patch tokens rather than a single pooled embedding. We introduce LeVLJEPA, the first fully non-contrastive end-to-end vision-language pretraining method. LeVLJEPA learns through cross-modal prediction with stop-gradient targets and per-modality distributional regularization, without negatives, temperature, momentum encoder, or teacher-student schedule, and trains stably at large scale. We find that the resulting encoder provides markedly stronger dense semantic features for downstream use: as a frozen vision-language-model backbone, LeVLJEPA is the strongest of the evaluated encoders across GQA, VQAv2, and POPE under two distinct language models, and outperforms contrastive baselines on semantic segmentation, while remaining on par on global readouts such as linear probing. These results establish non-contrastive pretraining as an effective means of producing dense semantic vision features.

</details>


### [92] [GKDT: General Keypoint Detection Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00752)
*Changsheng Lu,Yuxin Chen,Haokun Gui,Rong Wang,Jie Yang,Harry Yang,Anton van den Hengel,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通用关键点检测（GKD）任务，构建了包含130万实例的大规模统一关键点数据集MegaKPT，并基于DINOv3开发了支持视觉/文本提示的通用关键点检测模型GKDT，在22个测试集上展现出强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着预训练视觉-语言模型的发展，计算机视觉正从窄域识别转向开放域识别。现有关键点检测模型通常针对特定任务设计，缺乏通用性。构建一个能支持多样化任务的通用关键点检测模型变得日益重要，但此前缺乏大规模、统一标注的通用关键点数据集。

Method: （1）构建MegaKPT数据集：整合29个现有数据集，包含超过130万个多样化的目标实例，并提供带有文本描述的高质量统一标注。（2）提出GKDT模型：基于DINOv3的Transformer架构，支持视觉提示、文本提示或两者混合的输入方式。（3）提出训练策略：包括混合模态提示训练和动态重要性采样，以增强模型训练效果。

Result: 在22个测试集（包含已见和未见的物体类别）上进行评估，单个GKDT模型在广泛类别上表现出强大的关键点检测性能和泛化能力，大多数类别的PCK@0.1准确率超过90%。

Conclusion: GKDT模型展示了通用关键点检测的可行性，在多样化类别中取得高精度，具有很高的实际应用价值。数据集、模型和代码将开源发布。

Abstract: With the emergence of various pre-trained vision and language models, computer vision is shifting from narrow-domain to open-domain recognition. The construction of a more powerful yet general keypoint detection (GKD) model to support diverse tasks has become increasingly important in the field. To this end, we firstly present a large-scale unified keypoint dataset called MegaKPT. The dataset is composed of over 1.3 million diverse object instances from twenty-nine existing datasets, and enjoys high-quality unified annotations with keypoint text descriptions. Based on MegaKPT, we develop GKDT, a simple, flexible and powerful DINOv3 based Transformer model for General Keypoint Detection. Our GKDT supports visual prompts, text prompts, or both. To enhance model training, we also propose a suite of useful strategies such as mix-modal prompted training and dynamic importance sampling. By testing over 22 test sets with seen or unseen objects, our single GKDT model shows strong performance and generality in detecting keypoints on broad categories, with most categories over 90\% PCK@0.1 accuracy, offering high practical applicability to real-world problems. The dataset, models, and codes will be released at https://github.com/AlanLuSun/General-Keypoint-Detection.

</details>


### [93] [Pano2World: End-to-End 3D Generation via Unified Multi-View Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00832)
*Zhenjia Li,Jinrang Jia,Yifeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pano2World 提出了一种从单张室内全景图直接生成可探索3D高斯场景的方法，通过视角感知注意力路由实现联合多视图去噪，并引入潜空间特征适配器避免VAE解码带来的信息损失，在多位置全景新视角合成基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两大局限：一是迭代式逐视图补全方法会导致渐进式误差累积且流程繁琐；二是基于视频生成模型的方法受限于连续轨迹约束，无法同时从多个方向灵活覆盖场景。因此需要一种能直接从单张全景图生成持久、可自由探索的3D表示的方法。

Method: Pano2World 包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从源全景图重建粗略的3D高斯代理，并在自适应采样的邻近姿态下渲染获得几何对齐的引导全景图；(2) 全景扩散模型通过视角感知注意力路由（View-Aware Attention Routing）联合去噪所有目标视图，每个目标视图同时接收来自对应引导全景图的几何约束和来自源全景图的全局语义引导，从而自然保证跨视图一致性；(3) 引入潜空间特征适配器（Latent Feature Adapter），这是一个几何感知的桥接模块，直接将联合去噪过程中形成的多视图隐特征蒸馏为场景潜码，再解码为最终的3D高斯场景，避免了通过VAE解码回像素域所造成的信息损失。

Result: 在多位置全景新视角合成基准上的实验表明，Pano2World 显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Pano2World 实现了从单张室内全景图到可探索3D高斯场景的直接转换，克服了迭代补全的误差累积和视频模型连续轨迹约束的局限性，其核心创新在于联合多视图去噪与潜空间直接蒸馏的结合。

Abstract: A single panorama captures the full visual sphere from one camera center, yet confines users to looking around in place without enabling true scene exploration. Converting a single panorama into a persistent, renderable 3D representation for free-viewpoint navigation has attracted growing interest; existing methods either adopt iterative per-view completion that propagates inpainting results to update the underlying geometry, leading to progressive error accumulation and cumbersome multi-step pipelines, or leverage the temporal consistency priors of video generation models, yet the continuous-trajectory constraint intrinsic to such models limits their flexibility in covering scenes from multiple directions simultaneously. We present Pano2World, which takes a single indoor panorama as input and directly outputs a persistent, explorable 3D Gaussian scene. Given the source panorama, Pano2World first reconstructs a coarse 3D Gaussian proxy and renders it at adaptively sampled nearby poses to obtain geometrically aligned guidance panoramas; a panoramic diffusion model then jointly denoises all target views via View-Aware Attention Routing, where each target view simultaneously receives geometric constraints from its corresponding guidance panorama and global semantic guidance from the source panorama, naturally enforcing cross-view consistency. To avoid the information loss incurred by decoding the multi-view hidden features formed during joint denoising back to the pixel domain via VAE, we introduce Latent Feature Adapter, a geometry-aware bridge module that directly distills these hidden features into a scene latent, subsequently decoded into the final 3D Gaussian scene. Experiments demonstrate that Pano2World significantly outperforms existing methods on the multi-position panoramic novel-view synthesis benchmark.

</details>


### [94] [Decoupled Guidance: Disentangling Subject and Context Pathways in Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00766)
*Seongmin Kim,Kyucheol Shin,Heesun Jung,Jinseo Kim,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有文本到图像个性化方法中，主体身份（保真度）与场景上下文（可编辑性）共享同一条件化路径，导致两者竞争注意力图资源，引发"条件化纠缠"及保真度-可编辑性权衡。本文提出解耦引导框架（DeGu），通过两个独立引导流分别处理主体身份和场景上下文，并引入空间混合机制动态融合，在不修改骨干模型的前提下，以即插即用方式提升多种个性化方法的性能，并支持推理时调控保真度-可编辑度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到图像个性化方法普遍将主体身份和场景上下文通过同一条件化路径编码，造成所谓的"条件化纠缠"（conditioning entanglement），使得保真度（fidelity）和可编辑性（editability）在注意力图上相互竞争资源，形成不可调和的权衡。揭示并解决这一根本性问题，是实现高质量、灵活可控的个性化图像生成的关键动机。

Method: 提出解耦引导（Decoupled Guidance, DeGu）框架：(1) 将主体身份与场景上下文分别路由至两个独立的引导流，从根本上解除条件化纠缠；(2) 引入空间混合机制（spatial mixing mechanism），动态融合两个引导流，确保各自在其语义相关区域内运作而不相互干扰；(3) DeGu 以即插即用方式应用于现有个性化方法，无需修改底层骨干模型，支持包括 flow-matching Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) 在内的多种架构。

Result: 通过因果实验验证了条件化纠缠的存在：将目标主体 token 替换为通用主体 token 会导致注意力分配偏移和上下文遵循度变化，为保真度-可编辑性权衡提供了因果证据。DeGu 框架在多种个性化方法和骨干模型（含 DiTs）上均能一致地提升整体个性化性能，并实现推理时对保真度-可编辑性平衡的灵活控制。

Conclusion: 条件化纠缠是导致文本到图像个性化中保真度-可编辑性权衡的根本原因。DeGu 通过解耦引导与空间混合机制有效解决了该问题，作为一种通用的即插即用框架，可广泛适用于现有方法和多种骨干架构，显著提升个性化生成质量与可控性。

Abstract: Text-to-image personalization aims to generate a user-provided subject in novel scenes described by text. However, most existing methods encode subject identity (fidelity) and context (editability) through the same conditioning pathway, forcing the two to compete for attention-map resources. We refer to this phenomenon as conditioning entanglement and show that it induces a fidelity-editability trade-off. We further provide causal evidence by replacing the target subject token with a generic subject token, which produces shifts in attention allocation and corresponding changes in context adherence. To this end, we propose Decoupled Guidance (DeGu), a plug-and-play framework that routes subject identity and scene context through two independent guidance streams. We further introduce a spatial mixing mechanism that dynamically fuses these streams, ensuring each operates within its semantically relevant region without interference. Furthermore, DeGu can be readily applied to existing personalization methods without modifying the underlying backbone models, consistently improving the overall personalization performance while enabling inference-time control over the fidelity-editability balance, across diverse methods and backbones, including flow-matching Diffusion Transformers (DiTs).

</details>


### [95] [Soft Mixture-of-Recursions: Going Deeper with Recursive Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00774)
*Sang In Lee,Jihun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftMoR（Soft Mixture-of-Recursions），一种通过可学习的软混合权重融合各递归步骤的中间表示，从而构建更深、更强的Vision Transformer的方法。在ImageNet-1K上，仅增加1.7M参数即可将SR-ViT-S的top-1准确率从79.83%提升至82.48%，超越更大的DeiT-B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归Transformer主要通过共享参数构建紧凑模型，但未充分挖掘递归在增加模型深度与表征能力方面的潜力。在视觉Transformer中，简单增加递归深度并不能稳定提升性能，因为现有方法没有充分利用递归过程中产生的中间表示。

Method: 提出SoftMoR（Soft Mixture-of-Recursions），核心思想是学习token-wise的混合权重，对来自所有递归步骤的输出进行软组合，使中间表示能够以可学习的灵活方式被利用。在此基础上构建了SR-ViT（Soft Recursive Vision Transformer）。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，递归深度从1增至4时，SR-ViT-S的top-1准确率从79.83%提升至82.48%，仅增加1.7M参数，性能超越参数规模约3.7倍的DeiT-B。在多种视觉任务上，SR-ViT均随递归深度增加而持续提升。

Conclusion: SoftMoR通过软混合递归机制，提供了一条参数高效的路径来构建更深、更强的递归Vision Transformer，证明了递归在视觉模型扩深方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Recent recursive Transformer studies have primarily reused shared parameters across computation steps to construct compact, parameter-efficient models. In this work, we leverage recursion to build effectively deeper Transformers with stronger representational capacity. However, in Vision Transformers, simply increasing recursion depth does not reliably improve performance, as existing recursive approaches do not fully utilize the intermediate representations produced throughout recursive computation. We propose Soft Mixture-of-Recursions (SoftMoR) and its Vision Transformer instantiation, Soft Recursive Vision Transformer (SR-ViT). SoftMoR learns token-wise mixture weights to softly combine outputs from all recursion steps, allowing intermediate representations to be utilized in a learnable and flexible way. Across diverse vision tasks, SR-ViT consistently improves as recursion depth increases with minimal parameter overhead. On ImageNet-1K, increasing recursion depth from 1 to 4 improves SR-ViT-S top-1 accuracy from 79.83% to 82.48% with only 1.7M additional parameters, outperforming the substantially larger DeiT-B while using approximately 27% of its parameters. These results demonstrate that SoftMoR provides a parameter-efficient path to deeper and stronger Vision Transformers through recursion.

</details>


### [96] [SpiralFovea: Input-Adaptive Foveated Tokenization as a Third Lever of Resource-Adaptive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00780)
*Kyan Mahajan,Mohammad Saqlain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpiralFovea 提出一种无参数、输入自适应的图像分词策略，基于局部视觉熵在骨干网络查询前确定token的位置、尺度和数量，以≤78个多尺度螺旋采样patch替代ViT标准196-patch网格，在细粒度基准上以60%输入token减少实现1.7-2.1pp精度提升，同时自注意力FLOPs降低84%、吞吐量提升18-29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多数基础模型的自适应推理技术（早退、MoE路由、KV缓存压缩、动态注意力稀疏等）改变的是模型的行为，而输入始终采用固定网格分词，对图像内容不敏感。作者认为这是一个被忽视的可优化环节，主张在骨干网络参数查询之前即完成输入自适应，以内容驱动的非均匀采样替代均匀网格分词。

Method: 提出 SpiralFovea，一种无参数的输入自适应分词器。其核心机制为：(1) 基于局部视觉熵识别内容驱动的热点锚点（hotspot anchors）；(2) 围绕每个锚点生成多尺度螺旋环（multi-scale spiral rings），以模拟视网膜中央凹（fovea）的采样密度分布——中心密集、外围稀疏；(3) 最终产生 ≤78 个patch，完全替代标准 ViT 的 196-patch 网格输入，且所有选择在骨干网络参数查询之前完成，不引入额外交互开销。

Result: 在四个典型细粒度识别基准上，SpiralFovea 以 60% 的输入 token 减少实现了 +1.7 至 +2.1 个百分点的精度提升，每层 Transformer 自注意力 FLOPs 降低 84%，吞吐量提升 18-29%。在 CUB-200-2011 Genus 子集上对四种骨干网络的受控消融实验揭示了一个清晰的诊断规律：增益幅度与骨干网络的全图位置先验强度呈负相关，表明自监督基础模型是输入自适应分词最具价值的应用场景。

Conclusion: 输入自适应分词是一个被低估但有效的优化杠杆。SpiralFovea 表明，在不修改骨干网络参数的前提下，仅通过内容驱动的非均匀采样即可同时实现精度提升与计算量大幅降低。该方法的收益与骨干网络的位置先验强度反向相关，在自监督基础模型（位置先验较弱）上价值最大，为高效视觉推理提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Most adaptive-inference techniques for foundation models change what the model does - early exit, MoE routing, KV-cache compression, dynamic attention sparsity. The input that hits the backbone, however, remains a fixed-grid tokenisation indifferent to image content. We argue that this is a missed lever. We present SpiralFovea, a parameter-free, input-adaptive tokeniser in which token identity, location, scale, and count are all functions of local visual entropy and selection completes before any backbone parameter is queried. Around content-driven hotspot anchors, multi-scale spiral rings produce <= 78 patches that replace the standard 196-patch ViT grid at the input stage. Across four canonical fine-grained benchmarks, SpiralFovea yields +1.7-2.1 pp accuracy with a 60% reduction in input tokens, an 84% reduction in self-attention FLOPs at every transformer layer, and 18-29% throughput gains over the matched static tokenisation baseline. A controlled ablation on CUB-200-2011 Genus across four backbones reveals a clean diagnostic: the gain magnitude tracks inversely with the strength of the backbone's whole-image positional prior, isolating self-supervised foundation models as the regime where input-adaptive tokenisation is most valuable.

</details>


### [97] [Improving Sparse-View 3DGS Generalization via Flat Minima Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00885)
*Kangmin Seo,Sangeek Hyun,MinKyu Lee,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏视图下3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的过拟合问题，从平坦极小值(FM)优化角度出发，提出一种轻量级训练框架：通过各向异性高斯扰动和周期性重初始化来正则化优化过程，在不改变架构的前提下显著提升了稀疏视图下的泛化能力和渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在稀疏输入视图下容易过拟合到观测图像，对未见视角泛化能力差。现有方法缺乏从优化景观(optimization landscape)角度解决该问题的尝试，作者希望利用平坦极小值优化理论来提升3DGS在稀疏监督下的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 将高斯参数视为可训练权重，引入受控高斯扰动进行正则化，该扰动考虑了每个高斯的各向异性和训练进程，在保留细节的同时提升泛化性；2) 提出周期性重初始化策略，在短时间窗口内将非位置参数暂时恢复到初始状态，以进一步稳定平坦极小值优化过程。两者可无缝集成到现有3DGS管线。

Result: 在LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的实验表明，稀疏视图监督下，方法在定量指标和感知质量上均有提升，重建结果更清晰、更稳定、对新视角的泛化更优。

Conclusion: 通过将平坦极小值优化原理适配到3DGS的几何动态特性中，结合各向异性扰动与周期性重初始化，可以在不修改架构的前提下有效缓解稀疏视图过拟合问题，为3DGS的鲁棒训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in neural rendering have established 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) as a highly efficient representation for novel view synthesis, enabling fast training and real-time rendering with strong fidelity. However, when supervision is limited to sparse input views, 3DGS tends to overfit to the observed images and generalize poorly to unseen viewpoints. We address this challenge from the perspective of flat minima (FM) optimization, which seeks solutions that remain stable under small parameter perturbations. Viewing Gaussian parameters as trainable weights, we adapt FM principles to the geometric and dynamic nature of 3DGS with a lightweight training framework. Our method regularizes optimization with controlled Gaussian perturbations that account for each Gaussian's anisotropy and the training progress, preserving fine details while improving robustness to sparse-view overfitting. To further stabilize this flat minima optimization process, we introduce periodic reinitialization, which temporarily returns non-positional parameters to their initial states for a short window. Together, these techniques integrate seamlessly into existing 3DGS pipelines without architectural changes. Experiments on LLFF and Mip-NeRF360 datasets demonstrate improved quantitative metrics and perceptual quality under sparse-view supervision, producing reconstructions that are sharper, more stable, and better generalized to novel viewpoints.

</details>


### [98] [DeWorldSG: Depth-Aware 3D Semantic Scene Graph Generation via World-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00889)
*Seok-Young Kim,Abdelrahman Elskhawy,Taewook Ha,Dooyoung Kim,Eunjae Shin,Benjamin Busam,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeWorldSG是一个从RGB-D序列生成时空鲁棒3D语义场景图的框架，通过深度引导滤波的3D高斯分布建模对象节点，并利用世界模型(V-JEPA 2)的上下文先验聚合时空证据来补充缺失关系，在3DSSG和ReplicaSSG数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法因逐帧推理导致3D对象表征不稳定以及关系缺失，难以构建可靠的3D场景图。DeWorldSG旨在解决这两个核心问题：一是用概率化3D节点替代单一投影点来表示对象，二是通过时空证据聚合缓解关系稀疏性。

Method: 方法包含两大创新：(1) 通过深度引导滤波估计实例级几何3D高斯分布，将每个对象表示为概率化3D节点而非单一投影点；(2) 跨对象对聚合时空证据，并利用世界模型V-JEPA 2的上下文先验精炼关系预测。

Result: 在3DSSG和ReplicaSSG数据集上达到对象和谓词预测的最优性能，三元组召回率提升77.4%，谓词召回率提升23.2%，同时生成时间一致的场景结构。

Conclusion: DeWorldSG通过概率化3D节点建模和世界模型驱动的时空关系聚合，有效解决了3D场景图构建中的不稳定性和关系稀疏问题，在多项指标上大幅超越先前方法，适合机器人操作和AR应用。

Abstract: We present DeWorldSG, a novel framework that generates spatio-temporally robust 3D Semantic Scene Graphs from RGB-D sequences. Existing methods often struggle to construct reliable 3D scene graphs due to unstable 3D object representations and missing relations caused by frame-wise inference. DeWorldSG addresses these issues by estimating instance-level geometric 3D Gaussian distributions through depth-guided filtering and representing each object as a probabilistic 3D node rather than a single projected point. To mitigate relational sparsity from frame-wise inference, our framework further aggregates spatiotemporal evidence across object pairs and refines relations using contextual priors derived from a world model (V-JEPA 2). Experiments on the 3DSSG and ReplicaSSG datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art (SoTA) performance in both object and predicate prediction, while producing temporally consistent scene structures. In particular, our method improves triplet recall by 77.4% and predicate recall by 23.2% over prior SoTA approaches, making it suitable for robotic manipulation and AR applications. Our code and models are open-sourced.

</details>


### [99] [ClinRAG-GRAPH: Clinical-prior Retrieval-Augmented Graph Model with Domain Adversarial Learning for Breast pCR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00798)
*Yaofei Duan,Yuhao Huang,Tianyu Zhang,Yuan Gao,Luyi Han,Xin Wang,Xinyu Xie,Xinglong Liang,Chunyao Lu,Muzhen He,Patrick Pang,Yue Sun,Ning Mao,Tao Tan,Ritse Mann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClinRAG-GRAPH 是一个面向乳腺癌新辅助化疗（NAC）病理完全缓解（pCR）预测的框架，融合DCE-MRI、临床变量和病理生物标志物，通过临床先验图、图卷积网络、双分支域对抗学习和LLM驱动的子图检索增强生成模块，实现跨中心的鲁棒且可解释的pCR预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NAC前pCR预测面临三大挑战：(1) 多模态（影像、临床、病理）跨模态建模不充分；(2) 多中心影像采集协议差异导致的异质性；(3) 模型可解释性不足，缺乏循证支撑。因此需要一种能够鲁棒融合多模态数据、抑制中心间域偏移并提供临床可解释性的预测框架。

Method: ClinRAG-GRAPH框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建患者内临床先验图，采用先验引导的关系感知图卷积网络进行结构化多模态表示学习；(2) 提出双分支域对抗学习策略，在抑制与成像协议相关的MRI偏倚的同时保留pCR相关特征，提升跨中心鲁棒性；(3) 引入LLM驱动的子图检索增强生成（RAG）模块，检索临床相似的历史病例，融合检索证据进行pCR推理，增强可解释性。

Result: 在由两个公开数据集和三个内部中心数据组成的大规模多中心NAC乳腺癌队列上验证：内部测试集AUC为0.815，两个外部测试集AUC分别为0.774和0.712，展现出跨中心的鲁棒预测性能。

Conclusion: ClinRAG-GRAPH通过图神经网络建模多模态关系、域对抗学习消除中心间偏倚、LLM-RAG提供循证可解释性，有效实现了乳腺癌NAC前pCR的跨中心鲁棒预测。

Abstract: Neoadjuvant chemotherapy (NAC) response prediction is clinically important for treatment stratification in breast cancer. However, robust pre-treatment pathological complete response (pCR) prediction remains challenging due to insufficient cross-modal modeling, multicenter imaging heterogeneity, and weak evidence-grounded interpretability. We propose ClinRAG-GRAPH, a Clinically informed Retrieval-Augmented Generation Graph framework, for pre-treatment pCR prediction from DCE-MRI, structured clinical variables, and biopsy-derived pathological biomarkers. ClinRAG-GRAPH constructs an intra-patient clinical-prior graph and applies a prior-guided relation-aware graph convolutional network for structured multimodal representation learning. To improve cross-center robustness, we introduce a dual-branch domain-adversarial learning strategy to suppress protocol-related MRI bias while preserving pCR-relevant features. To enhance interpretability, we further incorporate large language model (LLM)-driven subgraph RAG module that retrieves clinically analogous historical cases and integrates retrieved evidence for pCR inference. We assemble a large-scale multicenter NAC breast cancer cohort for extensive validation, drawing from two public sources and three in-house centers.Results show that ClinRAG-GRAPH achieves AUCs of 0.815 on the internal test set and 0.774/0.712 on two external test sets, demonstrating robust pre-treatment pCR prediction across centers. The code is available at the anonymized https://github.com/miccai26-1181/ClinRAG-GRAPH.

</details>


### [100] [Spotted: Location-informed Reidentification of Hyenas and Leopards in Camera Trap Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00804)
*Halil Sina Kelebek,Julia Hindel,Kobus Hoffman,Lauren Hoffman,Andrew Loveridge,Bob Mandinyenya,Kudakwashe Ncube,Justin Seymour-Smith,Andrea Sibanda,Abhinav Valada,Matthew Wijers,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spotted是一个结合时空可行性与视觉相似度的动物重识别框架，通过相机位置推导的移动速度先验作为伪监督来微调视觉模型，并采用主动采样策略大幅减少人工审核量，在斑鬣狗和花豹数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动物重识别方法主要存在三个痛点：(1) 相机陷阱图像质量低、光照和视角变化大、个体观测数量极不平衡，导致全自动化识别精度不足；(2) 实际工作流程依赖专家人工审核候选匹配，效率低下；(3) 现有方法几乎只关注视觉特征，忽视了图像时间戳和相机位置等野外研究中常规可得的辅助信息。因此，作者希望利用这些被忽视的时空信息来减少专家审核负担。

Method: Spotted框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于最小行进速度的可行性分数——根据两张检测图像对应的相机位置和时间戳，计算同一只动物在两地间移动所需的最小速度，作为与模型无关的时空可行性先验；(2) 伪监督训练——将时空可行性分数作为软标签，在冻结的视觉基础模型之上训练一个轻量级分类头，使视觉特征适应特定物种和场景；(3) 融合匹配——将自适应后的视觉相似度与时空可行性融合，得到鲁棒的成对匹配分数。此外，还集成了主动配对采样策略，优先标注模型最不确定的配对，加速人工审核过程。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的相机陷阱数据集（LeopardID102、SpottedHyenaID109、SpottedHyenaID415）上进行评估，相比最佳基线方法，top-5识别准确率分别提升9个百分点、2个百分点和9个百分点。在人机协同策略下，可在保持同等正匹配数的同时，将需要查询的比较次数减少高达69个百分点。

Conclusion: Spotted通过有效融合时空先验与视觉信息，显著提升了动物重识别的准确率，并大幅降低了人工审核成本，展示了多模态信息融合和人在回路策略在生态监测实际应用中的巨大潜力。同时，作者公开了三个数据集，为该领域的后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Animal re-identification (ReID) in camera-trap surveys remains challenging due to low image quality, strong variation in illumination and viewpoint, and highly imbalanced numbers of observations per individual. As a result, current ReID performance is often insufficient for fully automated use, and practical workflows typically depend on expert review of algorithmically proposed candidate matches. Moreover, most existing approaches focus almost exclusively on visual cues and overlook auxiliary information routinely available in field studies, such as image timestamps and camera-trap locations. We introduce Spotted, a location-informed, human-in-the-loop animal ReID framework that integrates visual similarity with spatio-temporal feasibility priors derived from camera locations, thereby reducing the amount of required expert review. Our method (i) computes an image-model-agnostic feasibility score based on the minimum travel speed required for two detections to correspond to the same individual, (ii) uses these feasibility cues as pseudo-supervision to train a lightweight head on top of a frozen visual foundation model, and (iii) fuses adapted visual similarity with spatio-temporal feasibility to obtain a robust pairwise matching score. We additionally integrate an active pair sampling strategy to accelerate annotation by initially prioritizing uncertain predictions. We evaluate Spotted on three challenging camera-trap ReID datasets comprised of spotted hyenas and leopards, which we release as part of this work. Our model improves average top-5 identification accuracy by 9pp, 2pp and 9pp over the best baseline on our LeopardID102, SpottedHyenaID109 and SpottedHyenaID415 datasets, respectively. Further, we show that our human-in-the-loop strategy reduces the number of queried comparisons by up to 69pp while achieving equivalent positive matches.

</details>


### [101] [Towards High-Resolution Visual Perception via Hierarchical Entity Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00816)
*Ziyu Ma,Shidong Yang,Yuxiang Ji,Yiming Hu,Tongwen Huang,Yong Wang,Jianfei Cai,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HEE 是一种无需训练、与模型无关的框架，通过查询引导的动态实体探索将静态高分辨率图像理解转化为层次化的主动感知过程，在多个 HR 基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像在 MLLM 中常因整体处理而丢失细粒度细节；现有方法要么需要额外训练教会模型"看哪里"，要么启发式地将图像划分为固定区域，在复杂 HR 场景中泛化能力不足。因此需要一种无需训练、能自适应探索图像关键区域的方法。

Method: 提出 HEE（层次化实体探索）框架：1）双重评分机制评估每个区域是否已包含回答问题的充分证据；2）若证据不足，在最有前景的区域进行目标检测，提取细粒度实体；3）将实体聚类为连贯子区域，组织成多层次语义层次结构；4）当深层区域仍无法给出可信答案时，置信度引导的回溯机制重新访问替代路径，确保自适应感知。

Result: 在两个复杂 HR 基准（Visual Probe 和 HR-Bench）上，HEE 在准确率和效率方面均优于 ZoomEye、RAP 等免训练方法；在 Qwen2.5-VL 和 LLaVA-OneVision 等不同 MLLM 上表现一致；在 MME-RealWorld 基准上也展现了泛化能力。

Conclusion: HEE 作为一种免训练、模型无关的框架，通过动态、查询引导的层次化实体探索，有效解决了 MLLM 在高分辨率图像感知中细粒度细节丢失的问题，具有优秀的准确性、效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: High-resolution (HR) image perception remains a key challenge in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as fine-grained details are often lost when the image is processed as a whole. Existing methods either require training to teach models where to look or heuristically divide the image into fixed regions, both of which struggle to generalize in complex HR scenes. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Entity Exploration (HEE), a training-free and model-agnostic framework that transforms static image understanding into dynamic, query-guided entity exploration. HEE first evaluates each region using a dual scoring mechanism to determine whether it already contains sufficient evidence to answer the question. If not, it applies object detection within the most promising region to extract fine-grained entities, clusters them into coherent subregions, and organizes them into a multi-level semantic hierarchy for deeper exploration. When deeper regions still fail to yield confident answers, a confidence-guided backtracking mechanism revisits alternative paths to ensure adaptive perception. Extensive results show that HEE outperforms training-free methods like ZoomEye and RAP in both accuracy and efficiency on two complex HR benchmarks (Visual Probe and HR-Bench), across different MLLMs such as Qwen2.5-VL and LLaVA-OneVision. Moreover, HEE demonstrates generalization on the MME-RealWorld benchmark.

</details>


### [102] [Training-Free Debiasing of Diffusion Models via CLIP-Guided Denoising Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00817)
*Dain Kim,Jinseo Kim,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TES（Text Embedding Steering），一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过两阶段策略（早期全局对齐+迭代去噪时CLIP反馈优化）直接优化条件文本嵌入，在不修改模型参数的情况下缓解扩散模型中的人口统计偏差，在公平性上优于现有方法并保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型存在人口统计偏差问题——中性提示词会持续生成性别和种族方面的刻板印象表征。现有方法要么需要昂贵的重训练，要么在推理时干预会损害图像质量和语义对齐，因此需要一种无需训练、低成本且不牺牲质量的偏差缓解方案。

Method: 提出Text Embedding Steering (TES)框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段进行早期全局对齐（early-stage global alignment），第二阶段在迭代去噪过程中利用CLIP-based反馈进行文本嵌入的细化优化（iterative denoising-time refinement），实现对属性引导的稳定可控，全程不修改模型参数。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion上的大量实验表明，TES在公平性指标上优于现有无需训练的基线方法，同时保持了有竞争力的图像质量。

Conclusion: 推理时的文本嵌入优化是扩散模型中实现公平感知生成的一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，为无需重训练的高效偏差缓解提供了新路径。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models achieve impressive visual quality, yet demographic bias remains a challenge, as neutral prompts consistently produce stereotypical representations across gender and race. Existing approaches remain limited by costly retraining or by inference-time interventions that often degrade image quality and semantic alignment. We propose Text Embedding Steering (TES), a training-free framework that mitigates demographic bias by directly optimizing conditional text embeddings during the diffusion process. We show that a two-stage strategy - early-stage global alignment followed by iterative denoising-time refinement with CLIP-based feedback - enables stable and controllable attribute steering without modifying model parameters. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion demonstrate that TES outperforms existing training-free baselines in fairness while maintaining competitive image quality. These results highlight that inference-time text embedding optimization is a practical and scalable solution for fairness-aware generation in diffusion models.

</details>


### [103] [Post-Training Pruning for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00927)
*Chengzhi Hu,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Mengjuan Chen,Zhikai Li,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiT-Pruning方法，针对扩散Transformer（DiTs）设计定制化的显著性准则和聚类感知的剪枝粒度，从能量视角平衡权重与激活的贡献，在高达50%稀疏度下仍能保持图像质量，显著优于现有剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiTs在图像生成中计算开销巨大，后训练剪枝是潜在解决方案。然而DiTs独特的架构设计和参数分布使得传统剪枝方法（尤其是为LLMs开发的方法）不适用：LLMs的显著性度量通过近似推导放大了权重的相对贡献，而DiTs中权重的量级远大于LLMs；此外现有剪枝粒度忽视了模型结构的差异，导致性能严重退化。

Method: 提出DiT-Pruning方法，包含两个核心创新：(1) 从能量视角设计新型显著性度量指标，平衡权重和激活的相对贡献，更有效地识别重要元素；(2) 观察到二维权重空间存在明显的聚类模式，据此采用聚类感知的剪枝粒度，实现有效的稀疏分配。

Result: 在多种DiT模型上广泛评估表明，该方法在高稀疏度下仍能保持图像质量。以FLUX.1-dev在MJHQ数据集512×512分辨率为例，50%稀疏度下CLIP分数仅下降0.001，远超近期剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 通过定制化的显著性度量与聚类感知剪枝粒度，DiT-Pruning有效解决了扩散Transformer的后训练剪枝难题，在高稀疏率下实现了几乎无损的图像生成性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have demonstrated impressive performance in image generation but suffer from substantial computational overhead and resource consumption. Post-training pruning offers a promising solution; however, due to DiTs' unique architectural design and parameter distribution, traditional pruning methods are inapplicable, leading to significant performance degradation. Specifically, prior methods developed for LLMs, which derive metrics through a series of approximations, amplify the relative contribution of weights in the saliency metric. In addition, weights in DiTs exhibit significantly larger magnitudes than those in LLMs. Moreover, existing pruning granularity overlooks variations in model structures. In this paper, we propose DiT-Pruning, which improves pruning performance by introducing customized saliency criteria and pruning granularity. We design a novel metric that balances the contributions of weights and activations from an energy-based perspective, enabling more effective identification of important elements. Furthermore, we observe distinct clustering patterns in the two-dimensional weight space. Accordingly, we adopt a clustering-aware pruning granularity, enabling effective sparse allocation. Extensive evaluations on various DiTs show that our method consistently preserves image quality, especially under high sparsity. For FLUX.1-dev at 512x512 resolution on MJHQ, DiT-Pruning achieves only a 0.001 loss in CLIP score at 50% sparsity, dramatically outperforming recent pruning methods.

</details>


### [104] [Stitched Embeddings: A Unified Latent Space for 3D Garments and 2D Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00829)
*Andrea Sanchietti,Riccardo Marin,Bharat Lal Bhatnagar,Yuanlu Xu,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stitched Embeddings 提出了首个无需物理仿真的统一框架，在单一双向潜在空间中实现3D服装重建与2D缝纫纸样推断，利用预训练3D基础模型和BoxMesh中间表示克服数据稀缺并大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装拓扑多样性远高于参数化人体，传统方法依赖物理仿真将2D缝纫纸样映射到3D几何，计算开销大；同时高质量服装建模数据稀缺，亟需一个仿真无关的框架来打通2D纸样与3D服装之间的双向桥梁。

Method: 提出 Stitched Embeddings 框架：(1) 构建双向潜在空间统一3D重建与2D纸样推断；(2) 利用预训练3D基础模型的几何先验缓解数据稀缺；(3) 引入BoxMesh作为关键中间表示，实现2D面板到3D构型的对齐，规避物理仿真计算开销；(4) 整体管线可微分，支持端到端训练。

Result: 在纸样重建任务上达到SOTA精度，同时显著提升效率；展示了从网格恢复纸样、基于2D纸样的3D编辑等新应用。

Conclusion: 该工作在神经3D视觉与物理服装制造流程之间建立了可扩展的连接，为服装数字化提供了高效、统一的解决方案。

Abstract: While garments are essential for realistic digital humans, their topological variety makes them much harder to model than parametric bodies. Traditional tailoring relies on 2D sewing patterns, yet bridging these patterns to 3D geometry currently requires physical simulations. We present Stitched Embeddings, the first simulation-free framework to unify 3D garment reconstruction and sewing pattern inference within a single bidirectional latent space. By leveraging the geometric priors of a pretrained 3D foundation model, our approach overcomes the data scarcity typically associated with high-quality garment modeling. We propose to use the BoxMesh as a critical intermediate representation to align 2D panels into 3D configurations without the computational overhead of a simulator. This architecture achieves state-of-the-art accuracy in pattern reconstruction while significantly improving efficiency. Furthermore, our differentiable pipeline enables novel applications, including pattern recovery from meshes and 3D editing from 2D patterns.
