Finally, this work provides a scalable link between neural 3D vision and the physical garment manufacturing pipeline. Project Page: https://andreus00.github.io/stitchedembeddings

</details>


### [105] [Learning Cardiac Motion Priors for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00955)
*Andrew Bell,George Webber,Andrew P King,Steffen E Petersen,Muhummad Sohaib Nazir,Alistair Young*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种用于心脏运动隐式神经表示（INR）的先验学习策略：联合优化的群体先验、权重平均的共识先验、自动解码器以及元学习。在UK Biobank短轴标记心脏MRI数据上，评估了各策略对跟踪精度、运动行为及优化轨迹的影响。结果表明，所有先验均显著优于随机初始化；共识先验简单有效，自动解码器在早期适应中更快恢复大变形，而元学习在50次迭代内保持最佳适应轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏运动估计中，为每个图像序列单独拟合INR耗时长且对优化轨迹敏感。通过学习先验可以引导优化走向合理运动场并加速适应，但目前针对心脏运动INR的先验学习研究仍然不足，缺乏对不同先验策略的系统比较。

Method: 在短轴标记心脏MRI（UK Biobank）数据上，对比四种先验学习策略：(1) 联合优化的群体先验；(2) 权重平均得到的共识先验；(3) 自动解码器（auto-decoder）；(4) 元学习（meta-learning）。从跟踪精度、运动行为和适应轨迹三个维度进行评估。

Result: 所有学习先验在早期适应阶段均显著优于随机初始化。共识先验简单但有效；自动解码器在早期适应时能更快恢复大变形；元学习不仅早期表现强劲，且在50次迭代内保持了最优的适应轨迹。

Conclusion: 学习先验对心脏运动INR的优化具有显著加速和引导作用。不同策略各有优势：共识先验简洁实用，自动解码器擅长快速捕捉大变形，而元学习在收敛轨迹上整体最优，为心脏运动估计中先验选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) are well suited to cardiac motion estimation, providing continuous, compact representations of motion fields. However, fitting an INR to each image sequence is time-consuming and sensitive to the optimisation trajectory. Learned priors can help guide optimisation towards plausible motion fields and enable faster adaptation, but learning priors for cardiac motion INRs remains under-explored. In this work, we compare four strategies for learning cardiac motion priors, including a population prior learned by joint optimisation, a consensus prior obtained by weight averaging, auto-decoders, and meta-learning. Using short-axis tagged cardiac magnetic resonance images from the UK Biobank, we evaluate their impact on tracking accuracy, motion behaviour, and adaptation trajectory.
  All learned priors substantially improved early adaptation performance compared with random initialisation. While the simple consensus prior was effective, auto-decoders recovered large deformations faster during early adaptation. Meta-learning achieved strong early performance and maintained the best adaptation trajectory over 50 iterations.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking Multi-Label Image Classification With Deep Learning: Taxonomy, Challenge, and Outlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00839)
*Xuelin Zhu,Xiu-Shen Wei,Jiawei Ge,Shuai Xu,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于基于深度学习的多标签图像分类（MLIC）的综述性论文。文章系统回顾了MLIC的背景、问题定义、数据集与评估指标，并提出了一个六类分类法：面向区域、面向标签、面向架构、面向表示、面向学习和面向数据的方法。最后，文章深入阐述了MLIC中的学习博弈及其对其他视觉领域的启示，并总结了关键挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签图像分类是计算机视觉的基础任务，广泛应用于自动驾驶、疾病诊断、推荐系统和移动服务机器人等领域。尽管过去十年中CNN、RNN和Transformer等深度学习范式极大推动了该领域的发展，但现有文献缺乏一个系统、全面的综述来整合和梳理这些进展。本文旨在填补这一空白，为研究社区提供一个整体性的视角。

Method: 本文采用综述方法论，首先回顾MLIC的背景（问题定义、常用数据集、骨干网络和评估指标），然后提出一个六类分类法来组织现有的深度学习方法：1) 面向区域的方法（Region-oriented）；2) 面向标签的方法（Label-oriented）；3) 面向架构的方法（Architecture-oriented）；4) 面向表示的方法（Representation-oriented）；5) 面向学习的方法（Learning-oriented）；6) 面向数据的方法（Data-oriented）。最后对MLIC中的学习博弈进行深入分析，并实证总结挑战与方向。

Result: 该综述构建了一个系统化的六类分类框架，对现有深度学习驱动的MLIC方法进行了全面梳理和归类，揭示了各类方法的核心思想与演进脉络。同时，文章对MLIC中的底层学习博弈进行了深刻阐述，并指出了该领域的关键挑战（如标签相关性建模、不平衡数据、细粒度识别等）与未来有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文为研究社区提供了一个关于多标签图像分类的整体性和系统性视角。通过统一的分类体系和深入的分析，该综述不仅有助于理解现有方法的优势与局限，也为MLIC领域及更广泛的计算机视觉任务的后续探索与创新奠定了基础、指明了方向。

Abstract: Multi-label image classification (MLIC), a fundamental task in computer vision, focuses on identifying multiple objects or concepts within an image, underpinning numerous read-world applications, such as autonomous driving, disease diagnosis, recommendation system, and mobile service robot. Over the past decade, deep learning paradigms based on convolutional neural networks, recurrent neural networks, and Transformers have significantly advanced this field, owing to their powerful capability in visual representation and relationship modeling. These advances have markedly improved the robustness, scalability, and generalization ability of MLIC models across diverse datasets and application domains. In this survey, we provide a comprehensive review of the deep learning-based literature on MLIC. Concretely, we first revisit the background, including problem definition, datasets, backbones and evaluation metrics. Next, we develop a plausible taxonomy for the deep learning-based MLIC approaches, organizing them into six groups: region-oriented methods, label-oriented methods, architecture-oriented methods, representation-oriented methods, learning-oriented methods, and data-oriented methods. Finally, we provide an insightful exposition of the underlying learning game in MLIC and its implications for other vision domains, and we empirically summarize the key challenges and research directions in MLIC while outlining promising avenues for future development. We believe this survey offers the research community a holistic and systematic perspective on MLIC, thereby facilitating subsequent exploration and innovation in this field and beyond.

</details>


### [107] [TRCGL-Net: A Long-Tailed Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Classification Framework with Generative Data Augmentation and Label Co-Occurrence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00975)
*Tong Shao,Hongshun Ling,Li Zhang,Jinjing Wu,Junke Wang,Yuan Gao,Fang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRCGL-Net，通过文本引导条件扩散模型生成尾类样本、通道重加权机制增强特征判别力、类别感知注意力定位病灶区域、以及基于标签共现的图卷积网络建模类别关系，在PadChest数据集上显著提升了长尾分布下胸片多标签分类中罕见病的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床胸片数据呈极端长尾分布，导致尾部罕见疾病分类性能严重下降。除数据稀缺外，还有两个内在因素：(1)复杂解剖背景下尾类病变表征被削弱；(2)头类在标签共现关系建模中占据主导地位。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 提出TRCGL-Net，包含四个核心模块：(1)可学习的文本引导条件扩散模型，在疾病语义约束下生成高质量尾类胸片样本，缓解类别不平衡；(2)通道重加权机制，通过强调疾病相关特征通道进行特征重校准；(3)类别感知注意力机制，生成类别特定注意力图以定位细粒度病灶区域；(4)基于标签共现的图卷积网络，建立类别间信息传播机制。

Result: 在PadChest数据集上，TRCGL-Net取得尾类mAP 0.4904、整体mAP 0.4408、mAUC 0.8989，超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: TRCGL-Net有效提升了长尾分布下罕见疾病的识别性能，缓解了胸片多标签分类中极端类别不平衡的影响，为智能医学影像诊断提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Chest X-ray multi-label classification is a core task in intelligent medical imaging diagnosis. However, real clinical data often exhibit extreme long-tailed distributions, leading to degraded performance on rare diseases in tail classes. This issue is not only driven by data scarcity but also by two intrinsic factors:1) attenuation of tail-class lesion representations under complex anatomical backgrounds, and 2) dominance of head classes in modeling label co-occurrence relationships. To address these challenges, we propose TRCGL-Net. First, a learnable text-guided conditional diffusion model is employed to generate high-quality tail-class chest X-ray image samples under disease semantic constraints, improving data diversity and realism of rare disease patterns while alleviating class imbalance and preserving pathology-consistent semantics.Second, a channel reweighting mechanism is introduced to perform feature recalibration by emphasizing disease-relevant feature channels, thereby improving feature discriminability under long-tailed distributions.A class-aware attention mechanism is further applied to generate class-specific attention maps, enabling the model to localize disease-relevant regions and focus on fine-grained lesion areas.Finally, a graph convolution network based on label co occurrence is introduced to establish an information propagation mechanism among categories. Experiments on the PadChest dataset show that the proposed method achieves a tail-class mAP of 0.4904, an overall mAP of 0.4408, and an mAUC of 0.8989, outperforming state-of-the-art methods. TRCGL-Net effectively improves recognition performance for rare diseases under long-tailed distributions and mitigates the impact of extreme class imbalance in chest X-ray multi-label classification.

</details>


### [108] [Mirror-Fusion Attention for Reflection-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00850)
*Ruixin Li,Jin Liu,Yuling Shi,Stefano Lodi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MFASSL框架，通过镜像融合注意力模块和反射一致性损失，将软反射先验注入自监督学习，在医学影像和人脸等近似双侧对称数据上，以极小参数量代价显著提升下游性能、校准性和反射鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法强推增广不变性，但严格的翻转不变性会抑制医学影像、人脸等近似双侧对称数据中具有信息量的左右对应关系。需要在保留不对称线索的同时，温和引入几何对称先验。

Method: 提出MFASSL框架：(1)基于估计的对称轴构建镜像配对视图；(2)设计轻量级镜像融合注意力模块(MFA)，实现镜像区域间的自适应token级交互；(3)在基础SSL目标上联合反射一致性损失和中层token对齐损失进行优化。基于ViT架构，无需修改骨干网络。

Result: 在CheXpert、BraTS、CelebA-HQ和WFLW四个数据集上，MFASSL在下游性能、校准性和反射鲁棒性方面均优于MoCo-v3、DINO、MAE基线（均使用ViT-B/16）。相比近期的等变SSL方法，以仅约2.7%的额外参数实现了更强且更一致的提升。

Conclusion: 轻量级几何感知先验可以有效补充基于不变性的自监督学习，为对称结构数据的表征学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Most self-supervised learning (SSL) methods encourage invariance across augmentations, but strict flip invariance can suppress informative left--right correspondences in approximately bilateral data such as medical images and human faces. We propose Mirror-Fusion-Augmented Self-Supervised Learning (MFASSL), a Vision Transformer framework that injects a soft reflection prior into standard SSL without redesigning the backbone. MFASSL constructs mirror-paired views aligned to an estimated symmetry axis and introduces a lightweight Mirror-Fusion Attention (MFA) module for adaptive token-level interaction between mirrored regions while preserving asymmetric cues. The base SSL objective is further coupled with reflection-consistency and mid-layer token-alignment losses. Across CheXpert, BraTS, CelebA-HQ, and WFLW, MFASSL improves downstream performance, calibration, and reflection robustness over MoCo-v3, DINO, and MAE baselines under matched ViT-B/16 settings. It also achieves stronger and more consistent gains than recent equivariant SSL approaches with only approximately 2.7\% additional parameters. These results show that lightweight geometry-aware priors can effectively complement invariance-based SSL.

</details>


### [109] [MoVA: Learning Asymmetric Dual Projections for Modular Long Video-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00858)
*Peiyuan Zhu,Shaoan Xie,Zijian Li,Yifan Shen,Namrata Deka,Harsh Shrivastava,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频-文本对齐中存在的时空错位和语义不对称问题，本文提出MoVA框架，通过理论条件分析设计了双非对称投影机制，在保持全局跨模态语义的同时解耦帧级概念，在多个视频-文本对齐任务上取得优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-文本对比预训练模型继承了CLIP等图像-文本模型的表征纠缠缺陷，这一问题在视频域被两类特性严重放大：(1) 时序错位——文本描述常仅对应特定时间窗口，其余帧与文本无关；(2) 语义不对称——帧级视觉细节与字幕级概念之间存在稀疏、双向且非等价的关联。无论短字幕还是长字幕，均无法有效解决这一纠缠问题。

Method: 本文首先建立视频与文本表征在时序维度及不同粒度上灵活对齐的理论条件。基于该理论，提出MoVA（Modular Long Video-Text Alignment）框架，学习双非对称投影：(1) 文本侧投影——自适应选择字幕的帧感知子空间；(2) 视频侧投影——解耦文本相关的视觉概念。该框架能保持全局跨模态语义，同时解耦演化的帧特定概念，并自然扩展至长视频与长字幕。

Result: 实验表明，MoVA在多个视频-文本对齐任务上超越了现有方法，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MoVA通过双非对称投影机制有效解决了视频-文本对齐中的时序错位与语义不对称问题，实现了可扩展至长视频和长字幕的灵活对齐，为视频-文本表征学习提供了新的理论支撑与实践方案。

Abstract: Contrastive pre-training has propelled video-text alignment, yet models often inherit the critical limitations of their image-text predecessors like CLIP, resulting in entangled representations. These challenges are severely exacerbated by two fundamental properties in the video domain: Temporal Misalignment, where textual descriptions often correlate only to specific, constrained temporal windows, leaving other frames text-irrelevant; and Semantic Asymmetry, which dictates a sparse, bidirectional, and non-equivalent relevance between frame-level visual details and caption-level concepts. This failure persists whether captions are short and temporally disjoint, creating ambiguity, or long and detailed, fostering entanglement between static objects and their temporal evolution. In this paper, we establish theoretical conditions that enable flexible alignment between video and text representations across the temporal dimension and at varying levels of granularity. Building on these theoretical insights, we introduce MoVA, Modular Long Video-Text Alignment, which learns dual asymmetric projections: a text-side projection that adaptively selects frame-aware subspaces of the caption, and a video-side projection that disentangles text-relevant visual concepts. Our framework ensures that the model can preserve global cross-modal semantics while disentangling evolving, frame-specific concepts and scale naturally to long captions and videos. Empirical evaluations show that MoVA outperforms existing methods in multiple video-text alignment tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [110] [TrajLoc: Trajectory-Attention Localization for Multi-Object Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00861)
*Omer Sela,Inbar Huberman-Spiegelglas,Michael Rotman,Sagie Benaim,Avi Ben-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrajLoc 提出了一种在图像到视频生成中控制多对象运动的新方法。它通过在每个帧上用高斯热图替代交叉注意力权重，为每个对象施加独立的、逐对象的空间约束，从而在拥挤场景中保持对象身份并精准遵循目标轨迹。在多数据集和骨干架构上验证，其 PSNR 平均提升 4.3 dB，轨迹终点误差降低 51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多条轨迹纠缠在共享的密集条件信号中，导致在对象数量增加、轨迹交叉或遮挡的拥挤场景下难以保持对象级别的对应关系。因此需要一种能够为每个对象单独施加严格空间约束的方法。

Method: TrajLoc 的核心思路是在注意力层中直接操作：1) 用当前帧目标位置为中心的高斯热图替换每个对象 token 的交叉注意力权重，实现逐帧、逐对象的独立空间定位；2) 通过可学习的嵌入为每个对象 token 携带轨迹与深度信息；3) 利用第一帧的外观编码替代对象 token 来保持身份一致性。该方法可适配不同架构的骨干网络。

Result: 在 6 个数据集上评估（支持最多 20 个同时控制对象，含分布外真实场景），在 CogVideoX 5B 和 Wan 2.1 14B 两个不同架构上，PSNR 平均提升 +4.3 dB，轨迹终点误差相比最强基线降低 51%。

Conclusion: TrajLoc 通过严格的逐对象空间约束机制，在无需共享密集条件信号的情况下，显著提升了多对象图像到视频生成中的视觉保真度和轨迹遵循精度，并展现出良好的架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Controlling the motion of multiple objects in image-to-video (I2V) generation requires preserving object identities while enforcing adherence to distinct target trajectories. This becomes particularly challenging as the number of objects increases and their paths intersect or occlude one another. Existing approaches entangle multiple trajectories within a shared, dense conditioning signal, making object-level correspondence difficult to preserve in crowded scenes. We depart from this paradigm and enforce a strict, per object spatial constraint that isolates instances independently. Our method, TrajLoc, achieves this directly within the attention layers by substituting the cross-attention weights of each object token with a Gaussian heatmap centered on its target location at every frame. The same per object token interface carries trajectory and depth through a learned embedding and preserves identity by encoding first frame appearance in place of an object token. Evaluations across six datasets, featuring up to 20 simultaneously controlled objects and out of distribution real world scenes, demonstrate that our method consistently improves both visual fidelity and trajectory adherence. Applied to two architecturally distinct backbones (CogVideoX 5B and WaN 2.1 14B), our approach achieves average gains of +4.3 dB PSNR and a 51% reduction in trajectory end point error compared to the strongest baselines. Project page: https://sela-omer.github.io/traj-loc/

</details>


### [111] [EFlow: Learning Evidence Flow for Long-Video Reasoning with Adaptive Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00867)
*Wenhao Zhang,Kuanwei Lin,Xuyi Yang,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EFlow 是一个证据优先的长视频推理框架，通过将时序定位与逻辑推理显式解耦，并引入置信度感知的反思机制，有效解决了"过早语义承诺"导致的证据偏差问题，在多个基准上持续提升长视频推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强的视频推理框架通常将时序定位和答案推理交织在单条轨迹中，导致早期语义假设会偏向证据定位，使得检索到的证据不完整，而不完整的证据又反过来强化错误推理。作者将这一失败模式称为"过早语义承诺"（premature semantic commitment），并以此为核心动机提出改进方案。

Method: 提出 EFlow 框架，基于 Qwen3-VL 构建，核心包含三个设计：1）通过"时序定位思维链"（CoT for Temporal Grounding）和"推理思维链"（CoT for Reasoning）显式分离证据检索与逻辑推理两个阶段；2）引入置信度感知的反思机制，当检索证据可能不充分时，对完整视频进行重新评估；3）构建专用轨迹数据集，并通过监督微调、强化学习和强化微调三阶段训练 EFlow。

Result: 在五个视频理解基准测试上进行了广泛实验，EFlow 持续提升了长视频推理性能。

Conclusion: EFlow 的证据优先设计范式有效克服了过早语义承诺问题，为长视频推理提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案，证明了将证据检索与推理过程解耦的重要性。

Abstract: Long-video reasoning is fundamentally constrained by how models acquire and utilize visual evidence. Existing tool-augmented video frameworks often interleave temporal grounding and answer reasoning within a single trajectory, causing early semantic hypotheses to bias evidence localization. We term this failure mode premature semantic commitment, where biased grounding retrieves incomplete evidence and incomplete evidence further reinforces incorrect reasoning. To address this issue, we propose EFlow, an evidence-first video reasoning framework built upon Qwen3-VL. EFlow explicitly separates temporal grounding and logical reasoning through CoT for Temporal Grounding and CoT for Reasoning, enabling the model to retrieve relevant evidence before answer inference. In addition, EFlow introduces a confidence-aware reflection mechanism that re-evaluates the full video when retrieved evidence is potentially insufficient. We further construct dedicated trajectory datasets and train EFlow through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across five video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that EFlow consistently improves long-video reasoning performance.

</details>


### [112] [EchoRisk: A Multicentre Echocardiography Dataset and Benchmark for Cardio-Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01039)
*Grigorios Kalliatakis,Georgia Karanasiou,Georgios Manikis,Manolis Tsiknakis,Dimitrios Fotiadis,Dorothea Tsekoura,Kalliopi Keramida,Vasileios Bouratzis,Lampros Lakkas,Katerina Naka,Andri Papakonstantinou,Anastasia Constantinidou,Kostas Marias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoRisk 是一个多中心、纵向超声心动图数据集，包含 422 名乳腺癌患者共 2,159 段视频。该数据集围绕治疗诱导的心脏毒性，定义了三个任务：LVEF 自动估计、LV 功能障碍分类和基线影像早期预测心脏毒性。论文提供了基于 R(2+1)D + LSTM 的基线方法，并作为 MICCAI 2026 挑战赛的官方基准发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗诱导的心脏毒性是乳腺癌患者中断治疗的首要非肿瘤原因，但目前缺乏利用常规心脏影像进行早期、自动化风险分层的方法和标准化基准数据集。

Method: 构建了 EchoRisk 数据集（422 例患者，5 个欧洲中心，2,159 段超声视频，1,123 次临床检查，最多 5 个纵向时间点）。定义三项任务：Task 1 利用 cine 视频自动估计 LVEF；Task 2 基于纵向影像分类 LV 功能障碍；Task 3 仅用治疗前基线超声预测心脏毒性。采用 Kinetics-400 预训练的 R(2+1)D 视频骨干网络 + LSTM 聚合作为基线模型，并明确了评估协议、主/次要指标和排名流程。

Result: 基线模型在心脏功能评估和 LV 功能障碍分类任务上表现出较强的判别性能，但仅基于单段治疗前视频进行早期心脏毒性预测仍是一个显著的开放难题。

Conclusion: EchoRisk 为心脏-肿瘤学领域提供了首个带有明确心脏毒性标签的标准化超声心动图基准，数据集、评估代码和基线实现已全部公开，以促进社区协作、对比和新任务专用架构的开发。

Abstract: Therapy-induced cardiotoxicity is the leading non-oncological cause of treatment interruption in breast cancer patients, yet early, automated risk stratification from routine cardiac imaging remains an unsolved problem. We present EchoRisk, the first curated, multicentre, longitudinal echocardiography dataset with explicit cardiotoxicity labels, released as the primary technical reference for the EchoRisk-MICCAI 2026 challenge. The dataset comprises 422 patients enrolled in the EU-funded CARDIOCARE prospective study across five European sites, yielding 2,159 echocardiography videos across 1,123 clinical exams acquired at up to five longitudinal timepoints, alongside a dedicated cohort of 280 patients with baseline imaging for early cardiotoxicity prediction. Three clinically grounded tasks are defined: automated estimation of left ventricular ejection fraction from cine video (Task 1), classification of LV dysfunction from longitudinal imaging (Task 2), and early prediction of therapy-induced cardiotoxicity from pre-therapy baseline echocardiography alone (Task 3). For each task we specify the evaluation protocol, primary and secondary metrics, and ranking procedure. We establish baseline performance using an R(2+1)D video backbone with LSTM aggregation trained from Kinetics-400 pretrained weights, demonstrating strong discriminative performance for cardiac functional assessment and LV dysfunction classification, while early cardiotoxicity prediction from a single pre-therapy video remains a significant open problem for the community. The dataset, evaluation code, and baseline implementations are publicly available to serve as a benchmark for further collaboration, comparison, and the creation of task-specific architectures in cardio-oncology.

</details>


### [113] [OmniView-Space: Reinforcing Spatial Reasoning via Multi-Perspective Spatial Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00881)
*Xudong Li,Mengdan Zhang,Peixian Chen,Jiaxi Tan,Zihao Huang,Jingyuan Zheng,Yan Zhang,Xiawu Zheng,Xing Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniView-Space 提出了一种通过多模态自我中心证据保持空间一致性的框架，包含多视角空间映射、工具引导的自我中心推理和认知图谱蒸馏三个核心组件，在空间推理基准上达到 SOTA，且蒸馏模型减少了对外部几何管线的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在空间智能方面存在不足，尤其是多步推理中难以动态地将证据重新锚定到查询所需的特定相机、物体或方向中心参考系上。

Method: 提出 OmniView-Space 框架：(1) 多视角空间映射（MPSM），将重建几何重新锚定为查询对齐的视觉认知图谱和文本空间图；(2) 工具引导的自我中心推理，训练交错策略以主动选择查询所需的自我锚点并请求相应 MPSM 证据；(3) 认知图谱蒸馏，利用 MPSM 生成的轨迹和自我帧奖励训练模型基于自生成认知图谱进行推理。

Result: 在单图像和多图像空间推理基准上达到最先进性能；蒸馏模型在保持同等性能的同时降低对外部几何管线的依赖。

Conclusion: OmniView-Space 通过以自我中心证据为核心的多模态空间表示框架，有效解决了 MLLM 在多步空间推理中参考系动态重锚定的难题，且蒸馏策略使模型具备更强的自主空间推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), as it requires coherent spatial scene representations beyond basic object recognition. Existing methods typically build such representations through textual reasoning or 3D reconstruction. However, they often falter during multi-step reasoning, particularly when required to dynamically re-anchor evidence to the specific camera-, object-, or direction-centric reference frames demanded by complex queries. To address this, we propose OmniView-Space, a framework designed to maintain spatial consistency through multimodal egocentric evidence. Our approach consists of three core components: (1) Multi-Perspective Spatial Mapping (MPSM), which re-anchors reconstructed geometry into a query-aligned visual cognitive map and a textual spatial graph; (2) Tool-Guided Egocentric Reasoning, an interleaved policy trained to actively select the ego anchor required by the query and request the corresponding MPSM evidence; and (3) Cognitive-Map Distillation, which uses MPSM-generated trajectories and ego-frame rewards to train the model to reason with self-generated cognitive maps. Experiments on single- and multi-image spatial reasoning benchmarks show that OmniView-Space achieves state-of-the-art performance. Furthermore, the distilled model maintains this performance while reducing reliance on external geometry pipelines.

</details>


### [114] [LongVQUBench: Benchmarking Long-Term Video Quality Understanding of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01086)
*Arpita Nema,Hanwei Zhu,Xi Zhang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongVQUBench 是一个面向长视频质量理解的综合基准测试，包含超过1200个多样化视频和1500个问答，通过局部事件质量理解、跨事件质量推理和全局质量理解三个递进层级，系统评估大视觉语言模型在长时间视频中的感知推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频质量基准主要关注短视频片段和孤立失真，忽视了长视频内容中的时间连续性、累积退化和推理复杂性，导致对LVLMs长时视频质量理解能力的评估存在空白。

Method: 构建LongVQUBench基准，涵盖电影、纪录片、监控、第一人称和动画五类视频；设计三个递进评估层级（LQU局部事件质量理解、CQR跨事件质量推理、GQU全局质量理解）；嵌入Needle Distortion QA范式，在时空维度稀疏插入失真以探测细粒度检测和推理能力。

Result: 在14个最先进的LVLMs上的实验表明，随着视频长度和推理深度的增加，模型性能显著下降，暴露出长程时间整合和感知归因能力的局限性。

Conclusion: LongVQUBench为LVLMs的长时视频质量理解提供了系统化、层级化和可解释的评估基础，揭示了当前模型在长时间视频感知推理方面的不足。

Abstract: The evaluation of long-term video quality understanding remains an open challenge for large vision-language models (LVLMs). Existing video quality benchmarks predominantly focus on short clips and isolated distortions, overlooking the temporal continuity, cumulative degradation, and reasoning complexity inherent in long-duration content. To address these limitations, we present LongVQUBench, a comprehensive benchmark for long-term video quality understanding. LongVQUBench contains over 1200 diverse videos spanning movies, documentaries, surveillance footage, egocentric recordings, and animated content, accompanied by 1500 multiple-choice and open-ended questions for validation and testing. To assess perceptual reasoning across different temporal scopes, we introduce three progressively complex evaluation levels: (i) local event quality understanding (LQU) for analyzing localized distortions; (ii) cross-event quality reasoning (CQR) for integrating multiple degraded events; and (iii) global quality understanding (GQU) for holistic perceptual evaluation over extended durations. Furthermore, a needle distortion question-answering (NDQA) paradigm is embedded across all three levels, where spatial or temporal artifacts are sparsely inserted to probe fine-grained detection and reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments on 14 state-of-the-art LVLMs reveal significant performance degradation with increasing video length and reasoning depth, highlighting their limited capacity for long-range temporal integration and perceptual attribution. We envision LongVQUBench as a foundational step toward the systematic, hierarchical, and explainable evaluation of LVLMs' long-term video quality understanding.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Pixel Overlap: A Framework for Decomposing Segmentation Evaluation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00886)
*Youwei Pang,Xiaoqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个五阶段分析框架，将二值目标分割的评估指标分解为预测表示、目标提取、目标匹配、分数计算和指标报告五个模块化阶段，用以系统性地分析和理解各类指标的设计选择及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二值目标分割领域的评估指标众多，但通常被视作孤立的公式，缺乏统一的系统性理解。作者旨在通过模块化分解框架，揭示各指标背后的设计假设与权衡，使指标间的差异和演进逻辑透明化。

Method: 提出一个五阶段分解框架：(1) 预测表示——决定如何将预测结果转化为可处理形式；(2) 目标提取——从预测和真值中提取目标区域；(3) 目标匹配——建立预测目标与真值目标之间的对应关系；(4) 分数计算——基于匹配结果计算数值分数；(5) 指标报告——如何汇总和呈现最终指标。利用该框架对代表性指标进行案例分析，展示新指标如何针对早期协议的特定缺陷进行改进。

Result: 通过框架分析表明，各指标的差异本质上源于五个阶段中不同的设计选择；新指标（如针对伪装、透明、玻璃等特定任务的指标）确实有针对性地修正了早期通用指标的不足；框架使每个指标的隐性假设变得可见，便于研究者根据具体任务选择合适的评估协议。

Conclusion: 该框架为二值目标分割评估指标的设计与分析提供了统一的模块化视角，揭示了指标设计的广阔空间，并强调了面向具体任务的评估协议设计的重要性。配套代码库PySODMetrics已开源。

Abstract: Evaluation metrics are central to binary target segmentation because they determine how progress is measured, compared, and interpreted. In this paper, target denotes the task-defined positive region to be segmented rather than a generic foreground object. It may be salient, camouflaged, transparent, glass-like, mirror-like, shadow-like, lesion-like, or defined by other application-specific semantics. We treat existing metrics as compositions of modular design choices rather than isolated formulas. The proposed framework decomposes each metric into five stages covering prediction representation, target extraction, target matching, score computation, and metric reporting. We use this framework to analyze representative metrics and show how newer metrics address specific limits in earlier protocols. The stage choices keep each metric's assumptions visible. We then discuss the design space opened by the framework and its implications for task-aware evaluation protocols. Reference code is available at https://github.com/lartpang/PySODMetrics.

</details>


### [116] [Geometry-Aware Cross-Height Channel Knowledge Map Prediction for UAV-Assisted Communications With Uncertainty-Guided 3D Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00887)
*Zhihan Zeng,Amir Hussain,Yue Xiu,Phee Lep Yeoh,Lu Chen,Zhongpei Zhang,Guan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的条件预测框架，用于无人机通信中跨高度信道知识图谱（CKM）预测。该框架融合城市场景先验、稀疏多高度观测和目标高度描述符，结合FPN-Transformer架构实现稠密CKM重建，并通过不确定性头支持成本感知的在线传感策略。实验表明该方法在零样本和小样本场景下均显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机通常只能从少数高度层获取稀疏的信道观测，而需要推断整个高度范围内的信道知识。现有方法难以充分利用城市场景的几何结构先验，且缺乏对预测不确定性的量化，无法支持在运动和安全性约束下的自适应传感决策。

Method: 提出几何感知条件预测框架，核心包括三部分：(1) 融合城市三维场景几何先验作为输入条件；(2) 采用特征金字塔网络(FPN)-Transformer架构，结合稀疏多高度观测和目标高度描述符进行跨高度CKM重建；(3) 引入不确定性头量化预测置信度，并基于此设计成本感知的在线无人机传感策略，在运动和安全性约束下优化主动重建。

Result: 在分层空中CKM基准上，FPN-Transformer在未见场景零样本协议下RMSE降至5.347dB（最强基线3D-RadioDiff为6.937dB），在传统patch-random协议下RMSE降至1.111dB（基线为1.221dB）。未见场景小样本适配后，10样本双高度支持下RMSE从5.347dB进一步降至3.518dB。不确定性引导的成本感知传感策略在传感预算40时将主动重建误差从初始6.94dB降至4.79dB，优于仅不确定性策略(5.08dB)和随机采样(5.84dB)。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何感知条件预测框架有效解决了跨高度CKM预测问题，FPN-Transformer架构在多种评估协议下均取得最优性能，不确定性引导的成本感知传感策略显著提升了主动重建效率，为无人机通信中的信道知识获取提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: Low-altitude Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) often need to infer channel knowledge across a range of heights from only sparse observations collected at a few altitude layers. To address this challenge, this paper studies height-conditioned cross-height channel knowledge map (CKM) prediction for UAV-assisted communications in geometry-rich urban environments. We develop a geometry-aware conditional prediction framework that combines urban scene priors, sparse multi-altitude observations, and target-height descriptors to reconstruct dense CKMs at unobserved target heights. An uncertainty head is further introduced to characterize prediction confidence and to support cost-aware online UAV sensing under motion and safety constraints. Experiments on a layered aerial CKM benchmark show that the proposed Feature Pyramid Network (FPN)-Transformer achieves the best overall performance under both unseen-scene zero-shot and legacy patch-random protocols, reducing the Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) to 5.347dB and 1.111dB, respectively, compared with 6.937dB and 1.221dB for the strongest baseline 3D-RadioDiff. Moreover, after applying our unseen-scene few-shot adaptation, the RMSE further decreases from 5.347dB in zero-shot prediction to 3.518dB with 10-shot two-height support, while the uncertainty-guided cost-aware sensing policy improves active reconstruction from 6.94dB at initialization to 4.79dB at sensing budget 40, outperforming uncertainty-only sensing at 5.08dB and random aerial sampling at 5.84dB.

</details>


### [117] [Foundation Models vs. Radiomics for Lung Computed Tomography: A Benchmark of Feature Extractors, Classification Heads, and Segmentation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01001)
*Nils Neukirch,Martin Maurer,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了CT肺癌影像中五种特征提取器、七种分类头和三种分割策略的组合，在五项任务上发现最优设计高度依赖任务类型：分割驱动体积/分期分类，分类器选择驱动生存/组织学/年龄预测。推荐Curia+肿瘤分割+CatBoost作为安全默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影像组学与基础模型的比较研究未能将特征提取器、分类头和分割选择的贡献分离，也缺乏跨队列鲁棒性测试。需要一项系统性基准研究来阐明各组件对不同任务的相对重要性，为小样本肺癌CT分析提供实践指南。

Method: 在两阶段设计框架下（避免端到端微调过拟合），系统基准测试5种特征提取器（Curia、Curia-2、DINOv3、Radiomics2D、Radiomics3D）× 7种分类头（TabPFN、TabICL、XGBoost、CatBoost、Random Forest、逻辑回归、Ridge）× 3种分割方案（肿瘤分割、肺部分割、无分割），在LUNG1（n=338，内部测试84）和外部LUNG2（n=211）上完成肿瘤体积分类、分期分类、2年生存预测、组织学分类和年龄预测五项任务，以最差跨队列性能作为主要评估指标。

Result: 主导设计因素具有任务依赖性：分割策略主导体积和分期分类；分类器选择主导生存、组织学和年龄预测。影像组学在肿瘤体积、分期和生存预测上具有竞争力（前两者部分源于标签衍生效应）；Curia系列在生存预测上达到可比峰值；DINOv3在各任务上均略逊一筹。Patch和切片聚合方法影响可忽略。Curia+肿瘤分割+CatBoost在三个主要临床任务中取得最佳平均排名，但针对特定任务优化始终优于跨任务默认方案。

Conclusion: 无肿瘤勾画时，Curia-2+肺部分割+逻辑回归是可行替代方案。两阶段设计适合小队列场景。研究者应根据具体任务选择组件组合，而非依赖通用默认配置。

Abstract: Radiomics is the established approach for CT-based lung cancer phenotyping, yet comparisons with foundation models rarely isolate contributions of feature extractor, classification head, and segmentation choice, or test cross-cohort robustness. We benchmark five feature extractors (Curia, Curia-2, DINOv3, Radiomics2D, Radiomics3D), seven classification heads (TabPFN, TabICL, XGBoost, CatBoost, Random Forest, logistic regression, Ridge), and three segmentation regimes on five tasks: tumor volume and stage classification, 2-year survival prediction, histology classification, and age prediction. Models are trained on LUNG1 (n=338) and evaluated on an internal test set (n=84) and the external LUNG2 cohort (n=211), with worst-case cross-cohort performance as the primary metric. The dominant design factor is task-dependent: segmentation drives volume and stage classification, while classifier choice drives survival, histology, and age prediction. Radiomics is competitive for tumor volume, tumor stage and survival (partly due to label-derivation effects for the former); Curia variants reach comparable peak scores for survival; DINOv3 falls slightly short across tasks. Patch and slice aggregation have negligible impact. We recommend Curia with tumor segmentation and a CatBoost head as a safe default, achieving the best mean rank across the three primary clinical tasks, though task-specific selection consistently outperforms any cross-task default. When tumor delineations are unavailable, Curia-2 with lung segmentation and logistic regression offers a competitive alternative. All pipelines use a two-stage design suited to small cohort sizes where end-to-end fine-tuning would risk overfitting.

</details>


### [118] [MG-RWKV: Multi-Grained Context-Aware RWKV for Temporal Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00902)
*Jingchen Ni,Cangjin Yu,Dan Jiang,Quan Zhang,Keyu Lv,Shannan Yan,Linyue Pan,Ke Zhang,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MG-RWKV 是一种基于 RWKV 的多粒度时序伪造定位框架，通过双向 RWKV 架构、多粒度专家混合（MG-MoE）和跨粒度一致性（CGC）三大创新，在 O(T) 线性复杂度下实现 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TFL 方法受限于 CNN 局部感受野或 Transformer 二次复杂度，新兴线性模型又难以兼顾全局上下文压缩与局部伪造突变感知。因此需要一种线性复杂度、能高效处理全序列且兼具全局与局部建模能力的方案。

Method: 提出 MG-RWKV 框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）双向 RWKV 架构，以 O(T) 复杂度捕获双向时序上下文；（2）多粒度专家混合（MG-MoE），在显式时间感受野上进行动态路由，根据伪造时长自适应选择粒度，增强决策可解释性；（3）跨粒度一致性（CGC），通过层级尺度配对和空间边界感知加权对齐相邻特征金字塔层级，抑制真实区域的假阳性。

Result: 在 Lav-DF、TVIL 和 Psynd 三个数据集上进行广泛实验，MG-RWKV 以低计算成本取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MG-RWKV 成功将 RWKV 的数据依赖状态演化机制引入 TFL 任务，在线性复杂度下实现了高效全序列处理，兼顾了全局上下文与局部伪造感知，为视听内容鉴伪提供了高性能、可解释且低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Driven by Artificial Intelligence-Generated Content (AIGC), the authenticity of audio-visual content is facing severe challenges. Temporal Forgery Localization (TFL) aims to precisely identify manipulated segments within untrimmed sequences. However, existing methods are limited by CNNs' local receptive fields or Transformers' quadratic complexity, while emerging linear models often struggle to balance global authentic context compression with local abrupt forgery perception. To address this, we propose MG-RWKV, a multi-granularity framework that leverages the data-dependent state evolution of RWKV to achieve efficient full-sequence processing with O(T) complexity. Our framework features three core innovations: (1) a Bidirectional RWKV architecture that captures bidirectional temporal contexts without quadratic overhead; (2) a Multi-Granularity Mixture of Experts (MG-MoE) that performs dynamic routing over explicit temporal receptive fields, adaptively selecting granularities based on forgery duration to significantly enhance decision interpretability; and (3) Cross-Granularity Consistency (CGC), which aligns adjacent feature pyramid levels through hierarchical scale-wise pairing and spatial boundary-aware weighting, effectively reducing false positives in authentic regions. Extensive experiments on Lav-DF, TVIL, and Psynd datasets demonstrate that MG-RWKV achieves state-of-the-art performance with low computational cost.

</details>


### [119] [Autonomous Scientific Discovery via Iterative Meta-Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01131)
*Bingchen Zhao,Sara Beery,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiscoPER 是一个基于LLM的自主科学发现框架，能够在无预设研究目标的情况下，通过动态生成和执行代码进行开放式数据集探索。其核心创新在于引入"二阶推理机制"，周期性地分析已积累的发现，从而识别结构模式、混淆因素和认知盲区，主动将探索重定向至未知区域。在 iNatDisco 多模态生态知识基准上，DiscoPER 以72.7%的假设支持率恢复了9个已知模式中的8个，优于经典因果发现和LLM引导的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主科学发现系统存在两大局限：（1）搜索空间受限或需要预设研究问题，无法进行真正的开放式探究；（2）虽然能迭代生成假设，但缺乏显式综合自身积累发现以揭示复杂关联现象的能力。本文旨在突破这些限制，实现真正的开放式、自反思的科学发现。

Method: 提出 DiscoPER 框架，核心包含：（1）LLM驱动动态生成并执行代码，无预设目标地探索数据集；（2）统计检验机制确保每项发现的科学有效性；（3）二阶推理机制（second-order reasoning），定期将先前发现作为经验数据进行分析，识别结构模式、混淆因素和认知缺口，重定向假设探索方向；（4）工具使用能力扩展搜索空间，支持从图像等多模态源提取信息。评估使用自建的 iNatDisco 多模态生态知识基准，其模式级真值来自同行评审文献。

Result: 在 iNatDisco 基准上，DiscoPER 恢复了9个已知生态模式中的8个，假设支持率达72.7%，显著优于经典因果发现方法和LLM引导的基线。消融实验表明 DiscoPER 性能随数据量增加而提升，并验证了二阶元反思机制的收益。

Conclusion: DiscoPER 展示了LLM驱动的自主科学发现系统在开放式探究中的潜力，二阶元反思机制是突破孤立搜索局限的关键，框架具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous scientific discovery systems offer the potential to accelerate research by automating the process of hypothesis generation and validation. However, current systems operate within constrained search spaces or require predefined research questions, limiting their capacity for true open-ended inquiry. Furthermore, while they generate hypotheses iteratively, they largely lack the ability to explicitly synthesize their own accumulated findings to uncover complex, interconnected phenomena. We introduce DiscoPER, an autonomous large language model-powered framework that conducts open-ended research by dynamically generating and executing code to explore datasets without pre-specified research objectives. To ensure rigorous scientific validity, every proposed discovery must pass statistical testing. To overcome the limitations of isolated search, our framework introduces a second-order reasoning mechanism that periodically analyzes its own accumulated discoveries. By treating prior discoveries as empirical data, DiscoPER identifies structural patterns, confounds, and epistemic gaps, actively redirecting hypothesis exploration toward uncharted regions of the search space. The search space is further expanded by incorporating tool use, enabling the system to explore hypotheses beyond structured metadata by seamlessly processing and extracting useful information from multimodal sources like images. Evaluated on iNatDisco, a new multimodal ecological knowledge benchmark with pattern-level ground truth obtained from peer-reviewed literature, DiscoPER recovers 8 of 9 known patterns with a 72.7% hypothesis support rate, outperforming both classical causal discovery and LLM-guided baselines. Ablations show that DiscoPER scales with more data, and confirms the benefits of second-order meta-reflection.

</details>


### [120] [Condensing Large-Scale Datasets Directly with Minimal Information Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00916)
*Xinyi Shang,Peng Sun,Bei Shi,Zixuan Wang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CIM是一种度量驱动的数据集蒸馏框架，摒弃了传统的双重压缩范式，通过显式量化并最小化原始数据与合成数据之间的信息差距，在ImageNet-1K上以IPC=10、80分钟内实现48.7%的Top-1准确率，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展数据集蒸馏方法（如SQUEEZE-RECOVER-RELABEL流程）存在两个关键瓶颈：(1) 从数据到模型再到图像的双重压缩过程导致严重信息损失；(2) 信息损失引发的分布偏移使得RELABEL策略失效，预训练模型退化为不可靠的标签器。亟需一种新范式来克服这些缺陷。

Method: 提出CIM框架，放弃双重压缩范式，转而采用度量驱动的方法：显式量化原始数据集与合成数据集之间的信息差距，并通过直接对齐数据分布来实现高保真信息压缩，同时天然满足有效重标签的前提条件。

Result: CIM在ImageNet-1K上于单张RTX-4090 GPU、IPC=10的设置下仅需80分钟完成蒸馏，ResNet-18上达到48.7%的Top-1准确率，分别超越NRR-DD和DELT方法2.6%和2.9%，在跨架构泛化方面也表现优异。

Conclusion: CIM通过度量驱动的数据分布对齐策略，从根本上解决了双重压缩带来的信息损失和分布偏移问题，在效率与性能两个维度全面超越了现有最先进方法，为大规模数据集蒸馏设立了新标杆。

Abstract: Recent advancements in scaling dataset distillation rely heavily on decoupled information extraction pipelines, comprising SQUEEZE, RECOVER, and RELABEL stages. Despite their scalability to large-scale datasets, these methods suffer from prohibitive computational overhead and poor cross-architecture generalization. In this paper, we reveal the root cause of these bottlenecks: the implicit dual-compression process, from data to model and back to images, inherently induces severe information loss. Crucially, we empirically and theoretically demonstrate that this loss creates a distribution shift that fundamentally compromises the widely adopted RELABEL strategy, transforming the pre-trained model into an unreliable labeler that yields sub-optimal labels. To overcome these critical flaws, we propose CIM, a novel, metric-driven framework that abandons the flawed dual-compression paradigm. Instead, CIM explicitly quantifies and minimizes the information gap between the original and synthetic datasets. By directly aligning the data distributions, our approach ensures high-fidelity information condensation and inherently satisfies the prerequisites for effective relabeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CIM establishes a new state-of-the-art. Notably, it distills ImageNet-1K at an IPC=10 in merely 80 minutes on a single RTX-4090 GPU, achieving an unprecedented 48.7% Top-1 accuracy on ResNet-18 and significantly outperforming previous SOTA approaches, such as NRR-DD and DELT, by 2.6% and 2.9%, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/LINs-lab/CIM.

</details>


### [121] [GMO-E$^2$DIT: Grounded Multi-Operation Editing for E-Commerce Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00920)
*Zipeng Guo,Xiaoan Liu,Lichen Ma,Cheng Wang,Yu He,Xiaolong Fu,Jingling Fu,Xinyuan Shan,Shaojie Guo,Luohang Liu,Junshi Huang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对电商图像编辑中多步骤、局部化、可审计的组合编辑需求，GMO-E²DIT提出了一种智能体框架，将VLM的规划推理与掩码条件的图像编辑器解耦，通过反思驱动的迭代循环实现可靠的多轮任务完成，并在新基准EComEditBench上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实电商图像编辑往往需要多重、局部化的组合操作，而非全局风格变换。现有一次性编辑器将意图解析、空间定位和图像合成混为一步，常导致部分编辑执行失败，在商业场景中不可接受。因此，需要一种能将认知推理与生成渲染解耦、支持结构化多轮编辑并能可靠保留中间进度、重试失败操作、从错误中恢复的框架。

Method: 提出GMO-E²DIT智能体编辑框架：(1)使用VLM智能体根据欠指定的用户指令，构建基于区域定位的编辑议程，将认知推理与生成渲染解耦；(2)通过操作感知的掩码和参考图像执行子程序编辑；(3)引入反思驱动循环，检查中间结果并决定下一状态，可靠保留安全的部分进度、重试未完成操作并恢复错误；(4)开发统一数据流水线，为规划、执行和反思提供对齐监督；(5)构建综合评测基准EComEditBench。

Result: 在EComEditBench基准上的大量实验表明，GMO-E²DIT与强闭源模型相比具有竞争力，在指令准确性和编辑保真度上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: GMO-E²DIT通过智能体架构将规划与生成解耦，结合反思驱动的迭代机制，有效解决了电商场景中组合式、多轮图像编辑的可靠性问题，展现出超越现有基线的性能，为商业级图像编辑提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Real-world e-commerce image editing often requires multiple, localized, and auditable operations rather than global restyling. This compositional nature poses a dual challenge: models must precisely apply all requested edits to the correct regions while preserving unmodified content, even under ambiguous instructions. Existing one-shot editors conflate intent resolution, spatial grounding, and synthesis into a single step, frequently resulting in partial execution failures, which is unacceptable for commercial scenarios. To address this, we introduce GMO-E$^2$DIT, an agentic editing framework that couples a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with a mask-conditioned image editor to tackle structured multi-turn task completion. Given an underspecified instruction, the VLM agent constructs a region-grounded edit agenda, effectively decoupling cognitive reasoning from generative rendering. The framework then executes sub-programs via operation-aware masks and references, utilizing a reflection-driven loop to inspect intermediate results and determine the subsequent state. This iterative mechanism reliably preserves safe partial progress, retries unfinished operations, and recovers from errors. Furthermore, we develop a unified data pipeline providing aligned supervision for planning, execution, and reflection, alongside EComEditBench, a comprehensive benchmark for instruction-driven evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMO-E$^2$DIT achieves competitive performance compared to strong closed-source models, yielding superior instruction accuracy and edit fidelity over existing baselines.

</details>


### [122] [Dataset Biases and Shortcut Learning in Motion-Based AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00948)
*Joren Michels,Lode Jorissen,Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种基于运动的AI生成视频检测器，发现它们存在严重的预处理和采样偏差——现有数据集中AI视频的运动幅度通常小于真实视频。当消除这种运动偏差后，所有检测器性能骤降至接近随机水平；而基于频率的检测器则表现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频质量迅速提升，人类已难以区分真伪。现有基于运动的检测器虽然在标准基准上表现良好，但其高准确率可能来源于数据集中的系统性偏差（如AI视频与真实视频之间运动模式差异），而非真正学习到了生成伪影。该工作旨在揭示这些脆弱性，推动更鲁棒检测方法的研发。

Method: 选取四种最先进的基于运动特征的AI视频检测器，系统性地识别并量化其预处理与采样偏差；通过数据集重平衡（消除AI视频与真实视频间的运动幅度差异）和简单空间增强（如翻转、旋转等）构建受控评估实验；同时引入一种基于频率域的检测器作为对照，在所有数据集上横向对比性能变化。

Result: （1）四种基于运动的检测器在消除运动偏差后性能均崩塌至接近随机猜测水平；（2）数据集重平衡和简单空间增强导致所有运动检测模型性能严重退化；（3）基于频率的检测器在所有评估数据集上保持稳健性能，展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 当前基于运动的AI视频检测器过度依赖数据集中的运动模式偏差，而非学习到本质的生成痕迹，其实际泛化能力被高估。频率域方法可能是实现鲁棒、可泛化的AI视频检测的更有前景的技术路线。作者呼吁社区关注这些漏洞，构建更具代表性、无偏的数据集和更严格的评估协议。

Abstract: The visual quality of AI-generated videos has improved drastically in recent years, making it increasingly difficult for humans to distinguish between real and synthetic media. In this work, we evaluate the robustness and applicability of four state-of-the-art motion-based AI-generated video detectors. We identify significant preprocessing and sampling biases in these methods and demonstrate that they account for a substantial portion of their reported performance. Furthermore, we find that these detectors are highly sensitive to motion patterns specific to their evaluation datasets, where AI-generated videos generally exhibit less inter-frame movement than real videos. We show that for all detectors, performance collapses to near-random levels when evaluated on a dataset that does not contain this motion bias. Additionally, through dataset rebalancing and the application of simple spatial augmentations, we observe severe performance degradation across all evaluated models. In contrast, we find that an existing frequency-based detector maintains strong performance across all evaluated datasets, suggesting that frequency-based approaches may offer a more generalizable path forward for AI-generated video detection. We hope that our work raises awareness towards these vulnerabilities and encourages the development of more representative, unbiased datasets and more robust evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [123] [World from Motion: Generative Dynamic Gaussian Reconstruction from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01202)
*Liyuan Zhu,Shengyu Huang,Amrita Mazumdar,Tianye Li,Zan Gojcic,Gordon Wetzstein,Iro Armeni,Shalini De Mello,Alex Trevithick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World from Motion (WfM) 提出一种从单目视频生成可自由渲染的动态3D高斯表征的方法。通过训练一个视频模型，以密集像素对齐的渲染为条件，结合外观、几何和3D场景运动信息来修正初始重建中的伪影并填补缺失区域，最终蒸馏为一致的高质量动态3DGS，在4D重建任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频的动态3D重建存在固有的重建伪影和不可见区域缺失问题。现有方法难以处理大视角变化和动态运动，生成的动态场景无法自由渲染。作者希望利用视频生成模型的先验知识来修正这些缺陷，实现高质量、可自由渲染的4D重建。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：生成对齐的多视角视频对和动态3DGS表示，并模拟单目重建的典型伪影；2. 训练视频模型：以密集像素对齐的渲染为条件，这些渲染沿输入和目标相机轨迹编码外观、几何和3D场景运动信息；3. 测试时蒸馏：将模型生成的新观测区域和运动信息蒸馏回单一一致的动态3DGS表示中。

Result: 该方法在4D重建任务上达到了新的SOTA，能够无缝泛化到具有大视角变化和动态运动的in-the-wild视频，同时改善了新视角合成质量和底层3D运动估计。

Conclusion: World from Motion利用视频生成先验有效解决了单目动态重建的核心挑战——伪影修正和缺失区域补全，为单目视频到可自由渲染的动态3DGS提供了一条可行路径，展示了强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present World from Motion, a method for generating freely renderable dynamic 3D Gaussian representations from monocular videos. Our approach conditions a video model on dense, pixel-aligned renderings that encode appearance, geometry, and 3D scene motion along both input and target camera trajectories to correct rendering artifacts and fill in missing regions from an initial reconstruction. To train this model, we construct a dataset of aligned multiview video pairs and dynamic 3DGS representations, with simulated artifacts characteristic of monocular reconstruction. At test time, we distill the model's generations, including newly observed regions and motions, back into a single consistent, high-quality dynamic 3DGS, improving both novel-view synthesis and the underlying 3D motion. Our method sets a new state of the art in 4D reconstruction and seamlessly generalizes to in-the-wild videos with large viewpoint changes and dynamic motions.

</details>


### [124] [GaussianEmoTalker: Real-Time Emotional Talking Head Synthesis with Audio-Driven and Blendshape-Based 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00959)
*Haijie Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yixuan Dong,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianEmoTalker 是一个基于3D高斯泼溅的实时音频驱动情感说话头合成框架，将情感动画建模为中性到情感的 residual deformation 问题，通过空间-音频-情感注意力机制实现可控强度的情感表情生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话头合成在唇音同步和视觉质量上已取得显著进展，但在实时约束下生成可控强度的表情情感头像仍然具有挑战性。直接预测最终情感头像的方式效果有限，需要一种更有效的情感动画建模方法。

Method: 1. 将情感动画建模为中性到情感的 residual deformation 问题，而非直接预测；2. 使用 GaussianBlendshapes 构建身份特定的中性说话空间，提供高保真高斯属性和音素同步的中性运动；3. 结合 mesh displacement cues、音频特征、情感类别和强度编码，预测情感条件的残差变形；4. 引入 spatial-audio-emotion attention 模块融合异构信号，估计高斯属性偏移量，实现表情丰富且时间稳定的渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明，GaussianEmoTalker 在视频质量、唇音同步精度、情感表情可控性及实时渲染方面均达到与最新情感说话头方法相比具有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: GaussianEmoTalker 成功实现了实时、高质量、可控强度的情感说话头合成，验证了基于3D高斯泼溅的 residual deformation 范式在情感动画生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Audio-driven talking head synthesis has achieved impressive progress in lip synchronization and visual quality, yet generating expressive emotional avatars with controllable intensity remains challenging, especially under real-time constraints. In this paper, we present GaussianEmoTalker, an audio-driven framework for real-time emotional talking head synthesis based on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Instead of directly predicting the final emotional avatar from speech, we formulate emotional animation as a neutral-to-emotional residual deformation problem. GaussianEmoTalker first constructs an identity-specific neutral talking space with GaussianBlendshapes, which provides high-fidelity Gaussian attributes and phoneme-synchronized neutral motion. It then predicts an emotion-conditioned residual deformation by combining mesh displacement cues, audio features, emotion categories, and intensity encodings. To fuse these heterogeneous signals, we introduce a spatial-audio-emotion attention module that estimates the offsets of Gaussian attributes for expressive and temporally stable rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GaussianEmoTalker achieves competitive video quality, accurate lip synchronization, controllable emotional expression, and real-time rendering compared with recent emotional talking head methods. Our project page is available at https://njust-yang.github.io/GaussianEmoTalker.github.io/

</details>


### [125] [Slope-Guided Mamba and Angular-Refined Transformer for Light Field Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00965)
*Li Jin,Jian Huang,Junde Lu,Shuai Wang,Hao Sheng,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SMART网络，结合斜率引导的Mamba与角度精炼的Transformer，解决光场超分辨率中空间-角度相关性建模不足及扫描机制与极线几何不匹配的问题，在五个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光场超分辨率方法存在两个核心局限：(1) 空间与角度维度常被解耦建模，限制了早期跨维度交互，导致几何不一致性；(2) 连续序列建模范式（如Mamba）的刚性扫描机制与极线几何结构存在根本性冲突，阻碍了几何感知的特征聚合。

Method: 提出SMART混合网络：(1) 角度调制空间模块（Angular-Modulated Spatial Module），将角度先验融入空间建模，弥合解耦鸿沟，增强空间-角度相关性；(2) 流形对齐轨迹模块（Manifold-Aligned Trajectory Module），沿极线结构实现几何一致的序列建模，解决扫描-几何失配问题。整体架构整合斜率引导的Mamba与角度精炼的Transformer。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上进行实验，SMART以0.42 dB的PSNR增益超越先前最优方法，且显著减少了伪影。

Conclusion: 通过混合设计协同Mamba的序列建模能力与Transformer的全局建模能力，并针对光场4D结构的几何特性进行专门适配，SMART有效克服了现有范式的根本缺陷，实现了光场超分辨率性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Light Field Super-Resolution (LFSR) necessitates accurate modeling of spatial-angular correlations while preserving intrinsic 4D ray coherence. However, maintaining such high-dimensional consistency remains challenging, primarily due to two inherent limitations in prevailing modeling paradigms. First, spatial and angular dimensions are often modeled in a decoupled manner, restricting early cross-dimensional interaction and leading to geometric inconsistencies. Moreover, although continuous sequence modeling paradigms show promise in representing epipolar structures, their rigid scanning mechanisms fundamentally conflict with epipolar geometry, limiting geometry-aware feature aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose a hybrid light field super-resolution network, termed SMART, which integrates a Slope-Guided Mamba and an Angular-Refined Transformer to effectively overcome these limitations. Specifically, we introduce an angular-modulated spatial module to bridge the decoupling gap, incorporating angular priors to strengthen spatial-angular correlation modeling. To mitigate the scan-geometry mismatch, we propose a manifold-aligned trajectory module that enables geometry-consistent sequence modeling along epipolar structures. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that SMART achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing previous methods by 0.42 dB (PSNR) with significantly reduced artifacts.

</details>


### [126] [Privacy-Preserving Depth-Only Open-Vocabulary 3D Semantic Segmentation Via Uncertainty-Guided Test-Time Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00978)
*Xuying Huang,Sicong Pan,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UTTO，一个面向隐私保护的仅深度开放词汇3D语义分割框架，通过不确定性引导的测试时优化，利用基础模型的语义先验来修正不可靠预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D语义分割依赖RGB图像获取语义线索，存在隐私泄露风险；而仅使用深度几何信息虽能保护隐私，但缺乏外观语义线索导致开放词汇预测高度不确定且不可靠。

Method: 提出UTTO（不确定性引导的测试时优化框架）：将预测不确定性转化为引导信号，识别不可靠的语义响应区域，并利用基础模型提供的语义先验对这些区域进行正则化修正，整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在ScanNet20、ScanNet40和ScanNet200三个数据集上，UTTO持续提升仅深度开放词汇3D分割性能，并在隐私保护条件下优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: UTTO验证了在隐私保护约束下，通过不确定性引导的测试时优化策略，可以有效利用基础模型先验来提升仅深度3D语义分割的开放词汇预测质量。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving perception is a critical requirement for deploying 3D scene understanding systems in real-world indoor environments, yet it remains underexplored in open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation. Existing methods typically rely on obtaining rich semantic cues from RGB images, which may expose privacy-sensitive visual information. Depth-only 3D geometry provides a privacy-preserving alternative, but the absence of appearance-based semantic cues makes open-vocabulary predictions highly uncertain and less reliable. Under this setting, we propose to convert uncertainty into a guidance signal to identify unreliable semantic responses and use semantic priors from foundation models to regularize their refinement. We present UTTO, an uncertainty-guided test-time optimization framework for depth-only open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation. Without additional training, experiments on ScanNet20, ScanNet40, and ScanNet200 demonstrate that UTTO consistently improves depth-only open-vocabulary 3D segmentation and outperforms representative baselines under privacy-preserving conditions.

</details>


### [127] [QCA: Query- and Content-Aware Keyframe Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00983)
*Jun Peng,Baiyang Song,Jie Li,Hui Li,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种查询与内容感知的关键帧选择框架QCA，通过联合建模查询相关性和内容偏差来动态分配关键帧预算，无需额外训练且可无缝集成到现有Video-LLM中，在多个长视频理解基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解面临严重的时间冗余问题：密集帧序列处理既语义低效又计算昂贵，且通常只有少数帧与查询真正相关。现有方法缺乏同时考虑查询相关性和内容多样性的关键帧选择机制。

Method: QCA框架分两步：（1）将视频划分为时间片段，通过联合建模查询相关性与内容偏差来估计每个片段的信息贡献，动态分配关键帧预算；（2）在每个片段内，以查询最相关的帧为锚点，迭代加入额外帧以最大化多样性，同时保持高语义相关性。整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个长视频理解基准上达到SOTA性能，例如LongVideoBench上使用128帧达到67.8%，而GPT-4o使用256帧仅达到66.7%，以更少帧数超越了更强模型。

Conclusion: QCA是一种高效、即插即用的关键帧选择方法，通过查询与内容双重感知实现紧凑而信息丰富的帧选择，兼具强性能和良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Video understanding is often plagued by severe temporal redundancy, where processing dense frame sequences is both semantically inefficient and computationally expensive. This challenge is further amplified when only a small subset of frames is truly relevant to the given query. In this paper, we propose a Query- and Content-Aware (QCA) keyframe selection framework that can select a compact yet information-rich set of frames from long videos. QCA first partitions the video into temporal segments and estimates the information contribution of each segment by jointly modeling query relevance and content deviation, and dynamically allocates keyframe budget to each segment. Within each segment, QCA anchors on the most query-relevant frame and iteratively incorporates additional frames to maximize diversity while maintaining high semantic relevance to the query. Crucially, our method requires no additional training and can be seamlessly integrated into existing Video-LLMs. Extensive experiments across multiple long video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed approach achieves state-of-the-art performance and has strong generalization ability. For instance, QCA achieves 67.8\% on LongVideoBench using 128 frames, while GPT-4o achieves 66.7\% using 256 frames. Our codes are available in \href{https://github.com/hktk07/QCA}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [128] [AVSR-Diff: Scale-Agnostic Diffusion Priors for Temporally Consistent Arbitrary-Scale Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00987)
*Geunhyuk Youk,Jeonghyeok Do,Dayeon Kim,Jihyong Oh,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVSR-Diff提出了一种解耦框架，将尺度无关的潜在去噪与连续坐标渲染分离，通过时间门控特征递归模块和尺度感知傅里叶细化模块，实现了任意尺度视频超分辨率，在保持高频细节和时间一致性方面超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型在VSR中局限于固定上采样尺度，而基于坐标的任意尺度方法在大缩放因子下存在严重过度平滑问题；将生成先验与连续解码结合虽有望解决此问题，但扩散采样的随机性会引入严重的时间闪烁。

Method: 提出AVSR-Diff解耦框架：(1) 将尺度无关的潜在去噪与连续坐标渲染分离，避免分辨率相关的重采样；(2) 设计时间门控特征递归(TGFR)模块提取严格对齐的时间一致性潜在先验；(3) 构建连续视频VAE解码器，引入尺度感知傅里叶细化(SAFR)模块动态适应任意目标尺度的频率分量。

Result: 在多种尺度下，AVSR-Diff一致地保持了高频细节和强时间稳定性，超越了最先进的任意尺度基线方法，甚至在固定尺度生成模型的原生分辨率上也表现更优。

Conclusion: AVSR-Diff成功将扩散模型的生成能力与任意尺度灵活性统一，通过TGFR和SAFR模块分别解决时间一致性和尺度适应问题，为任意尺度VSR设立了新的最优方法。

Abstract: Diffusion models have significantly advanced video super-resolution (VSR) but remain largely constrained to fixed upsampling scales. Conversely, while coordinate-based arbitrary-scale VSR methods offer scale flexibility, they inherently suffer from severe over-smoothing at large scaling factors. Integrating generative priors with continuous decoding is promising but currently hindered by severe temporal flickering caused by the stochasticity of diffusion sampling. To address this, we propose AVSR-Diff (Arbitrary-scale Video Super-Resolution with Diffusion), a novel decoupled framework that separates scale-agnostic latent denoising from continuous coordinate rendering, effectively avoiding computationally heavy resolution-specific sampling. Our approach introduces a Temporally-Gated Feature Recurrence (TGFR) module to extract strictly aligned, temporally consistent latent priors. Furthermore, we design a continuous video VAE decoder incorporating a Scale-Aware Fourier Refinement (SAFR) module to dynamically adapt frequency components to any target scale. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVSR-Diff consistently preserves high-frequency details and strong temporal stability across various scales, surpassing state-of-the-art arbitrary-scale baselines. Remarkably, our framework outperforms recent fixed-scale generative models even on their native resolution.

</details>


### [129] [SuperFlex: Deformable Superquadrics for Point Cloud Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01015)
*Gabriel Tavernini,Elisabetta Fedele,Tiago Novello,Leonidas Guibas,Marc Pollefeys,Francis Engelmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SuperFlex 提出一种增强的超二次曲面分解框架，通过新颖的损失函数、弯曲与锥化变形支持，以及针对局部点云的鲁棒性训练，显著提升了三维物体重建的精度和表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超二次曲面方法存在三个关键局限：重建精度有限、仅支持刚性基元、对局部点云缺乏鲁棒性。这些问题限制了超二次曲面在真实场景中的表达能力和应用范围。

Method: （1）提出新颖的损失函数，大幅提升重建精度；（2）引入弯曲（bending）和锥化（tapering）变形，使基元能够表示曲面和不对称几何；（3）利用高质量分解结果作为监督信号，训练一个对局部真实点云具有鲁棒性的模型。

Result: 实验表明，SuperFlex 在重建精度上显著优于优化类和基于学习的基线方法，同时保持了高度紧凑的基元表示。

Conclusion: SuperFlex 成功扩展了超二次曲面分解的表达力和适用性，在精度、灵活性和鲁棒性三个维度均取得实质性进展，为紧凑三维表示提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Superquadrics have proven to provide a compact, geometrically meaningful representation for 3D objects. However, existing methods suffer from limited reconstruction accuracy, are restricted to rigid primitives, and lack robustness to partial point clouds. In this work, we present SuperFlex, an enhanced framework that expands the expressive power and applicability of superquadric decompositions. First, we introduce a novel loss formulation which significantly improves reconstruction accuracy. Second, we include bending and tapering deformations, enabling high-fidelity representation of curved and asymmetric geometries. Finally, we leverage these high-quality decompositions as supervision to train a model that is robust to partial real-world point clouds. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in reconstruction accuracy over both optimization- and learning-based baselines while maintaining a highly compact primitive representation.

</details>


### [130] [GenAU: Language-Grounded Industrial Anomaly Understanding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01049)
*Hongkuan Zhou,Tristan Rehm,Nadeem Nazer,Lavdim Halilaj,Jingcheng Wu,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenAU 是一个统一的视觉语言框架，通过引入两个分割令牌 [SEG_defect] 和 [SEG_normal]，将图像级检测、像素级分割、多类型异常检测和缺陷分析整合到一个指令遵循模型中，在零样本多类型检测上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测需要超越简单的二值异常检测，实际系统应具备异常存在性判断、缺陷区域定位、缺陷类型识别以及可解释的视觉证据提供等能力。现有 CLIP 方法检测和定位好但缺乏语言级缺陷理解，而指令微调的视觉语言模型能描述缺陷却无法原生输出像素级掩码。

Method: GenAU 在视觉语言模型基础上引入两个分割令牌（[SEG_defect] 和 [SEG_normal]），其隐藏状态作为语言引导的查询，跨多尺度视觉特征进行像素级定位；图像级得分将该分割图与解码器的文本正常/缺陷决策融合，语言解码器生成结构化的缺陷感知响应。训练采用语言建模与分割联合目标。

Result: 在 VisA 和 Real-IAD 的跨数据集基准上，GenAU 在基于 CLIP 的零样本方法中取得了最强的图像级检测性能；分割性能接近但未超越专门的 CLIP 基线方法。

Conclusion: GenAU 以单一架构统一了工业异常理解的四大任务，成功实现了零样本多类型检测和语言引导的缺陷分析，在检测上达到最优，但分割性能仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Industrial inspection requires more than binary anomaly detection: a practical system should determine whether an anomaly exists, localize the defective region, identify the defect type, and provide interpretable visual evidence. Existing CLIP-based methods detect and localize anomalies well but offer limited language-level defect understanding, while instruction-tuned vision-language models can describe defects but do not natively produce pixel-level masks. We introduce GenAU, a Generalist vision-language framework for industrial Anomaly Understanding that unifies image-level detection, pixel-level segmentation, multi-type anomaly detection, and defect analysis in a single instruction-following model. GenAU augments a vision-language model with two segmentation tokens, [SEG_defect] and [SEG_normal], whose hidden states act as language-grounded queries over multi-scale visual features for pixel-level localization; the image-level score fuses this map with the decoder's textual normal/defect decision, while the language decoder produces structured defect-aware responses. Trained with a joint language-modeling and segmentation objective, GenAU covers all four tasks within one architecture and recipe, adding zero-shot multi-type detection and language-grounded defect analysis at a quantified cost to detection and segmentation. Across cross-dataset benchmarks, GenAU attains the strongest image-level detection among CLIP-based zero-shot methods on VisA and Real-IAD, with segmentation approaching but not surpassing specialized CLIP baselines.

</details>


### [131] [GeoSearcher: Anchor-Guided Progressive Reasoning for Remote Sensing Visual Grounding with Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01050)
*Dianyu Wang,Yidan Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Xuyang Li,Xiaoxuan Liu,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoSearcher将遥感视觉定位（RSVG）重新定义为锚点引导的渐进式推理过程，通过ACR-SFT和PF-GRPO两阶段框架，解决了遥感图像中小目标定位和复杂查询的难题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感视觉定位面临两大挑战：高分辨率遥感图像中大范围场景中目标极小且被大量视觉相似干扰物包围；查询通常包含参考对象、空间关系和目标属性等多重线索。现有MLLM方法将RSVG视为一步坐标生成，导致小目标定位不稳定和复杂查询处理困难。

Method: 提出GeoSearcher框架，包含两个耦合阶段：（1）ACR-SFT（锚点中心推理监督微调）：使用锚点中心推理数据，教会模型将关键视觉线索表示为锚点，并围绕锚点逐步整合位置、关系和属性线索；（2）PF-GRPO（过程忠实群组相对策略优化）：引入过程感知奖励（PAR）和推理信息样本选择器（RISS），通过联合评估关键推理步骤和目标定位来优化推理行为，聚焦训练对渐进推理更有益的样本。

Result: 在DIOR-RSVG、OPT-RSVG和VRS-Bench三个数据集上的广泛实验表明，GeoSearcher超越了现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将大规模视觉搜索转化为更受约束的局部推理过程，GeoSearcher有效解决了遥感视觉定位中小目标定位和复杂查询的核心难题，验证了渐进式推理策略在RSVG任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong cross-modal understanding and coordinate generation abilities in visual grounding. However, transferring these abilities to remote sensing visual grounding (RSVG) remains challenging. High-resolution remote sensing images usually cover large-scale scenes, where targets are often extremely small and surrounded by numerous visually similar distractors. Meanwhile, queries often contain multiple clues, such as reference objects, spatial relations, and target attributes. Existing MLLM-based methods usually formulate RSVG as one-step coordinate generation, which may lead to unstable predictions for small-object localization and complex queries. To address these challenges, we propose GeoSearcher, which reformulates RSVG as an anchor-guided progressive reasoning process and realizes it through two coupled stages: Anchor-Centric Reasoning Supervised Fine-Tuning (ACR-SFT) and Process-Faithful Group Relative Policy Optimization (PF-GRPO). In ACR-SFT, anchor-centric reasoning data are used to teach the model to represent key visual clues as anchors and progressively integrate location, relational, and attribute clues around them. In PF-GRPO, Process-Aware Reward (PAR) and Reasoning-Informative Sample Selector (RISS) further optimize this reasoning behavior by jointly evaluating key reasoning steps and target localization, while focusing training on samples that are more beneficial for improving progressive reasoning. Through this design, GeoSearcher transforms large-scale visual search into a more constrained local reasoning process. Extensive experiments on DIOR-RSVG, OPT-RSVG, and VRS-Bench show that GeoSearcher outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. The project will be released at https://github.com/wangdianyu954-xixi/GeoSearcher.

</details>


### [132] [CPDDNet: Color-Polarization Denoising and Demosaicking Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01100)
*Qihang Zhang,Yusuke Monno,Masayuki Tanaka,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CPDDNet联合框架，通过特征融合模块同时执行颜色-偏振图像去噪与去马赛克，在真实数据集上显著提升图像质量和偏振参数精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPFA传感器在低光照下输出的原始马赛克图像存在严重噪声和分辨率损失，现有方法将去噪和去马赛克分离处理，忽略了两者之间的耦合关系及共享的低层特征。

Method: 提出CPDDNet（Color-Polarization Denoising and Demosaicking Network），一个联合去噪与CPFA插值的端到端框架，核心是特征融合模块，在去噪和去马赛克两个阶段均保留CPFA原始数据的特征。

Result: 在真实数据集上，CPDDNet显著提升了图像质量和偏振参数（如线偏振度、偏振角）的精度，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 联合处理去噪与去马赛克任务、利用特征融合保留CPFA原始数据特征，是提升低光照下CPFA成像质量的有效策略。

Abstract: Color-polarization imaging using a color-polarization filter array (CPFA) sensor captures both texture (color intensity) and physical (polarization) information of the scene in a single shot, enabling various applications in computer vision. However, the raw mosaic output from a CPFA sensor often suffers from severe noise and resolution loss, especially under low-light conditions. Existing methods generally focus on either denoising or demosaicking tasks, failing to capture the coupling between them and neglecting shared low-level features. In this paper, we propose a color-polarization denoising and demosaicking network (CPDDNet), which is a joint framework that performs noise removal and CPFA interpolation using a feature fusion module that retains the features from the CPFA raw data at both the denoising and the demosaicking stages. Experimental results demonstrate that CPDDNet significantly enhances image quality and polarization parameter accuracy, outperforming existing approaches on a real dataset.

</details>


### [133] [MoHallBench: A Benchmark for Motion Hallucination in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01117)
*Jiale Li,Sihan Chen,Mengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoHallBench 是一个用于诊断视频大语言模型中运动幻觉的基准测试，涵盖三种幻觉来源（共现先验、序列推理、相似性混淆），包含 11,306 个视频片段和 40,493 个问答对，揭示当前模型在对抗性负样本上表现薄弱，序列推理幻觉最为严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VideoLLM 幻觉基准主要关注物体幻觉或粗粒度动作感知，忽视了视频特有的运动幻觉问题——即模型推断出视频中不存在的人体运动。本文旨在系统性地诊断和评估这一被忽视的问题。

Method: 提出 MoHallBench 基准，系统评估三大幻觉来源：共现先验（co-occurrence priors）、序列推理（sequential inference）和相似性混淆（similarity confusion）。数据集包含 11,306 个视频片段和 40,493 个问答对，覆盖二选一、多选和生成式三种设置。引入双向提问协议（bi-directional questioning protocol）和偏倚感知指标（bias-aware metrics），以减轻二值评估中的肯定偏倚。

Result: 在 10 个近期开源 VideoLLM 上的实验表明：动作识别能力与幻觉抵抗能力之间存在明显脱耦——在正向动作识别上表现良好的模型，在对抗性负样本上往往失败。三类幻觉中，序列推理幻觉最为严重，说明当前模型倾向于从局部运动线索过度推断预期结果。更强的先验和更细粒度的相似性会显著放大幻觉。

Conclusion: MoHallBench 揭示了当前 VideoLLM 在运动幻觉方面的系统性缺陷，尤其是序列推理幻觉的严重性，希望该基准能推动未来对视频大语言模型运动幻觉的评估与缓解研究。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) have shown strong progress in video understanding, yet they still suffer from hallucinations that are inconsistent with visual evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on object hallucination or coarse action perception, leaving a key video-specific problem underexplored: motion hallucination, in which models infer human motions that are absent from the video. We present MoHallBench, a benchmark for diagnosing motion hallucination in VideoLLMs. MoHallBench systematically evaluates three major sources of hallucination: co-occurrence priors, sequential inference, and similarity confusion. It contains 11,306 video clips and 40,493 question-answer pairs, covering binary-choice, multiple-choice, and generative settings. We further introduce a bi-directional questioning protocol with bias-aware metrics to reduce affirmation bias in binary evaluation. Experiments on ten recent open-source VideoLLMs reveal a clear decoupling between action recognition and hallucination resistance, as models that perform well on positive action recognition often fail on adversarial negatives. Among all settings, sequential inference hallucination is the most severe, showing that current models tend to over-infer expected outcomes from partial motion cues. Our analyses further confirm that stronger priors and finer-grained similarity substantially amplify hallucination. We hope MoHallBench can facilitate future evaluation and mitigation of motion hallucination in VideoLLMs.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Metric-Agnostic Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01133)
*Markus Knoche,Daan de Geus,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前轨迹预测模型为不同基准指标定制训练目标，但这些指标会鼓励相互冲突的行为。本文提出范式转变：用指标无关的概率目标训练模型，将指标优化作为后处理下游任务，通过TraDiE策略将预测分布映射为指标所需轨迹集，并基于DONUT-NLL在Waymo数据集上取得全指标SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Argoverse 2和Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的SOTA轨迹预测模型为不同基准指标量身定制训练目标，但这些指标（如minADE、minFDE、Miss Rate、mAP等）鼓励相互冲突的预测行为，导致无法用一个统一目标同时优化所有指标。

Method: 提出Trajectory Distribution Evaluation (TraDiE)策略——一组与指标特定的策略，将预测概率分布映射为轨迹预测指标所需的K条轨迹及其置信度。具体实现上，提出DONUT-NLL，将DONUT模型的训练目标改为直接优化预测分布（负对数似然），使其成为指标无关的概率模型，再通过TraDiE策略针对各指标进行下游优化。

Result: DONUT-NLL结合TraDiE策略在Waymo运动预测基准的所有指标上均达到SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 将训练目标与指标优化解耦——训练阶段专注于学习准确的预测分布，指标优化作为下游任务——是一种更优的范式，能同时提升多个原本相互冲突的预测指标。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory forecasting of surrounding traffic participants is a core capability for autonomous driving, enabling vehicles to anticipate behavior and plan safe maneuvers. We observe that current state-of-the-art forecasting models on Argoverse 2 and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset tailor their training objectives to the different benchmark metrics. Because these metrics encourage conflicting behavior, we propose a paradigm change for trajectory forecasting: training models with metric-agnostic probabilistic objectives and treating metric optimization as a downstream task applied to the predictive distribution. Concretely, we introduce Trajectory Distribution Evaluation (TraDiE) policies, metric-specific policies that map a predictive distribution to the set of $K$ trajectories and confidences required by trajectory forecasting metrics. We evaluate this framework by introducing DONUT-NLL, which adapts the training objective of the state-of-the-art trajectory forecasting model DONUT to directly optimize the predictive distribution. Using our policies, DONUT-NLL achieves state-of-the-art results on all metrics of the Waymo motion prediction benchmark.

</details>


### [135] [SD-RouteFusion: Ego-Trajectory Prediction with SD-Map Route Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01139)
*Sviatoslav Voloshyn,Bruno K. W. Martens,Wangxin Liu,Jakob Vinkås,Junsheng Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SD-RouteFusion 是一种可部署的端到端自车轨迹预测方法，融合前视摄像头、车辆运动学和标准定义地图导航路线，无需高精地图即可实现路线感知的轨迹预测，在8秒预测时域上相比基线实现16.9%的ADE下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路线感知的轨迹预测方法普遍依赖高精地图（HD-map）几何信息，但高精地图的构建和维护成本高昂、覆盖范围有限，难以大规模部署。本文旨在利用可规模化获取的标准定义（SD）地图路线信息，在不依赖HD-map基础设施的条件下，实现鲁棒的路线感知自车轨迹预测。

Method: 提出SD-RouteFusion：一个端到端融合框架，输入为前视摄像头图像、车辆运动学状态和SD地图导航路线。核心设计包括：（1）将SD路线先验嵌入预测网络，提供长时域语义引导；（2）采用双假设（dual-hypothesis）设计配合门控分类器，以应对路线损坏和视觉不确定性带来的干扰，增强鲁棒性。此外，发布了一个SD路线生成工具包，支持在任意包含自车位姿与未来轨迹的数据集上生成SD路线条件。

Result: 在覆盖10个欧洲国家和美国的48万驾驶场景大规模真实数据集上验证：仅加入SD路线先验，相比图像+运动学基线即实现10.5%的ADE改善；完整的融合策略在8秒预测时域上达到16.9%的ADE下降。

Conclusion: SD-RouteFusion 证明了SD地图路线是一种强大且实用的长时域语义先验，为大规模、可部署的路线感知自车轨迹预测提供了一条切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper presents SD-RouteFusion, a deployable end-to-end ego-trajectory prediction method that fuses a front-facing camera, vehicle kinematics, and a navigation route derived from a Standard Definition (SD) map. Unlike approaches that rely on High Definition (HD) map geometry, SD-RouteFusion aligns the learning objective with scalable and production-ready SD-map route inputs, enabling route-aware prediction without requiring HD-map infrastructure. First, we demonstrate that SD-map route prior provides a powerful long-horizon semantic prior. Through a comprehensive study on a large-scale real-world dataset comprising 480k driving scenarios across 10 European countries and the U.S., we quantify the value of SD-route conditioning: incorporating SD-map routes yields a 10.5% ADE improvement over an image-and-kinematics baseline, while our full fusion strategy achieves a 16.9% ADE reduction given a prediction horizon of 8 seconds. The fusion strategy consists of a dual-hypothesis design paired with a gated classifier, to ensure robustness under route corruption and visual uncertainty. Finally, to support broader evaluation, we release an SD-route generation toolkit that enables SD-route-conditioned ego-trajectory prediction on all datasets containing ego pose and future trajectories. Together, SD-RouteFusion establishes a practical path toward robust, route-aware ego-trajectory prediction at scale.

</details>


### [136] [Relation-Centric Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussian Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01140)
*Eunsung Cha,Hyunjoon Lee,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PairGS 将 3D 高斯分割重新定义为建模高斯点之间的成对关系，通过构建稀疏关系图实现无需繁重优化的开放词汇分割，速度比基于优化的实例特征方法快 50 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇 3D 高斯分割方法存在两难困境：将语言知识嵌入单个高斯点的方法容易产生噪声分割，而基于逐高斯实例特征优化的方法虽然准确但计算开销大、需要逐场景优化。论文旨在找到一种既能保证分割精度又无需昂贵优化的新范式。

Method: PairGS 提出基于成对关系建模的分割框架：(1) 利用 3D 高斯表示中固有的丰富信号（如视角贡献权重、多视图掩码证据）来显式构建关系图；(2) 使用低维描述符提出稀疏边候选集；(3) 仅在候选边上计算精确的成对亲和度；(4) 构建层次聚类树以支持多粒度查询。

Result: 在开放词汇 3D 高斯分割基准上达到 state-of-the-art 性能；快速变体比基于优化的实例特征方法快 50 倍。

Conclusion: 将分割问题重构为高斯点间成对关系建模，能够有效利用 3D 高斯表示的内在信号，在保持高精度的同时显著提升效率，为开放词汇 3D 场景理解提供了高效可行的新思路。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D Gaussian segmentation is challenging because it requires language understanding for diverse queries and accurate separation of Gaussians along object boundaries. Prior approaches either embed language knowledge into individual Gaussians to improve query responsiveness or optimize per-Gaussian instance features to encode object identity. However, these strategies may produce noisy Gaussian segmentations or rely on cost-inefficient per-scene optimization. We propose PairGS, a framework that reframes Gaussian segmentation as modeling pairwise relations between Gaussians. 3D Gaussian representations provide rich signals for relation estimation, such as view contribution weights and multi-view mask evidence. By leveraging these cues, PairGS explicitly constructs a relation graph for segmentation without a heavy optimization process. PairGS first proposes sparse edge candidates using low-dimensional descriptors, computes precise pairwise affinities only on those candidates, and builds a hierarchical cluster tree for multi-granular querying. It achieves state-of-the-art results on open-vocabulary 3D Gaussian segmentation benchmarks, while the fast variant is 50x faster than optimization-based instance-feature approaches.

</details>


### [137] [EquiSteer: Cross-Attention Steering Towards a Fairer Text-Guided Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01147)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Akshit Achara,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EquiSteer 是一种无需训练、基于推理阶段交叉注意力引导的去偏方法，通过预计算对比提示的引导向量，结合提示感知门控机制，在保持图像质量的同时将性别偏差平均降低 87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文生图扩散模型在生成"护士"、"CEO"等中性职业提示时，会因训练数据统计偏差而倾向某一性别。现有去偏方法需要重新训练、批次级控制或针对特定提示调参，可扩展性差。

Method: 提出 EquiSteer：一种无需训练、按样本独立工作的推理时去偏方法。首先，针对每个目标属性，从对比提示对中预计算"引导向量"；生成时，通过提示感知门控判断提示是否已包含属性信息——若已含属性则不做干预，若为中性提示则清除交叉注意力中的既有属性信号并注入目标属性引导向量。

Result: 在 SD-1.5、SD-2.1、SDXL 和 SANA 四个主流扩散模型上进行验证，EquiSteer 平均将性别偏差（parity gap）降低 87%，同时对图像质量和图文对齐度影响极小。

Conclusion: EquiSteer 是一种高效、可扩展的推理时去偏方案，无需训练即可逐样本纠正扩散模型中的性别偏差，为文生图模型的公平性部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models power everyday creative tasks, but they still reproduce the demographic biases in their training data. On common prompts such as ``a photo of a nurse,'' ``a photo of a CEO'', they skew their outputs toward one gender, driven by the statistics of training data rather than anything in the text. Existing debiasing methods show promise in narrow settings but require retraining, batch-level control, or prompt-specific tuning, limiting their scalability. We propose \emph{EquiSteer}, a training-free method that works per sample by steering cross-attention (CA) activations at inference time. For each target attribute, EquiSteer precomputes steering vectors from contrastive prompts. Then at generation time, a prompt-aware gate leaves attribute-specific prompts untouched, while for neutral ones it clears existing attribute signals from the CA activations and injects a target attribute. Across SD-1.5, SD-2.1, SDXL, and SANA, EquiSteer reduces the average parity gap by up to $87\%$, with minimal effect on image quality and text-image alignment. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Atmyre/EquiSteer}{https://github.com/Atmyre/EquiSteer}.%

</details>


### [138] [High-dimensional Embedding Prior for Noisy K-space Domain MRIReconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01176)
*Yu Guan,Tianjia Huang,Qinrong Cai,Qiuyun Fan,Dong Liang,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高维表示提升（representation lifting）的统一k空间MRI重建框架，通过在增强的数据表示空间上运行扩散模型求解器，显著改善噪声环境下的MRI重建质量，尤其在高噪声场景中增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的MRI重建方法在现实采集条件下的噪声环境中表现不佳，尤其是直接在k空间域操作时。需要一种不修改底层优化过程、但能增强扩散求解器抗噪能力的通用方法。

Method: 提出高维k空间重建框架，通过表示提升（representation lifting）增强数据表示空间，使现有扩散模型求解器能够在更丰富的k空间嵌入上运行。该方法不对底层优化过程进行修改，而是通过扩充表示维度来提升表达能力和鲁棒性。

Result: 在自建数据集和公开数据集上进行大量实验，涵盖不同噪声水平和欠采样因子。结果表明该框架能够持续提升多种扩散-based逆问题求解器的重建质量，高噪声场景下提升最为显著，与误差传播理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 高维表示提供了一种通用的、模型无关的机制来改善噪声环境下的扩散模型MRI重建，为鲁棒k空间生成建模在实用逆问题中的应用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction under realistic acquisition conditions can be fundamentally viewed as estimating the underlying k-space distribution from incomplete and noise-corrupted measurements. While diffusion models have recently shown strong potential as generative prior for inverse problems,existingapproachesstruggletohandlenoisyreconstruction settings, especially when operating directly in k-space domain. In this work, we propose a unified high-dimensional k-space reconstruction framework tailored for noisy inverse problems, whichenhancesdiffusion-based solversthroughrepresentation lifting.Ratherthanmodifyingthe underlying optimization procedures, the proposed framework augments the data representation space, enabling existing diffusion-based solvers to operate on enriched k-space embeddings with improved expressiveness. Extensive experiments on both in-house and public datasets across varying noise levels and undersampled factors demonstrate that the proposed frame work consistently improves reconstruction quality for multiple diffusion-based inverse solvers. Notably, the largest gains are observed in high-noise regimes, which is consistent with our theoretical analysis of error propagation under high-dimensional representation. These results suggest that high-dimensional representation provides a general and model-agnostic mechanism for improving diffusion-based MRI reconstruction in noisy settings, offering a new perspective on robust k-space generative modeling for practical inverse problems. The code will be available at https://github.com/yqx7150/HEP-MRIRec.

</details>


### [139] [Perceive-to-Reason: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01191)
*Hongxing Li,Xiufeng Huang,Dingming Li,Wenjing Jiang,Zixuan Wang,Haolei Xu,Hanrong Zhang,Haiwen Hong,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2R 将细粒度视觉推理解耦为"感知-推理"两阶段框架，先定位关键视觉证据再推理回答，并配合 PRA-GRPO 强化学习策略，在多个高分辨率基准上显著提升了 VLMs 的性能。


<details>
