Unlike translated benchmarks, MSQA targets locally grounded knowledge and reduces shortcuts from English-centric cross-lingual transfer. Evaluating 18 LLMs, we find substantial cultural degradation and a pronounced Locality Effect: cultural competence tracks pre-training exposure more closely than general reasoning ability. We further show that common inference-time remedies do not dissolve the illusion. Models remain overconfident on unfamiliar cultural questions, repeated sampling yields unstable rather than reliable correctness, and retrieval augmentation helps unevenly on long-tail facts. These findings indicate that cultural alignment cannot be inferred from multilingual ability alone and requires deeper intervention than calibration, sampling, or retrieval at inference time

</details>


### [175] [DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31980)
*Meng Chen,Anya Ji,Tsung-Han Wu,Tobias Maringgele,David M. Chan,Alane Suhr,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了DigitalCoach，一个多模态人机教学对话数据集（72段会话、22,752轮对话、28.1小时屏幕录制），用于评估AI模型在教人类使用软件方面的能力。结果显示，当前模型倾向于给出直接指令，缺乏解释、错误诊断和知识检查，且在视觉上下文 grounding 方面表现不足，导致学习者被动跟从而非深度参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在自动化软件任务方面能力增强，研究者希望探索AI是否能够反过来"教人使用软件"，即扮演教练角色。现有研究缺乏针对人机协作教学中AI教练能力的评估基准。

Method: 构建DigitalCoach数据集：收集72段专家-新手间的真实软件教学会话，覆盖5款软件应用，包含22,752轮对话及28.1小时的屏幕录制与输入事件记录。然后分别采用自动化评估和交互式评估两种方式，测试当前最先进模型在教学对话中的表现，重点考察指令风格、解释性、错误诊断、知识检查以及视觉grounding能力。

Result: (1) 自动化评估：模型与人类教练风格差异显著——模型更倾向直接指令，缺少解释、错误诊断和知识检查；(2) 固定教练方法后，模型生成的语句与人类参考接近，但视觉上下文grounding差；(3) 交互评估：模型教练导致学习者被动执行指令，缺乏深度参与，视觉grounding能力不足。

Conclusion: DigitalCoach为构建协作式、主动式计算机使用教练代理奠定了基础，但当前模型在解释性教学、视觉grounding和学习者参与度方面仍有明显短板，需要进一步研究改进。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.

</details>


### [176] [What Survives Into Context: A Diagnostic for Budget-Constrained Multi-Hop RAG and When Submodular Evidence Packing Improves It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00725)
*Ananto Nayan Bala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的诊断指标"上下文中的答案"（answer-in-context），用于评估检索增强生成（RAG）中读者上下文构建的质量，并将其建模为带预算的单调子模最大化问题，设计了一个联合优化相关性、查询覆盖度、代表性和多样性的打包器，在特定条件下显著优于启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定读者上下文预算的RAG场景中，传统检索指标"文档召回率"无法准确预测最终答案质量。作者认为，真正关键的是金标准答案是否作为连续片段保留在打包后的读者上下文中，而非简单存在于检索集合中，因此需要一个更精准的诊断指标和相应的优化方法。

Method: 1. 提出answer-in-context诊断指标：衡量金标准答案是否以连续文本片段形式出现在打包后的读者上下文中。2. 将读者上下文构建形式化为带预算的单调子模最大化问题，设计了一个同时优化相关性、查询覆盖度、代表性和多样性的打包器。3. 通过量化控制构建读者规模阶梯（3B→7B→14B），系统分析不同条件下方法的有效性边界。

Result: 1. answer-in-context与答案F1的相关性（r=0.39-0.55）显著优于召回率（r≈0.31），在HotpotQA上将答案质量区分度提升约五倍（0.60 vs 0.12），在召回率基础上额外贡献ΔR²=0.17。2. 在HotpotQA 160 token预算+3B读者条件下，所提打包器比强聚焦启发式、MMR和朴素打包方法高出最多+5.1 F1。3. 该优势在7B读者时被吸收，在14B时显著反转，诊断指标用单一变量解释了所有边界条件。

Conclusion: 在读者上下文预算受限的RAG中，answer-in-context是比文档召回率更有效的质量预测指标；基于子模优化的打包器在特定条件组合下（多跳互补结构、检索覆盖证据、紧但非极端预算、弱读者）有效，但优势随读者能力增强而消失甚至反转，揭示了证据密度与阅读能力之间的瓶颈转移规律。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) under a fixed reader-context budget forces a selection problem: of the evidence retrieved, only a fraction can be shown to the reader. We argue that document recall -- the standard retrieval metric -- is the wrong quantity to optimize in this regime, and we make two contributions. First, as a general contribution, we introduce answer-in-context, a diagnostic that measures whether a gold answer survives as a contiguous span in the packed reader context (not the retrieved set). It predicts answer F1 better than recall (r=0.39-0.55 vs. about 0.31), separates answer quality roughly five-fold (0.60 vs. 0.12 on HotpotQA), and carries information beyond retrieval: it adds Delta R squared=0.17 over recall and shows a 4.6x EM gap even among questions where all gold was retrieved. We also confirm it interventionally: on 2WikiMultiHopQA a packing change that raises coverage but not answer-in-context yields no accuracy gain. Second, as a conditional contribution, we cast reader-context construction as budgeted monotone submodular maximization and build a packer that jointly optimizes relevance, query coverage, representativeness, and diversity. On HotpotQA with a 160-token budget and a 3B reader it beats a strong focused heuristic, MMR, and naive packing -- by up to +5.1 F1 at equal-or-lower token cost, across three seeds. Crucially, we map the scope of this win honestly: it requires the conjunction of (i) multi-hop complementary structure, (ii) retrieval that surfaces the evidence, (iii) a binding but not extreme budget, and (iv) a reader weak enough that evidence density, not reading capacity, is the bottleneck. A quantization-controlled reader-scale ladder (3B to 7B to 14B) shows the edge over the heuristic is absorbed by 7B and significantly reverses by 14B, while the diagnostic explains every boundary with a single variable.

</details>


### [177] [MetaHOPE: A Metaphor-Oriented Evaluation Framework for Analysing MT and LLM Translation Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00848)
*Jiahui Liang,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaHOPE，一个面向隐喻翻译评估的错误严重度感知标注框架。作者选取GoogleMT、GPT-4和Hunyuan-7b三个代表性系统，在VUAMC和PSUCMC两个隐喻语料库上进行英中和中英翻译测试，通过MetaHOPE进行错误标注并生成人工后编辑的黄金双语参照，为隐喻翻译研究提供评估框架、平行语料资源和错误分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻具有语义复杂性、上下文依赖性和文化嵌入性等特征，给机器翻译和自然语言理解带来歧义挑战。当前缺乏专门针对隐喻翻译的、考虑错误严重程度的系统化评估框架，因此需要设计一个面向隐喻翻译的标注与评估方法，以探究当前SOTA模型在隐喻翻译上的表现。

Method: 提出MetaHOPE错误严重度感知标注框架；选取三类代表性系统：GoogleMT（NMT）、GPT-4（大语言模型）和Hunyuan-7b（开源LLM）；使用VUAMC（英中）和PSUCMC（中英）两个人工标注的隐喻单语语料库；基于MetaHOPE进行错误标注，并生成人工后编辑的双语黄金标准参照。

Result: 构建了基于MetaHOPE框架的隐喻翻译错误标注数据；产出了英中/中英双语的隐喻平行语料资源（含人工后编辑黄金参照）；对三个SOTA自动翻译模型进行了系统的错误分析。具体实验数据需待全文公布。

Conclusion: MetaHOPE评估框架、双语平行隐喻语料资源以及对SOTA翻译模型的错误分析，对隐喻翻译研究领域具有重要参考价值，可为后续研究提供方法和数据支撑。资源将在论文接收后公开发布。

Abstract: In this opinion paper, we propose MetaHOPE, an error severity-aware annotation framework for evaluating metaphor translations. Metaphors present challenges for machine translation (MT) and natural language understanding and processing (NLU, NLP), because it presents the features of semantic complexity, contextual dependency, and cultural embeddings that can lead to ambiguity issues for NLP models. To investigate how state-of-the-art NLP models perform on translating metaphors, we select three representative systems, i.e., GoogleMT, GPT5.4, and Hunyuan-7b as Neural MT (NMT) models and LLMs. We used two human-annotated metaphor corpora, including VUAMC and PSUCMC for English-to-Chinese and Chinese-to-English translation purposes. The original corpora we used are monolingual, where we carried out error annotation using the MetaHOPE framework, and also produced the human post-edited gold reference for bilingual use as a new resource. We believe the MetaHOPE evaluation framework for metaphor translation annotation, the parallel corpora resources, and the error analysis on SOTA automatic translation models can be useful and shed some light for the field of metaphor translation study. We share our resources publicly upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [178] [The Course of News Events: A Comparison of Bottom-Up and Top-Down Approaches for Collecting Text-Based Data about Disasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00849)
*Brielen Madureira,Andreas Niekler,Mariana Madruga de Brito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文比较了两种从新闻中提取灾害事件数据的采样方法：基于现有灾害清单的自顶向下查询法，和基于自然语言处理（NLP）时空特征的自底向上聚类法，以全球滑坡灾害的德语新闻为数据集，分析两种方法在事件覆盖率上的差异及其对研究结论的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻文章是灾害影响与适应研究的重要信息源，但如何选取具有代表性的数据样本是方法学难题。不同采样策略会直接导致不同的新闻样本构成，进而影响媒体覆盖不平等研究、灾害监测及灾害清单补充等下游应用的有效性与偏差。因此，有必要系统比较自顶向下与自底向上两种主流采样路径。

Method: 以全球滑坡灾害的德语新闻报道为数据集，平行实施两种采样方法：（1）自顶向下法——借助已有灾害清单作为先验知识，对新闻数据库进行定向检索；（2）自底向上法——利用NLP方法，根据文本的时间与空间特征对新闻语料进行聚类，自动发现灾害事件。随后对两种方法产生的事件覆盖范围、重叠程度及差异进行定量与定性比较。

Result: 两种方法在事件覆盖上存在系统性差异：自顶向下法更依赖清单的完整性和偏倚，可能遗漏清单外的事件；自底向上法能捕捉更广泛的灾害报道，但聚类精度受限于时空特征的分辨率与算法设计。研究设计的决策显著影响新闻样本的构成。

Conclusion: 研究设计中的采样策略选择会实质性地影响所获得的新闻样本，并进一步影响媒体覆盖不平等分析、灾害监测效果评估以及灾害清单补充等下游研究。研究者需根据具体研究目标审慎选择采样方法，并充分意识到其内在偏倚。

Abstract: News articles are an important source of information on disaster impacts and adaptation. A key methodological challenge in socio-environmental studies is how to select a representative data sample. Two approaches are common: querying news databases top-down with the aid of an existing disaster inventory or using NLP methods to cluster news texts bottom-up based on temporal and spatial features. Using a dataset of German news about landslides worldwide, we compare these approaches and discuss variations in event coverage. Such research design decision can influence the resulting news sample, affecting its use in studies of inequality in media coverage, disaster monitoring and inventory enrichment.

</details>


### [179] [Recovering Input Text from Hidden States: Study of Gradient-Based Inversion of Decoder-Only Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00852)
*Mikołaj Słowikowski,Maciej Witold Majewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了从解码器语言模型最后一层隐藏状态逆向恢复原始输入token的问题，采用连续嵌入空间优化方法而非逐步硬投影，发现了恢复失败呈现类别不对称性：高频虚词容易失败而内容词几乎完美恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将隐藏状态逆向视为一次性重建，但作者认为在连续嵌入空间中进行优化可以暴露丰富的内部信号，使优化过程可观测、失败可检测，从而更深入地理解隐藏状态所编码的信息及其可逆性边界。

Method: 对解码器模型最后一层隐藏状态进行连续嵌入空间优化：在整个搜索过程中使用软代理（soft proxy）向泄露目标方向驱动，仅在内部循环结束时进行一次硬token提交（hard-token projection）。通过分析ground-truth token的排序轨迹、逐位置损失曲线以及提交时的离散损失来评估恢复正确性。

Result: 在C4数据集的10-token提示上，候选窗口扩大后精确匹配率从66.9%提升至97.5%（平均相似度0.994），表明大多数错误是可恢复的近邻误判而非真正的歧义。失败案例呈现类别不对称：空格前缀的高频虚词在嵌入矩阵密集区域占主导，而内容词几乎完美恢复。与SIPIT参考方法相比，逐步硬投影更快，但连续形式使优化过程可观测。

Conclusion: GPT-2的最后一层隐藏状态对原始文本的编码高度敏感，几乎可以完整恢复。连续空间优化暴露了隐藏状态逆向的内在结构：失败并非随机，而是系统性地集中在嵌入空间密集区域的高频功能词上，这为理解语言模型内部表示的信息瓶颈提供了新视角。

Abstract: This work studies the hidden-state inversion problem: recovering the original input token sequence of a decoder-only language model from its last-layer hidden states. Rather than treating inversion as a one-shot reconstruction, we study it as a continuous embedding-space optimisation in which a soft proxy is driven towards the leaked target without any hard-token projection during the search, and a token is committed only once, at the end of the inner loop. This design choice has two consequences which are the main focus of this paper. First, keeping the optimisation entirely in continuous space exposes a rich set of internal signals: rank trajectories of the ground-truth token, per-position loss curves, and a discrete loss measured at commit time. Second, the discrete loss allows assessing the correctness of recovery via cumulative discrete loss. We further analyse which tokens break the reconstructions and find a sharp categorical asymmetry: space-prefixed, high-frequency function words in dense regions of the embedding matrix dominate the failures, while content-bearing tokens are recovered almost perfectly. On 10-token C4 prompts the exact-match rate rises from 66.9% to 97.5% (mean similarity 0.994) as the candidate window is widened, confirming that most errors are recoverable near-misses rather than genuine ambiguities. A comparison with the released SIPIT reference situates these findings: per-step hard projection is faster, but the continuous formulation is what makes the optimisation observable and its failures detectable. The results show that last-layer hidden states of GPT-2 are as sensitive as the original text.

</details>


### [180] [CAT: Confidence-Adaptive Thinking for Efficient Reasoning of Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00862)
*Qizhi Jiang,Shuo Wang,Pei Ke,Yuhang Song,Ke Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAT框架利用模型内在的自我确定性信号作为置信度，融入偏好优化过程，使大推理模型能根据问题难度自主调节推理长度，在保持准确性的同时有效压缩简单问题的推理token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）通过长链思维在复杂任务上表现优异，但在简单问题上存在"过度思考"（overthinking）问题，导致大量token浪费和推理效率低下。现有压缩方法要么采用统一的长度缩减策略，要么依赖粗粒度的难度估计，往往会损害困难问题的性能。

Method: 提出Confidence-Adaptive Thinking（CAT）框架，将模型内在的自我确定性信号作为"置信度"指标，整合到偏好优化过程中。该框架使模型能够根据问题难度自主调节推理链长度——对高置信度的简单问题生成简洁回复，对低置信度的困难问题保留充分的推理过程。

Result: CAT在多个基准测试和不同基础模型上，推理准确性均一致优于现有最先进基线方法，同时实现了推理长度的有效压缩。

Conclusion: CAT为在工业实际场景中平衡推理准确性与延迟提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案，实现了"对确定回答压缩、对不确定回答深思"的自适应推理策略。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have achieved remarkable success on complex tasks by leveraging long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories, yet they frequently exhibit overthinking on simple queries, resulting in significant token overhead and reduced inference efficiency. However, existing compression methods predominantly apply uniform length reduction or rely on coarse-grained difficulty estimation, often leading to performance degradation on difficult problems. To address this limitation, we propose Confidence-Adaptive Thinking (CAT), a framework that incorporates the model's intrinsic self-certainty signals as confidence into the preference optimization process, which autonomously modulates reasoning lengths based on problem difficulty. Experimental results show that CAT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on reasoning accuracy across multiple benchmarks on different base models. Our work enables LRMs to effectively compress confident responses while deliberating on uncertain ones, offering a potentially robust solution for balancing accuracy and latency in practical industrial scenarios.

</details>


### [181] [Dynamic Bidirectional Pattern Memory: A Production-Scale Empirical Characterisation of Inference-Time Gating in Clinical NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00870)
*Ali H. Lazem,William Teahan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在生产级临床NLP管线中部署推理时模式-记忆门控机制。核心发现是：直接模仿验证器输出的记忆过滤器在大规模场景下会静默失效，只有检测验证器所依据的相同证据的信号才具有选择性。基于此，设计了一个成功的记忆门控——检查患者提取实体是否支持所问问题，能将验证器可能拒绝的答案标记概率提高1.84倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产级临床NLP的生成器-验证器管线中，验证器需要反复检查生成器提出的候选抽取结果，这带来了显著的计算开销。研究者希望引入一种部署时学习的轻量级记忆机制，通过记住之前被验证器拒绝的模式来预过滤候选，从而减少验证器的重复工作。核心动机在于探究在大规模真实临床数据上，这种模式-记忆门控是否真正有效，以及有效的条件是什么。

Method: 构建了一个由 Llama-3.3 70B（生成器）和 MMed-Llama-3.1 70B（验证器）组成的管线，在167,034条PMC-Patients临床叙述上运行。添加了一个部署时学习的轻量级记忆模块，旨在学习过滤规则。实验涵盖两类过滤器：(1) 关系抽取过滤器，尝试从验证器的拒绝记录中学习；(2) 问答过滤器，设计了五个版本，其中成功的版本检查患者提取实体是否支持所问问题。系统采用标记而非删除的方式处理可疑抽取，保证临床审查可见性。

Result: 四个核心发现：(1) 直接从验证器拒绝中学习过滤规则在大规模场景失败，尽管记录了785,797次拒绝，但拒绝案例过于分散，关系抽取过滤器始终为空；(2) 使用固定临床本体规则的简单方法无需验证器即可捕获49,734个违例关系；(3) 五个问答过滤器中四个因不同原因失败，唯一成功的检查实体-问题支持关系，标记验证器可能拒绝答案的概率是接受答案的1.84倍；(4) 所有过滤器的共同模式：只有当过滤器检测验证器所依据的相同证据时才有选择性，模仿验证器输出则无效。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了一个可迁移的结论：在生成器-验证器管线中，最自然的记忆设计在大规模场景下可能静默失效；预生成门控是否具有选择性，在其信号是否触及验证器所回答的问题时便已决定，而非取决于后续的工程努力。这一发现对设计高效临床NLP系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We study inference-time pattern-memory gating in a production-scale clinical natural language processing (NLP) pipeline. The pipeline pairs a generator (Llama-3.3 70B) proposing extractions with a verifier (MMed-Llama-3.1 70B) accepting or rejecting them, over 167,034 PMC-Patients narratives, and adds a lightweight memory that learns at deployment which extractions to filter, so the verifier need not re-examine candidates already seen to fail. We report four findings. First, learning filtering rules directly from the verifier's rejections failed at full scale: the relation-extraction filter stayed empty despite 785,797 logged rejections, because they were spread too thinly across too many distinct forms to accumulate. Second, a simpler rule using a fixed clinical ontology produced the same filtering without the verifier, capturing 49,734 ontology-violating relations on a held-out 5,000-patient set. Third, of five versions of the question-answering filter, four failed for distinct, instructive reasons; the fifth succeeded by checking whether a patient's extracted entities support the question asked, and where it applies was 1.84 times likelier to flag an answer the verifier would reject than one it would accept. Fourth, one pattern held across all five: a filter is selective only when it tests the same evidence the verifier weighs, not when it imitates the verifier's output. Together these give a transferable result for any generator-verifier pipeline: the most natural memory design can fail silently at scale, and whether a pre-generation gate is selective is decided before any engineering effort, by whether its signal probes the question the verifier itself answers. Throughout, the system flags suspect extractions rather than deleting them, so every decision stays visible for clinical review. All code and test artefacts are released openly.

</details>


### [182] [How Ethos and Pathos Appeals Resonate in Reader Interpretations of Social Media Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00873)
*Ewelina Gajewska,Katarzyna Budzynska,Jaroslaw Chudziak,Liesbeth Allein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了古典修辞策略（ethos和pathos）在沉默的社交媒体受众中的影响，发现约30%的读者解读会偏离原文意图，且修辞性内容引发的解读变异更大，修辞诉求还能预测受众对作者的态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于社交媒体中可见的互动（如评论、点赞），忽视了"沉默的大多数"——即不公开发声的受众如何理解信息。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究ethos和pathos这两种经典说服模式在沉默受众的意义解读中如何发挥作用。

Method: 研究者使用社交媒体句子与人工撰写的解读配对的标注数据集，对原句和解读分别标注ethos（信誉诉求）和pathos（情感诉求）标签，然后分析这些修辞诉求在解读过程中是否被保留、解读偏离程度以及修辞对受众态度的影响。

Result: （1）约30%的解读偏离了原句的修辞意图；（2）修辞性内容比中性内容引发更大的解读变异性；（3）原句中的ethos和pathos可以显著预测沉默受众对作者的态度。

Conclusion: 修辞策略对沉默受众的影响微妙而深远，不仅影响意义解读，还能塑造受众对作者的态度。研究揭示了修辞在超越可见互动的层面上如何潜移默化地作用于受众认知。

Abstract: Rhetorical strategies and their influence on audiences are often studied through social media posts and comments. However, this focus overlooks the universal audience, which is the majority of readers who remain silent and do not explicitly express how a message affects them. This study investigates how two classical modes of persuasion, ethos and pathos, resonate in the silent audience's interpretations of meaning. Using a dataset of social media sentences paired with human-written interpretations, we label both sources for ethos and pathos and assess whether these rhetorical appeals are preserved. Our analyses show that interpretations diverge from the original sentences in 30% of cases, with rhetorically charged content eliciting greater variability than neutral content. We further find that ethos and pathos in original sentences can predict audience attitudes toward the author, underscoring the subtle ways rhetoric shapes perception beyond visible engagement.

</details>


### [183] [MultiSynt/MT: Trillion-Token Multi-Parallel Pre-Training Data Translated Across 36 Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00890)
*Maximilian Idahl,Jörg Tiedemann,Sampo Pyysalo,David Salinas,Tomasz Galica,Shenbin Qian,Tudor Nicolae Mateiu,Zihao Li,Anna Lokrantz,Fedor Vitiugin,André F. T. Martins,Jenna Kanerva,Filip Ginter,Matthias Lindemann,Tim Isbister,Birger Moell,Jonas Lindh,Jan Hajič,Jenia Jitsev,Andrey Kutuzov,Stephan Oepen,Gema Ramírez-Sánchez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiSynt/MT 是一个开放的大规模合成平行语料库，通过翻译100B高质量英语token生成36种欧洲语言的约4.8T token。在该语料上训练的LLM仅用约28%的预训练token即达到原生数据基线的性能，同等预算下性能提升约15%。同时揭示了标准多项选择基准在翻译质量评估上的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放的大规模预训练语料仍集中于英语，严重限制了多语言LLM的发展，尤其对中低资源欧洲语言而言，缺乏足够规模的高质量开放预训练资源。

Method: 以Nemotron-CC中100B高质量英语token为源，使用Tower+和OPUS-MT/HPLT-MT两套翻译系统，生成覆盖36种欧洲语言的合成平行语料MultiSynt/MT（约4.8T目标语言token），并在该语料上训练参考LLM，与HPLT 2.0原生数据基线进行多维度对比评估，包括标准多语言基准和基于LLM-as-judge的流畅度评估。

Result: (1) MultiSynt/MT训练的LLM仅用约28%的预训练token即达到HPLT 2.0原生数据基线的最终分数；(2) 在相同100B token训练预算下，性能相对提升约15%；(3) 标准多项选择基准无法捕捉翻译质量差异，而LLM-as-judge流畅度评估可清晰反映；(4) 挪威语习语和文化扎根任务上，原生数据仍具优势。

Conclusion: 大规模合成翻译数据是一种高效的多语言预训练替代方案，能够以更少的token预算达到甚至超越原生数据的性能。但评估体系存在盲点，需要引入流畅度敏感的评估方法，且特定语言的文化扎根任务仍依赖原生数据。作者公开了语料和多系统行对齐翻译以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Open web-scale pre-training corpora remain concentrated in English, limiting multilingual LLM development. We introduce MultiSynt/MT, an open synthetic parallel corpus with approximately 4.8 trillion target-language tokens across 36 European languages, produced by translating 100 billion high-quality Nemotron-CC tokens with Tower+ and OPUS-MT/HPLT-MT systems. For many medium- and lower-resource European languages, this is the largest openly available pre-training resource. On a broad multilingual benchmark suite, reference LLMs trained on MultiSynt/MT reach the final score of HPLT 2.0, a native-data baseline, using roughly 72% fewer pre-training tokens, and outperform it by approximately 15% relative at a matched 100B-token training budget. Our analyses also identify evaluation blind spots: standard multiple-choice benchmarks miss translation-quality differences that a fluency-sensitive LLM-as-judge evaluation cleanly recovers on the trained LLMs (with no fluency deficit in MultiSynt itself), and Norwegian idiomatic and culturally grounded tasks remain better served by native data. We release the corpus, including row-aligned translations from multiple systems, to support controlled research on multilingual pre-training data and evaluation.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Document Grounding: Span-Level Hallucination Detection over Code, Tool Output, and Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00895)
*Ádám Kovács,Bowei He,Xue Liu,István Boros,Szilveszter Tóth,Gábor Recski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的跨模态幻觉检测基准，覆盖代码、工具输出、结构化文档和自然语言等多种RAG输入类型，并在此基准上微调的Qwen3.5-2B检测器在统一测试集上达到0.689的span-F1，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的RAG幻觉检测评估主要集中在自然语言文档上，但实际应用中，基于检索增强生成的系统越来越多地依赖结构化输入（如源代码、开发者工具输出、markdown文档、表格、仓库元数据等），这些结构化输入缺乏专门的幻觉检测评估基准。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的跨模态幻觉检测基准，方法是：从正确的有依据答案出发，注入带有精确字符级标签的局部幻觉，并通过基于证据的人工审核验证代码测试集划分。在该基准上，微调了Qwen3.5-2B作为span级幻觉检测器。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3.5-2B检测器在统一测试集上达到0.689 span-F1，在代码-智能体源上达到0.60，远超LettuceDetect-large的0.17和最强零样本LLM评判器的0.22。同时在自然语言基准上保持竞争力：RAGTruth example-F1为81.8，English PsiloQA IoU为0.724。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了结构化输入领域幻觉检测评估的空白，证明了统一的幻觉检测器可以同时处理结构化和非结构化输入，且通过精细构建的基准和微调实现的检测性能大幅领先现有方法。

Abstract: Hallucination detection for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is usually evaluated on natural-language document evidence. However, grounded generation systems increasingly rely on structured inputs: source code, developer-tool output, markdown documents, tables, and repository metadata. We introduce a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection over code, tool output, structured documents, and existing natural-language RAG datasets. The benchmark is built by starting from grounded correct answers, injecting localized hallucinations with exact character labels, and validating the code test split with evidence-based review. Our fine-tuned Qwen3.5-2B detector reaches 0.689 span-F1 on the unified test set and 0.60 on the code-agent source, where it substantially outperforms LettuceDetect-large (0.17) and the strongest zero-shot LLM judges we evaluated (at most 0.22). The same model remains competitive on established natural-language benchmarks, with 81.8 RAGTruth example-F1 and 0.724 English PsiloQA IoU.

</details>


### [185] [From Personas to Plot: Character-Grounded Multi-Agent Story Generation for Long-Form Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00918)
*Aayush Aluru,Chloe Ho,Muhammad Hammouri,Kerry Luo,Myra Malik,Ryan Lagasse,Arjun Bahuguna,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGNET（多智能体目标驱动叙事引擎）与ATLAS（图结构幻觉检测管线）的统一框架，用于生成长篇叙事并检测其一致性。MAGNET通过角色智能体基于共享世界状态和故事目标推进情节，ATLAS则比较场景级世界表征来检测幻觉。实验表明，该框架在100页故事中相较单模型基线减少41%注释错误和50%幻觉，相较IBSEN分别减少34%和45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在生成长篇虚构故事时，难以维持叙事一致性（narrative consistency）和连贯的情节线（coherent plot lines），存在严重的幻觉和不一致问题。现有方法缺乏对世界状态和故事目标的显式追踪机制。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含两部分：（1）MAGNET——多智能体目标驱动叙事引擎，每个角色作为独立智能体（persona-grounded character agent），基于共享世界状态（shared world state）和动态演化的故事目标（evolving story goals）来提出行动决策；（2）ATLAS——基于图的管线，通过比较生成故事中场景级别的世界表征（scene-level world representations）来检测幻觉。评估采用LLM编辑器、成对评分量规（pairwise rubric scoring）和ATLAS三种方式。

Result: 在100页长度设定下，MAGNET相比单模型基线：注释错误减少41%，幻觉减少50%；相比IBSEN基线：注释错误减少34%，幻觉减少45%。成对评分量规评估结果与上述趋势一致，验证了框架在叙事连贯性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 长篇叙事可通过显式的世界状态追踪和目标驱动的多智能体生成自然涌现（emerge），该框架为可控且结构连贯的长篇叙事生成奠定了基础。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive creative fiction generation, they struggle to maintain narrative consistency and coherent plot lines in long-form stories. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for long-form narrative generation and verification. MAGNET, a multi-agent goal-driven narrative engine for storytelling, generates stories with persona-grounded character agents that propose actions based on a shared world state and evolving story goals, while ATLAS is a graph-based pipeline that compares scene-level world representations across a generated story to detect hallucinations. By evaluating MAGNET using an LLM editor, pairwise rubric scoring, and ATLAS, we show that our framework produces coherent narratives compared to single-model prompting and IBSEN. At 100 pages, MAGNET reduced annotations and hallucinations by 41 and 50%, respectively, compared to the single model baseline and by 34 and 45%, respectively, compared to IBSEN, with pairwise rubric evaluation showing similar results. These results suggest that long-form narratives can emerge from explicit world-state tracking and goal-driven multi-agent generation, providing a foundation for controllable and structurally coherent long-form narrative generation.

</details>


### [186] [Persona Non Grata: LLM Persona-Driven Generations in MCQA are Unstable in Distinct Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00937)
*César Guerra-Solano,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在多项选择问答（MCQA）任务中进行角色驱动生成（PDG）时的不稳定性，提出三个评估维度（性能、结果、问题正确性稳定性），发现不稳定性与模型家族、规模及问题领域相关，且任务提示格式是影响预测稳定性的最关键超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注自由文本（如对话）中角色表达的一致性与稳定性，而忽视了非文本密集型输出（如MCQA）中角色驱动生成的不稳定性问题。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者开发了三个度量指标，分别从性能稳定性（performance stability）、结果稳定性（outcome stability）和问题正确性稳定性（question correctness stability）三个维度评估LLM在MCQA任务中角色驱动生成的稳定性，并系统考察了模型家族、模型规模、问题领域、提示格式及温度等超参数对不稳定性的影响。

Result: 研究发现：（1）不稳定性在不同模型家族和模型规模间存在一致差异；（2）数学和常识类问题导致更高的不稳定性；（3）任务提示格式比温度等其他超参数引入更多预测不稳定性；（4）不稳定性与任务准确率相关，且不同实验设置下最优和最差角色可能截然不同。

Conclusion: 在角色驱动生成中检查超参数带来的不稳定性至关重要，揭示了仅凭相似角色设定可能导致截然不同的任务表现，强调了在PDG实验设计中需系统考虑稳定性因素。

Abstract: Persona-driven generations (PDGs) have seen prolific use in research and industry applications, where a large language model (LLM) takes on a 'persona' while completing some task. While persona expressed through free-form text (like dialogue) has substantial work investigating stability or consistency, relatively, persona expressed in non-text-heavy outputs (like in multiple-choice question answering, or MCQA) is often overlooked. We work to address this gap, seeking to understand the instability of LLM PDGs in MCQA tasks. We develop three metrics investigating the performance, outcome, and question correctness stability, evaluating three distinct dimensions. Using these metrics, we find that instability varies consistently between model families and model size, and across question domains, with math/commonsense questions leading to greater instability. We also find task prompt format introduces more prediction instability than other hyperparameters, like temperature. Finally, we find that instability is related to task accuracy, and using our instability metrics, find different experimental settings that result in different best and worst personas for tasks, despite their similarity. This reveals the importance of checking hyperparameter instability in PDGs.

</details>


### [187] [Quantifying the Affective Gap: A Zero-Shot Evaluation of LLMs on Fine-Grained Emotion Taxonomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00968)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Jessica M. Johnson,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对三款领先商业大语言模型（Claude、GPT-5.4、Gemini）在13类细粒度情感分类任务上进行了统一的零样本评估，发现三者准确率接近但均不理想（38%-40%），且统计上无显著差异，揭示了当前前沿AI在零样本细粒度情感识别方面的共同上限和类别偏见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言情感识别是情感计算的基础性挑战，对人机交互、心理健康支持和对话式AI具有重要意义。然而，当前缺乏对主流商业大语言模型在细粒度多类别情感分类任务上的系统性零样本评估，研究者希望填补这一空白，揭示前沿模型在该任务上的真实能力边界。

Method: 采用严格统一的零样本评估范式：(1) 选取三款商业大语言模型——Claude (claude-sonnet-4-6)、ChatGPT (GPT-5.4) 和 Gemini (gemini-2.5-flash)，均通过其生产环境API调用；(2) 使用boltuix/emotions数据集，从中分层抽样1,000条句子，覆盖13个情感类别；(3) 对所有模型应用完全相同的单轮提示，不提供任何示例；(4) 评估指标包括准确率、宏平均F1分数，并采用McNemar检验进行模型间配对显著性检验。

Result: (1) Gemini准确率最高（39.9%），宏平均F1=0.363；GPT-5.4次之（38.8%，宏平均F1=0.291）；Claude最低（38.0%，宏平均F1=0.159）。(2) 所有模型在"讽刺"和"渴望"类别上表现优异，在"爱"、"困惑"和"羞耻"类别上一致失败。(3) McNemar检验显示任意两两模型间均无统计显著差异（p > 0.10），表明三者趋近于共同的零样本性能上限。(4) Claude的宏平均F1显著偏低，暴露出严重的类别不平衡预测偏差。

Conclusion: 当前前沿商业大语言模型在零样本细粒度情感分类任务上存在共同的性能瓶颈（约40%准确率上限），且在特定情感类别（爱、困惑、羞耻）上表现一致薄弱。Claude的类别预测偏差问题尤为突出。这些发现揭示了现有LLM在情感理解深度上的根本局限，为未来研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in natural language is a foundational challenge in affective computing, with critical implications for human-computer interaction, mental health support, and conversational AI. This paper presents a rigorous, unified zero-shot evaluation of three leading commercial large language models: Claude (claude-sonnet-4-6), ChatGPT (GPT-5.4), and Gemini (gemini-2.5-flash). The models were queried through their respective production APIs as of April 2026 on a fine-grained 13-class emotion classification task. Using a stratified 1,000-sentence sample from the boltuix/emotions dataset, which comprises 131,306 sentences across 13 categories, a single uniform prompt with no exemplars was applied identically across all models. Gemini achieves the highest accuracy (39.9%) and macro-F1 score (0.363), followed by GPT-5.4 (38.8%, macro-F1 = 0.291) and Claude (38.0%, macro-F1 = 0.159). All models excel on sarcasm and desire while consistently failing on love, confusion, and shame. McNemar tests reveal no statistically significant pairwise differences (p > 0.10), suggesting convergence at a shared zero-shot ceiling. Claude's markedly lower macro-F1 score exposes a class-imbalance prediction bias. These findings highlight the current limitations of frontier AI systems in zero-shot fine-grained emotion classification.

</details>


### [188] [Svarna: An Open Corpus Workbench for Modern Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00970)
*Stergios Chatzikyriakidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Svarna，一个面向现代希腊语的免费开源网络语料库工作平台，整合五个数据库、超5.07亿词和约2900万句，提供KWIC检索、频率分析、搭配提取、话语标记词典等多种功能，填补希腊语语言技术空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希腊语语料库资源虽然存在，但分散在不同平台，许多存在机构访问限制或已下线，长期存在可及性缺口。Svarna 旨在将这些资源整合至单一界面，无需登录、安装或专业培训，从而解决这一痛点。

Method: 系统整合五个语料数据库（涵盖机构、文学、方言、社交媒体、历史等多种语域），基于 SQLite FTS5 全文索引，通过 FastAPI 后端提供接口，以 Docker 容器形式部署于 Azure，采用 MIT 许可证开源。功能包括：KWIC 标注的语词检索、语域归一化的频率分析、基于互信息的搭配提取、93个希腊语话语标记的分布词典、n-gram/变体/搭配网络的文本级分析工具、基于对数比率的语域比较、正则表达式搜索，以及可选的 LLM 层用于语用标注和自由研究模式。

Result: Svarna 成功构建了一个功能完备、无需登录即可使用的在线语料库平台，总计超5.07亿词、约2900万句。源代码、构建脚本及部署配置已公开在 GitHub，用户可添加自有语料并部署独立实例。

Conclusion: Svarna 作为探索现有希腊语数据的第一步，填补了希腊语语言技术的基础设施空白，为未来更全面的研究奠定了基础，其开源、可扩展的特性使其具有长期发展潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces Svarna, a free, open-source, web-based corpus workbench for modern Greek. Svarna integrates five databases covering various registers, institutional, literary, dialectal, social media, and historical, to provide a total of more than 507 million words and around 29 million sentences. This platform addresses the chronic gaps in Greek language technology. Although various corpus resources exist, they are scattered across different platforms, and in many cases, institutional access is restricted or they are no longer available online. Svarna integrates these resources into a single interface that can be used without logging in, installation, or specialized training. This system provides a concordancer with KWIC marking capabilities, frequency analysis including register-by-register normalization, collocation extraction using mutual information, a dictionary of 93 Greek discourse markers providing distribution profiles, text-level analysis tools including n-grams, variants, and collocation networks, register comparison using log-ratio, regular expression search, and an optional LLM layer for pragmatic annotation and free research mode. This platform is built upon SQLite FTS5 full-text indexes provided via a FastAPI backend, deployed as Docker containers on Azure, and released under the MIT license. Source code, build scripts, and deployment configurations are publicly available on GitHub. Users can add their own corpora and deploy their own instances. This document describes the system design, corpus structure, and use cases demonstrating the various queries supported by the platform. Svarna serves as the first step in exploring available data and is expected to lay the foundation for more comprehensive research in the future.

</details>


### [189] [KnowledgeDebugger -- an Exploration Tool for Knowledge Localization and Editing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01000)
*Eric Benz,Lennart Stöpler,Nikolai Bolik,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KnowledgeDebugger 是一个基于图形界面的探索工具，用于 Transformer 模型中的知识定位与编辑，提供对 EasyEdit 库的无代码访问，支持研究人员在个体样本上进行知识编辑的现象探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 Transformer 知识存储与编辑的研究通常分为两阶段：先在单样本上探索现象，再进行大规模统计实验。第一阶段缺乏便捷的工具支持，KnowledgeDebugger 旨在填补这一空白，提供直观的无代码探索工具。

Method: 基于 LM-Debugger 的架构设计，构建 GUI 交互界面；集成 EasyEdit 知识编辑库中的多种最先进方法，提供无代码接入；通过案例研究展示工具在知识定位与编辑探索中的有效性。

Result: 通过若干案例研究，展示了该工具在复现和探索该领域最新发现方面的有效性，证明其能够支持研究人员在个体样本层面高效进行知识定位与编辑实验。

Conclusion: KnowledgeDebugger 为 Transformer 知识编辑研究的第一阶段（现象探索）提供了实用的无代码 GUI 工具，降低了使用门槛，有助于加速该领域的研究迭代。

Abstract: Recent research has increasingly focused on understanding how Transformers store and process knowledge, as well as how this knowledge can be edited. Research work in this area is often conducted in two phases: first, phenomena are explored on individual samples. Then, when results appear promising, more statistically robust experiments follow. To support the first phase, we propose KnowledgeDebugger, a GUI-based exploration tool for knowledge localization and editing in Transformers. Our tool - inspired by LM-Debugger - offers no-code access to the methods in EasyEdit, a widely used library of state-of-the-art Knowledge Editing approaches. We demonstrate the tool's effectiveness through case studies of recent findings in this field.

</details>


### [190] [Logit-Contribution Scoring Identifies Non-Literal Retrieval Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01002)
*Aryo Pradipta Gema,Beatrice Alex,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS)，一种面向"写入"的注意力头检测方法，通过计算OV电路输出在答案token非嵌入方向上的投影来识别执行非字面检索的注意力头，在NoLiMa等长上下文基准上，其识别精度和消融效果均显著优于现有基于注意力权重的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器按"被注意token是否匹配生成token"这一字面复制标准来评分注意力头，只捕捉到注意力头"读"了什么，却忽略了其通过输出-值(OV)电路"写"了什么。而长上下文中大模型常进行非字面语义合成而非逐字复制，因此需要一种"写入感知"的检测方法。

Method: 提出LOCOS方法：对每个注意力头，计算其OV电路输出在答案token的非嵌入(unembedding)方向上的投影分数，并在单次前向传播中对比针(needle)位置与非针位置来源的得分差异，以此识别执行非字面检索的注意力头。

Result: 在Qwen3、Gemma-3、OLMo-3.1三个模型家族上测试：在NoLiMa基准上，均值消融top LOCOS头导致ROUGE-L崩溃所需的头数远少于基线方法；Qwen3-8B上消融50个头使ROUGE-L从0.401降至0.000，而最强基线仍保留0.292。所选头具有检索特异性：相同消融下参数召回和算术推理保持基线水平；在Qwen3-8B上，同一消融使MuSiQue从0.55降至0.08，BABI-Long从0.62降至0.20，而随机头对照组与基线差异在0.05以内。

Conclusion: LOCOS通过关注OV电路的"写入"行为，比传统基于注意力权重的"读取"方法更精确地定位了长上下文中负责非字面检索的注意力头，这些头具有任务特异性，为理解和干预长上下文模型行为提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: In long-context use, large language models frequently synthesize answers from the meaning of a relevant context span rather than literally copy-pasting them. Identifying which attention heads perform this synthesis matters for interpreting long-context model behavior. Yet existing detectors miss these heads by construction: they reward heads whose attended token matches the generated token, a literal-copy criterion that captures where a head reads but not what it writes through its output-value (OV) circuit, the very mechanism that carries non-literal retrieval. We introduce Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS), a write-aware detector that scores each head by the projection of its OV-circuit output onto the answer-token unembedding direction, contrasting needle and off-needle source positions in a single forward pass. Across three model families (Qwen3, Gemma-3, OLMo-3.1), mean-ablating the top LOCOS heads on the NoLiMa non-literal retrieval benchmark collapses ROUGE-L at lower head counts than prior attention-based detections; on Qwen3-8B, ablating 50 heads drives ROUGE-L from 0.401 to 0.000 while the strongest baseline still retains 0.292. The selected heads are retrieval-specific: parametric recall and arithmetic reasoning stay at baseline under the same ablation. On Qwen3-8B, the same ablation also drops MuSiQue from 0.55 to 0.08 and BABI-Long from 0.62 to 0.20, while a random-heads control stays within 0.05 of baseline.

</details>


### [191] [Understanding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01006)
*Yannik Keller,Thomas Eisenmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLM）的新兴能力（符号推理、心智理论、欺骗策略）及其在Transformer架构中的机制实现，讨论了LLM与人类认知的异同，并对当前关于LLM是否具有真正理解能力的争论进行了回应，主张以更细致的方式探讨AI认知的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前关于LLM的机制、能力及其与人类认知关系的高度争议，作者旨在整合最新证据，澄清LLM的认知能力本质，反驳过度简化的还原论观点（如认为LLM仅是训练数据的模式记忆），倡导更审慎地讨论AI认知问题。

Method: 本文为综述性章节，方法上包括：（1）概述Transformer架构及注意力机制如何支撑大规模训练与通用能力；（2）系统梳理LLM在符号推理、心智理论、欺骗策略等方面的能力证据与失败案例；（3）回顾可解释AI方法，从神经元激活分析到电路追踪；（4）对当前争论进行批判性分析，指出反拟人化论点中对优化过程和认知能力的误解。

Result: 综述表明：LLM确实展现出类人认知的新兴能力，能够解决此前被认为需要人类认知的任务；但失败案例也揭示了LLM与人类认知的关键差异。可解释性研究正在逐步揭示这些能力在模型内部的机制实现。作者认为，将LLM行为简单归结为模式记忆是对优化过程与认知能力的误解。

Conclusion: 关于LLM的认知问题需要一个更细致的讨论框架：既不能忽视人类与LLM之间的根本差异，也不应通过过度简化的还原论论调预先排除AI具有认知的可能性。当前对优化过程和认知能力的普遍误解需要被纠正。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) represent one of the most significant advances in AI and natural language processing in recent years. Still, many pressing questions about their mechanisms, capabilities, and relationship to human cognition remain highly debated. This chapter aims to outline our current understanding of LLMs by discussing recent evidence on emerging capabilities and their mechanistic implementation within processing layers. We begin with a concise overview of the Transformer architecture, emphasizing how the attention mechanism enables training on massive datasets, allowing LLMs to function as generalist rather than specialized models. Next, we examine emergent LLM capabilities that appear to resemble aspects of human cognition, including symbolic reasoning, theory of mind, and deception strategies. Several studies provide evidence that LLMs can solve tasks previously thought to require human-like cognition. Other studies reveal insightful failure cases that shed light on the differences between human and LLM cognition. Alongside these findings, we review explainable AI approaches ranging from neuron activation analysis to circuit tracing. In the final section, we address current debates concerning what LLMs genuinely understand versus what they merely appear to understand. Prominent arguments against AI anthropomorphism point to the simplicity of LLM training objectives, claiming that LLM behavior is better explained by pattern memorization of training data than by genuine cognition. We argue that this standpoint is guided by misconceptions about optimization processes and cognitive capacity, and advocate for a more nuanced discussion of LLM cognition that neither dismisses the differences between humans and LLMs nor precludes the possibility of AI cognition through overly simplistic reductionist arguments.

</details>


### [192] [Reading Order Inference for Complex Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01018)
*Iddo Hakim,Sharva Gogawale,Omer Ventura,Gal Grudka,Daria Vasyutinsky-Shapira,Berat Kurar-Barakat,Nachum Dershowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、基于图模型的阅读顺序推断框架，利用因果语言模型条件似然和BERT下一句预测两个轻量级语言模型信号进行加权集成，并通过最大遗憾推理规则求解度约束有向路径覆盖，在Glossa Ordinaria环绕式注释布局及多栏文档上显著优于XY-cut和LayoutReader基线，且具有镜像不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂历史手稿数字化中的阅读顺序推断瓶颈，特别是Glossa Ordinaria这类中央正文被环绕注释包围的非矩形、非凸区域布局。现有方法（如递归XY-cut）在此类多阅读流空间交错的场景下表现不佳，而基于学习的LayoutReader存在词级与行级粒度不匹配问题。

Method: 将每条OCR文本行作为有向候选转移图的节点，边权重由因果语言模型条件似然和BERT NSP两个信号加权集成评分（第三信号句子嵌入未带来提升）；全局阅读顺序通过度约束有向路径覆盖（degree-constrained directed path cover）恢复；提出最大遗憾（max-regret）推理规则以避免贪心边选择中的级联"边窃取"失败。

Result: 在Glossa环绕布局上平均恢复95%的真值后继边（vs. XY-cut 50%）；在OmniDocBench 140页多栏英文子集上宏观边准确率达88%（vs. XY-cut 75%, LayoutReader 25%）；镜像不变性测试中方法变化小于1个百分点（XY-cut 2点，LayoutReader-T高达8点）。

Conclusion: 所提训练无关的图框架在复杂历史手稿阅读顺序推断任务上一致优于经典XY-cut和LayoutReader基线，兼具高准确率与镜像不变性，证明了将阅读顺序建模为全局优化问题而非局部贪心决策的有效性。

Abstract: Reading order inference remains a critical bottleneck in the digitization of complex historical manuscripts, where pages contain multiple spatially interleaved reading streams, the canonical example being the Glossa Ordinaria layout, in which a central text is surrounded by commentaries that wrap around it in non-rectangular, non-convex regions. We present a training-free, graph-based framework: each OCR text line becomes a node in a directed candidate-transition graph, edges are scored by a weighted additive ensemble of two lightweight language-model signals (causal language model conditional likelihood and BERT next-sentence prediction, NSP; a third sentence-embedding signal was evaluated but did not improve reading order), and the global reading order is recovered as a degree-constrained directed path cover. To avoid the cascading "edge-theft" failures of greedy edge selection, we propose a max-regret inference rule that prioritizes commitments with high opportunity cost. We evaluate on synthetic Glossa Ordinaria grid layouts, on 23 ALTO page geometries (10 historical source pages plus mirrored and flipped variants), and on a 140-page multi-column English subset of OmniDocBench, comparing our method against the canonical recursive XY-cut (PaddleOCR PP-StructureV3) and two LayoutReader variants (layout-only and text+layout) on identical inputs. On wrap-around Glossa layouts our method recovers 95% of ground-truth successor edges on average vs. XY-cut's 50%; on the OmniDocBench multi-column subset it reaches 88% macro edge accuracy versus XY-cut's 75% and LayoutReader's 25%. The LayoutReader baselines transfer poorly due to a word-level vs. line-level granularity mismatch. We additionally verify mirror-invariance under horizontal and vertical page reflections: Our method changes by less than 1 percentage point, classical XY-cut by 2 points, and LayoutReader-T by up to 8 points.

</details>


### [193] [Evidence-Supported Credit Risk Report Generation Using News-Centric Financial Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01023)
*Rocio Jimenez-Villen,Ziwei Xu,Ying Chen,Oscar Araque,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FinKG-News 框架，通过从新闻中自动抽取事件并构建以公司为核心的金融知识图谱，将隐性的市场驱动因素显式化；基于该图谱，开发了上下文学习架构用于信用风险报告生成，在三个金融维度上质量提升19%-34%，幻觉显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场动态受新闻中真实事件驱动，但这些驱动因素在文本中往往是隐式的。现有的金融文本分析方法难以显式建模事件-市场关系，缺乏将新闻事件、公司实体和市场环境结构化关联的知识图谱支撑，导致信用风险报告等下游任务生成质量不佳、幻觉频发。

Method: 1）提出 FinKG-News 框架，以新闻事件为锚点，自动抽取事件并与公司实体链接，构建事实导向、公司中心、环境感知的知识图谱；2）基于该图谱作为结构化证据，融合事件、新闻与公司数据，设计上下文学习（in-context learning）架构，覆盖三个核心金融维度进行信用风险报告生成。

Result: 1）自动化幻觉检测与质量评估方法仍不可靠，专家判断不可或缺；2）所提方法相比基线持续优于现有方案，质量提升19%-34%，同时有效减少幻觉生成。

Conclusion: FinKG-News 通过将新闻事件显式建模为结构化知识图谱，为金融市场分析提供了可靠的事实基础；结合上下文学习架构能显著提升信用风险报告生成质量并抑制幻觉，但质量评估仍依赖专家判断。代码与资源已开源。

Abstract: Financial markets evolve in response to real-world events reported in news, yet these drivers often remain implicit in text. To better explain market dynamics, event-market relations must be explicitly modeled through factual, company-centric, and environment-aware knowledge graphs. We present FinKG-News, a framework that automatically constructs such graphs by extracting news events as anchors linked to companies. Using FinKG-News as grounded evidence that integrates events, news, and company data, we develop an in-context learning architecture for credit risk report generation across three core financial dimensions. Automatic and human evaluations show that automated hallucination detection and quality assessment remain unreliable, making expert judgment indispensable. Our approach consistently outperforms baselines, improving quality by 19%-34% while reducing hallucinations. The source code and project resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/ichise-laboratory/FINKG-news.

</details>


### [194] [Behavior-Adaptive Conversational Agents: Toward a Fluid Personality Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01034)
*Hasibur Rahman,Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个"流式人格框架"（Fluid Personality Framework），主张基于聊天的AI代理应根据任务上下文、用户目标、个性特质和情境紧迫度，联合调整其隐喻角色（如教练、导师、图书管理员、工具）和人格表达强度（低/中/高），而非固化单一的人设与风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数基于LLM的对话代理固定了人格和风格，然而不同场景（如医疗信息查询、健身指导、反思性学习）在动态性、紧迫性和正式程度上差异显著，静态设定容易导致人-代理之间出现错配。已有证据表明，适度的人格表达优于极端高低表达，且情境匹配的隐喻优于一成不变的助手角色，因此需要一种能动态校准代理人格与角色的设计框架。

Method: 提出"流式人格框架"（Fluid Personality Framework），核心是两个联合适应的维度：（1）代理的隐喻角色（教练、导师、图书管理员、工具等）；（2）人格表达强度（低、中、高）。两者作为任务上下文、用户目标与特质、情境紧迫度的函数进行动态调整。论文勾勒了该框架及其核心设计维度。

Result: 本文为框架性/理论性论文，尚未报告实证结果。文中引用了两条先验证据作为支撑：（i）在目标导向任务中，适度人格表达在信任、愉悦感和采纳意愿上优于低或高极端表达；（ii）情境匹配的隐喻在用户体验和接受度上优于静态单一助手。

Conclusion: 对话代理的设计应从"固定人格"转向"流式人格"，通过隐喻角色与表达强度的联合动态适配，实现更精准的人机匹配，从而提升AI介导行为改变的效果。该框架为后续设计与实证研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents (CAs) are now ubiquitous, creating new opportunities for AI-mediated behavior change. Their capacity to project nuanced personalities and adopt diverse metaphorical roles raises a design question: how should an agent's persona and personality be calibrated to the moment? Recent evidence suggests that (i) moderate personality expression outperforms low or high extremes on trust, enjoyment, and intention to adopt in goal-oriented tasks, and (ii) context-appropriate metaphors outperform static one-note assistants on user experience and uptake. Yet most CAs still fix both persona and style, risking misalignment when dynamics, urgency, and formality vary, for example in medical information seeking, fitness coaching, and reflective learning. We propose a Fluid Personality Framework that jointly adapts (1) the agent's metaphorical persona, such as coach, tutor, librarian, or tool, and (2) its personality expression intensity, low, medium, or high, as a function of task context, user goals and traits, and situational urgency. We sketch the framework and its core design dimensions.

</details>


### [195] [Conversable Complexity: Agentic LLM Collectives as Interpretable Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01047)
*Elias Najarro,Ane Espeseth,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出，将具有智能体特性的LLM群体作为人工生命（ALife）研究的计算基底。由于这些智能体以自然语言交流，其集体行为可通过文本痕迹和直接询问进行解释。文章扩展了LLM研究中的可解释性概念至智能体群体，并综述了已有实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂性与可解释性通常不可兼得：能够涌现复杂行为的系统往往不透明，而透明的系统又过于简单。作者试图打破这一困境，利用LLM智能体群体的自然语言交互特性，使得复杂集体行为的涌现与可解释性可以共存，从而为人工生命研究提供新的计算基底。

Method: （1）将LLM赋予持久记忆、工具、共享技能及自主发起行动的能力，使其成为智能体（agentic LLM）；（2）构建此类智能体的群体，观察其涌现的集体动力学；（3）利用自然语言交流的文本痕迹和直接询问智能体的方式，对其集体行为进行可解释性分析；（4）综述已有的智能体LLM群体实例，从受控实验到真实部署。

Result: 单个LLM是静态的人工制品，几乎不表现生命相关的涌现属性；但LLM群体在交互中会涌现出孤立模型所缺乏的集体动力学。通过自然语言痕迹和询问智能体本身，这类群体的集体行为具备前所未有的可解释性。文章展示了该思路已有初步实例，从受控实验到真实世界的部署。

Conclusion: LLM智能体群体可以成为ALife研究的有力计算基底，在保留复杂行为涌现能力的同时，通过自然语言交互实现高度的可解释性。这一范式有望弥合复杂性与可解释性之间的鸿沟，为人工生命研究开辟新路径。

Abstract: Complexity and interpretability rarely coincide: systems rich enough for complex behaviours to emerge are usually too opaque to question, while transparent ones are too simple for anything complex to emerge. A single large language model (LLM) is a static artefact, hardly exhibiting any of the emergent properties we associate with life. This changes through interaction: populations of LLMs display emergent dynamics absent from isolated models. Furthermore, LLMs can be endowed with persistent memory, tools and shared skills, and the capacity to initiate actions unprompted, i.e., turning LLMs agentic. In this paper, we argue that such collectives of agents can serve as a computational substrate for Artificial Life (ALife) research. Critically, since the agents communicate in natural language, their collective behaviour can be directly interrogated by examining textual traces and asking the agents themselves. We outline the notion of interpretability in language-model research and extend it for collectives of agents. Lastly, we survey recent examples of agentic LLM collectives that already instantiate the idea of agentic substrates, from controlled experiments to deployments in the wild.

</details>


### [196] [Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01077)
*Xuecheng Liu,Daman Arora,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MPLMs 提出一种消息传递框架替代传统 fork-join 并行推理，通过线程间轻量级通信和抢占机制实现更高效的 LLM 推理扩展，在数独、3-SAT 和长上下文问答任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展（如 CoT）虽提升 LLM 推理能力，但生成长链思维的计算开销巨大。现有 fork-join 并行方法中的线程是瞬态的且缺乏点对点通信，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出消息传递语言模型（MPLMs），通过轻量级 send/receive 原语实现线程间直接通信。核心机制包括：(1) 避免冗余上下文共享以降低通信成本；(2) 抢占机制，允许线程基于同伴的部分信息提前终止。

Result: 在数独任务上，MPLMs 所需上下文空间渐进小于串行 CoT 和并行 FJ；微调后能求解 25×25 数独（标准 CoT/FJ 及前沿推理模型难以处理）；在 3-SAT 上抢占机制提升效率；在大规模预训练模型上，适当提示即可取得有竞争力的长上下文问答结果。

Conclusion: MPLMs 通过消息传递范式克服了 fork-join 的通信与可扩展性局限，为 LLM 并行推理提供了高效的新框架。

Abstract: While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers.
  We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.

</details>


### [197] [Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01103)
*William Philipp,Finn Fassbender,Thorsten Langer,Martje Pauly,Rebecca Herzog,Alexander Baumann,Markus Hobert,Theresa Paulus,Ip Chi Wang,Lukas Goede,Johanna Reimer,Sebastian Löns,Ronald Böck,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedQADE，首个德语开放回答临床评测基准，包含3800个条目，由10位执业医师和9个LLM评估者标注。结果显示，顶尖评估模型Gemini 3 Flash的统计对齐度接近医师上限（κ=0.694 vs. κ=0.709），但自动化评估者缺乏临床元认知——医师会随题目难度增加而主动弃权，而前沿模型对所有题目均给出确定性评分。此外，评估者存在系统性的血统依赖偏差，偏好评分架构相近的同类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放回答评估虽比多项选择基准更具临床效度，但评分瓶颈催生了LLM-as-a-Judge方法。然而，这些自动化评估者是否能复现临床校准能力和审慎性尚未经过检验，尤其是在德语这类缺乏本地评估基础设施的主要临床语言中。

Method: 构建MedQADE基准：首个标准化德语开放回答临床评测数据集，包含3800个条目，由10位执业医师提供人工标注作为金标准，同时由9个LLM评估者进行评分。通过Cohen's κ系数衡量统计对齐度，分析评估者的弃权行为与题目难度的关系以衡量临床元认知，并量化评估者对不同架构模型的血统依赖偏差。

Result: （1）Gemini 3 Flash达到κ=0.694，接近医师天花板κ=0.709，但置信区间较宽；（2）医师随题目难度升高而增加弃权比例，但所有前沿模型均无一例外地给出确定性评分，临床元认知近乎缺失；（3）发现系统性的血统依赖偏差：评估者倾向于给予架构同源模型更高分数，该效应与语言无关。

Conclusion: 统计对齐并不保证临床审慎性，评估者的独立性需要经过显式验证。单纯的评分一致性指标不足以评判LLM评估者在临床等高风险场景中的适用性，还需考察其元认知行为与偏差模式。

Abstract: Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.

</details>


### [198] [Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01115)
*Md Abu Hanif Shaikh,Abdullah Al Shafi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大学的多模态检索增强生成（RAG）聊天机器人系统，结合大语言模型与语义检索，支持文本和图像查询，通过量化推理在受限硬件上部署，将幻觉率从31.7%降至6.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家大学缺乏智能支持系统，现有基于规则的聊天机器人无法处理复杂的领域特定查询，也难以适应不断变化的机构政策，用户难以获取及时可靠的信息。

Method: 系统采用检索增强生成（RAG）架构，将大语言模型与语义检索相结合，从大学手册等机构资源中生成上下文相关的回答。通过视觉-语言模型支持文本和图像多模态查询，应用量化推理以在受限硬件上快速部署。后端使用FastAPI构建可扩展服务，前端采用Next.js实现响应式界面。

Result: 多模态评估显示，系统在文本和图像查询上均保持了较高的满意度评分，但视觉输入的响应时间有所增加。定量评估表明，基于RAG的系统将幻觉率从31.7%显著降低至6.6%，验证了检索增强的有效性。

Conclusion: 多模态RAG架构能有效解决大学信息获取难题，通过检索增强显著抑制幻觉现象，为资源受限环境下的智能校园服务提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.

</details>


### [199] [$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01127)
*Jeremias Bohn,Tizian Dippold,Mahdi Koubaa,Elias R. Wahl,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种可调节底数的对数级量化方法Log_bQuant，在4-bit精度下优于非对称线性量化，适用于消费级GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均匀量化方法在处理低频但高幅值的权重时表现不佳，会产生次优的表示结果。

Method: 提出Log_bQuant，一种具有可调节底数的对数级量化方法，使其能够适应常见的参数分布。

Result: 在4-bit精度下，多个性能基准测试中超越张量粒度的非对称线性量化，同时实现中等推理加速和高内存节省。

Conclusion: Log_bQuant在低比特场景下是一种有效的量化替代方案，特别适合消费级GPU上的私有部署场景。

Abstract: Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [200] [AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01152)
*Roger Beaty,Vijeta Deshpande,Clin K. Y. Lai,Anna Attuch,Namrata Shivagunde,Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Paul V. DiStefano,Sherin Muckatira,Claire E. Stevenson,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AGC-Bench 是一个统一的人工通用创造力基准，涵盖78个数据集、6个领域，并引入基于项目反应理论的 AGC-Judge 校准评分模型。研究发现 LLM 存在单一创造力因子 'c'（解释81.5%方差），且"发挥创造力"提示比推理提示更能提升表现，但顶尖人类在创造力上仍领先顶级 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造力研究长期存在两个核心争论：创造力是领域特定的还是领域通用的？创造力在心理测量上是否可与一般智力分离？随着大语言模型的发展，这两个问题同样适用于 AI 系统，但目前缺乏统一的 AI 创造力基准。现有基准各自为政、代码库碎片化，且 LLM-as-judge 存在严重评分偏差。

Method: 作者系统综述了3101篇论文，筛选出497个基准，构建了 AGC-Bench。采用 agentic harness 将异构代码库转换为 HELM 标准化格式。为校正 LLM-as-judge 偏差，引入 Judge Response Theory（裁判反应理论）进行心理测量校准，并用三个前沿 LLM 的偏差校正评分微调 Qwen3-30B，得到开源评分模型 AGC-Judge。在83个 LLM 上进行因子分析，并通过"发挥创造力"提示与推理提示的对比实验验证基准的有效性。

Result: （1）因子分析揭示单一创造力因子 'c'，解释81.5%的方差，与通用知识/推理相关但可分离；（2）"发挥创造力"提示对模型表现的提升远超启用推理，证明基准追踪的是创造力而非通用能力；（3）在人类匹配子集上，顶尖人类仍领先顶尖 LLM。前沿闭源模型在排行榜上领先，开源模型紧随其后；LLM 在不同领域表现各异，写作类优于科学构思类。

Conclusion: AGC-Bench 为大规模测量 AI 创造力提供了标准化开放基础设施。研究首次在 LLM 中实证发现了类似于智力 'g' 因子的创造力 'c' 因子，并验证了创造力与通用能力的可分离性，同时表明人类在创造力上仍具优势。作者公开了基准、AGC-Judge 模型及人类数据。

Abstract: Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

</details>


### [201] [Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01153)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"对抗语用学"（adversarial pragmatics）作为评估语言模型在指令冲突、嵌入式命令、引语、范围歧义、指示语、间接言语行为及多轮代理对话等情境下行为的基准与标注协议，并构建了包含分类体系、种子基准、专家评估协议及多元指标的实证方法论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估基准往往将复杂的自然语言行为简化为通过/失败二元标签，掩盖了失败根源是能力不足、策略歧义、指令冲突、脚手架失效还是评估者判断不稳定。作者旨在通过引入语言学控制的分类体系，精细化区分这些混淆因素。

Method: 提出"对抗语用学"框架，具体包括：(1) 一个受语言学控制的分类体系（taxonomy）；(2) 一个含18项的种子基准，配备验证器强制执行的元数据；(3) 一个54行的本地种子试点；(4) 一套专家评估协议，区分任务成功、策略合规、安全风险、拒绝结果和评估者置信度五个维度；(5) 评估裁判有效性、诊断歧义性和分类漂移的量化指标。

Result: 论文验证了该框架在实际应用中的可行性，表明通过语言学精细标注和专家多维评估，能够有效诊断和区分模型行为中不同来源的失败，为安全评估提供了更高的透明度和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架将语言学的判断方法论转化为验证安全评估、LLM裁判、黄金集构建、提示注入测试及安全文档的实用工具，为语言模型安全评估领域提供了方法论贡献。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments.
  This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.

</details>


### [202] [Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01208)
*Shayan Talaei,Abhinav Chinta,Devvrit Khatri,Amin Karbasi,Azalia Mirhoseini,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: D2D 通过将疑似模型与基模型的 logit 分布差异蒸馏为 KV-cache 前缀适配器（cartridge），放大隐藏的偏向信号至生成文本中，从而实现对隐蔽偏向的可靠检测，并辅以 Fisher 加权投影的理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在高风险场景中可能被植入隐蔽的偏向（stealth preferential bias），这种偏向仅在特定主题上显现，其余行为与基模型无异，且信号仅存于软 logit 分布中，文本检查无法发现。防守方面临根本性不对称：在不知晓偏向主题的情况下，现有方法无法可靠检测。因此亟需一种无需先验主题知识的隐蔽偏向检测方法。

Method: 提出 Distill to Detect (D2D) 方法：将疑似模型与基模型之间的分布偏移（logit 分布差异）蒸馏到一个 cartridge（KV-cache 前缀适配器）中，该适配器以紧凑的容量捕获主导性分歧，从而将偏向信号放大到生成文本中。同时从 Fisher 加权投影角度建立理论框架，解释该方法的有效性。

Result: D2D 成功放大了隐蔽模型的隐藏偏向，使其达到可被可靠检测的程度，并在多种偏向类型上得到验证。理论分析表明该方法本质上是对 logit 分布偏移进行 Fisher 加权投影，实证观察与理论框架一致。

Conclusion: D2D 将前缀微调适配器的容量瓶颈转化为检测工具，为审计已部署语言模型中的隐藏行为提供了一种实用的构建模块，有效解决了隐蔽偏向检测中的根本性不对称问题。

Abstract: Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.

</details>


### [203] [The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01218)
*Giovanni Monea,Nathan Godey,Kianté Brantley,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"状态-预测分离假设"，即Transformer中状态存储与下一token预测应使用不同的计算流。作者设计了一种双计算流的Transformer变体，实验表明该分离设计在数据效率和计算效率上均优于标准Transformer，下游任务平均提升2-3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer使用同一条前向计算流同时完成两项任务：(1) 预测下一个token；(2) 存储对未来预测有用的状态信息。作者认为这两项功能存在本质冲突，耦合在一起会限制模型性能，因此提出将其解耦分离。

Method: 作者提出一种具有两条独立计算流的Transformer变体：一条流专门负责状态存储（维护长期依赖信息），另一条流专门负责基于当前状态进行下一token预测。通过这种架构分离，两条流可以各自优化，避免梯度信号相互干扰。同时进行了多规模的预训练实验，并通过广泛的实证分析排除了潜在混淆因素。

Result: 实验表明，状态-预测分离在多个规模下均一致地提升了数据效率和计算效率：验证损失更低，在下游任务上平均比标准Transformer高出2-3个百分点。梯度分析进一步揭示了两条计算流在梯度模式上的根本性差异，验证了分离设计的合理性。

Conclusion: 将状态存储与token预测功能解耦是一种有效的架构改进方向，能够显著提升语言模型的训练效率和下游表现，为未来Transformer架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.

</details>


### [204] [Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01233)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种大规模评估框架，通过反向工程人类论文的核心灵感来源，对比LLM与人类研究者产生的创意分布差异。结果表明，LLM的创意过度集中于"桥梁式"机会和"综合式"方法，而人类创意在机会框架和贡献构建方式上分布更为广泛，揭示了LLM在科研品味上与人类的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM科研创意的评估多局限于新颖性、可行性或专家偏好的单一维度判断，缺乏对LLM创意与人类研究者之间系统性差距的刻画。作者旨在量化这一分布性鸿沟，而非仅评估个体创意的优劣。

Method: 作者构建了一个大规模评估框架：首先从高质量人类论文出发，反向工程出一组可能启发其核心创意的紧密相关前期工作；然后以这些论文的标题和摘要作为提示，让LLM生成新创意；最后引入一个双轴"科研品味"分类法——机会模式（opportunity pattern）与研究范式（research paradigm），对每个创意进行画像，并以此量化人类与LLM创意之间的分布差异。

Result: 不同LLM生成的创意集合呈现一致的分布性鸿沟：LLM创意不成比例地集中于"桥梁式"机会和"综合式"方法，而人类论文的参考分布更广泛地覆盖了多种差距框架方式和贡献构建方式。尽管强LLM能产生一系列合理的创意，但其范围始终比人类科研品味更窄且存在系统性偏移。

Conclusion: LLM在科研创意生成上虽具备一定能力，但其"科研品味"与人类存在本质差异——创意范围更窄且分布重心偏移。这一发现为理解AI辅助科研的边界及未来改进方向提供了重要参考。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"建构性对齐"(Constructive Alignment)范式，将AI对齐重新定义为对动态人类偏好轨迹的调控问题，而非静态偏好满足。基于行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，作者将偏好建模为在与AI交互中演化的分层状态变量，并采用控制论框架来形式化这一观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法多将人类偏好视为固定目标进行推断和优化，但这与大量实证证据相悖——偏好是分层的、动态的，并在与技术互动中建构形成。随着AI系统日益持久化、个性化和社会嵌入化，它们正越来越深地参与塑造人类的关注点、价值观和认可内容。因此需要一种新的对齐范式来应对这一现实。

Method: 提出"建构性对齐"范式：(1)借鉴行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为在AI交互下演化的分层状态变量；(2)采用控制论框架，将系统行为与交互设计共同形式化为同时影响世界状态和人类评估状态的联合输入；(3)提出对齐的核心目标是调控AI对人类偏好演化的影响，确保价值轨迹满足一致性、反思性认可、认知根基性、抗操纵性和不确定性下的赋权性等准则。

Result: 作者论证了AI对齐应从"控制AI行为"转向"治理长期价值形成过程"，即对齐问题的本质不在于满足静态偏好，而在于监管AI系统如何影响人类偏好的演化轨迹。该框架为理解和操作化动态偏好环境下的对齐问题提供了新的概念工具。

Conclusion: 对齐应被重新定义为对长期价值形成的治理问题。建构性对齐范式强调，AI系统的设计和部署必须关注其对人类偏好轨迹的塑造作用，确保演化过程而非仅仅结果满足规范性标准，从而实现真正意义上的人机价值协同。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [206] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有限道德"（Bounded Morality）框架，将道德认知形式化为有限智能体在"道德广度"与"道德深度"两个正交维度间的计算权衡问题，主张伦理理论并非互斥的道德真理，而是在不同计算需求体制下的局部高效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型将伦理理论（义务论、后果论、美德伦理学）视为固定的规则或价值函数，忽视了有限智能体在道德推理中面临的真实计算约束。作者希望借鉴赫伯特·西蒙"有限理性"的思路，建立一套能够刻画道德问题计算需求的形式化框架。

Method: 沿两个正交维度形式化道德情境：（1）道德广度（moral breadth）——被视为道德相关实体的范围；（2）道德深度（moral depth）——评估实体间互动所需的推理整合程度。有限资源在这两个维度间施加了不可避免的权衡，由此界定"可行道德计算空间"。在此空间内，不同伦理理论被重新解释为适应不同需求体制的局部高效策略。

Result: 该框架给出了"道德遗憾"（moral regret）和约束条件下的"道德进步"（moral progress）的形式化定义；推导出人工系统中的道德对齐取决于道德推理能力的规模扩展与资源分配方式，而非对人类判断的直接模仿。

Conclusion: 道德不应被理解为在互斥伦理理论间的选择，而应视为有限智能体在广度与深度维度间的计算资源分配问题。这一视角对人工道德智能体的设计具有重要启示：关键在于推理能力的可扩展配置，而非行为层面的简单模仿。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [207] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMM数据模型，一种面向知识文档化的新型信息组织方式。该模型结合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达自由度，旨在突破传统以文档为中心的信息系统的局限性，实现跨学科、跨应用、跨部署的互操作性，同时不强制语义收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息系统围绕文档构建，优化了印刷生产和线性阅读，但限制了知识的结构化、更新、共享与复用。形式化方法虽能解决部分问题，却因过度优先形式结构而牺牲了人类可用性和适用范围，难以获得广泛采用。AI系统正在重塑文档生产，但尚未提供统一的、可移植的传统文档替代方案。本文的动机源于跨学科协作研究中的实际需求，旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先对信息系统的设计空间进行比较分析，定位现有方案的不足。在此基础上提出MMM数据模型，其核心设计哲学是将少量规范性约束（normative constraints）与自由文本标签（free-text labels）的表达自由度相结合。通过参考实现（reference implementation）和试点部署数据进行可行性与初步可用性的实证验证。

Result: MMM模型在不要求语义收敛的前提下实现了跨学科、跨应用和跨部署的互操作性。参考实现证明了模型的可实施性，试点部署数据展示了其早期可用性。该模型在形式化方法的严格性与传统文档的灵活性之间取得了平衡。

Conclusion: MMM数据模型为知识文档化提供了一种兼顾结构化与人类可用性的新路径。它打破了文档中心化组织的局限，在保留表达自由度的同时引入必要的规范性约束，有望成为替代传统文档的统一知识表达与交换方案，尤其适用于跨学科协作研究场景。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [208] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种受约束、可验证的智能体框架，将LLM的自由形式代码生成转变为基于六类采集器分类法的类型化JSON配置，通过模板约束、静态DAG执行和规则化质量检查，实现可复用的确定性网页数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接利用LLM和智能体从自然语言需求生成网页爬虫虽然可行，但存在依赖错误、选择器失效、模式不匹配和页面结构异构等可靠性问题，因此需要一个更稳定、可验证的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个受约束、可验证的智能体框架，将LLM输出从自由形式代码转变为类型化JSON采集器配置。核心组件包括：(1) 六类采集器分类法；(2) 模板与工具函数约束；(3) 静态Airflow DAG执行；(4) 基于规则的质量检查；(5) 结构化反馈修正机制。

Result: 在138个任务上实验表明，分类法支持基于描述的需求类型判定；在80个独立来源验证的任务上，该框架实现零执行阶段LLM token消耗和最低平均墙钟时间，以适度的一次性质量换取可复用、确定性、可验证的执行路径。

Conclusion: 该框架为重复性定时开放网页数据采集提供了一条可复用、低成本且可验证的执行路径，适合需要稳定周期性采集的场景。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [209] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向航路空中交通管制（ATC）的无冲突路径规划算法，核心设计理念是兼顾人类管制员的可解释性需求与操作约束。算法在解空间框架中集成了三种基于意图的冲突检测方法，并提出了两种搜索节点变体（SSPPV和SSPPE）。在MUAC Delta扇区场景中，基于区域冲突检测的SSPPV表现最优，5海里网格下平均计算时间仅3.69毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法虽多，但大多未充分考虑空中交通管制员的实际需求，导致在战术控制中的操作化应用受限。可解释性差、计算效率不足、与人类决策逻辑脱节是核心痛点。因此，需要设计一种具备内在可解释性、计算高效且明确面向人类使用的决策支持算法。

Method: 算法以两条原则为指导：(1) 解空间显示的可解释性与灵活性——暴露所有可行的安全动作并适应变化的优化目标；(2) 管制员在执行间隔标准、机动性限制、航路点最小化和路由实用性等操作约束时的自然决策逻辑。在此基础上，将三种基于意图的冲突检测方法（基于距离、基于时间区间、基于区域）集成到解空间框架中，并提出基于顶点（SSPPV）和基于边（SSPPE）两种搜索节点方案进行路径规划。

Result: 在基于马斯特里赫特高空区域管制中心（MUAC）Delta扇区的运行相关场景中，使用5海里网格进行评估，SSPPV配合基于区域的冲突检测方法性能最优，平均路径计算时间仅为3.69毫秒。

Conclusion: 面向管制员使用需求设计的解空间路径规划算法，尤其是SSPPV与区域冲突检测的组合，能够在保持可解释性的同时实现极高的计算效率，展示了在战术ATC环境中作为决策支持工具的实用潜力。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality.
