*Miruna Cretu,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Saeed Saremi,Omar Mahmood,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Vishnu Sresht,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynLaD 是一个面向小分子生成的潜在扩散框架，将基于配体的药物设计目标（"做什么分子"）与合成可及性（"怎么做分子"）统一在同一模型中。它通过编码器-双解码器架构和扩散Transformer，同时生成3D分子结构与合成路线，在保持药效团匹配的同时确保分子可合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前小分子生成模型通常在分子质量（如活性、药效团匹配）和合成可及性之间难以兼顾：优化一者往往牺牲另一者，导致发现的优分子难以实际合成，形成瓶颈。SynLaD 旨在打破这一困境，实现"设计即合成"的统一框架。

Method: SynLaD 采用潜在扩散框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 编码器将分子映射到潜在表征；(2) 两个解码头——几何头重建原子类型和3D坐标，自回归合成头以序列化反应标记输出合成路线；(3) 扩散Transformer在学习的潜在空间中生成新分子，并以药效团特征为条件进行引导。训练时同时约束3D结构重建和合成路径生成。

Result: 在针对生物活性配体的类似物生成任务中，SynLaD 在生成分子的合成可及性、结构多样性和命中率方面均优于现有基线方法，证明单一模型能够同时产出形状匹配目标药效团且附带可行合成方案的分子。

Conclusion: SynLaD 首次在统一框架中实现药效团条件3D分子生成与合成路线预测的联合建模，为基于配体的药物设计提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，有望加速从计算设计到实验验证的迭代周期。

Abstract: We present SynLaD, a latent diffusion framework for small-molecule generation that unifies ligand-based drug design objectives (what to make) with synthetic accessibility (how to make it). Current models typically optimize one objective at the expense of the other, creating a bottleneck for discovering high-scoring and synthesizable molecules. SynLaD combines reaction-constrained generation with pharmacophore-conditioned 3D design by learning a latent space that decodes to both 3D structures and synthesis pathways. An encoder maps molecules to a latent representation used by two decoder heads: (i) a geometric head that reconstructs atom types and coordinates and (ii) an autoregressive synthesis head that outputs synthetic routes in a serialized, reaction-based notation. A diffusion transformer generates novel latents in the learned space, conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Across analogue generation tasks for bioactive ligands, SynLaD outperforms existing baselines in synthesizable and diverse hit generation, demonstrating that a single model can produce shape-aligned molecules with feasible synthesis plans.

</details>


### [317] [Muon as a Residual Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01124)
*Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对Muon优化器的一种机制性解释：Muon可被理解为训练过程中的隐式残差连接。通过对更新进行正交化，Muon牺牲了部分即时梯度保真度，但改善了下游层的表征保持能力，从而在"局部拟合速度"与"下游可用性"之间实现权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为训练大神经网络的优秀优化器，其成功经验已有多种不同视角的解释，但缺乏一个统一且简洁的机制性理解，促使作者寻找一个更根本的解释框架。

Method: 作者在受控的线性优化环境中研究Muon的行为，分析正交化更新如何影响表征学习：对比直接梯度下降与Muon在"拟合局部目标"和"下游层可利用性"两个维度上的表现差异，揭示其内在的权衡机制。

Result: 研究表明，Muon学习的表征在拟合局部目标时较慢，但更容易被下游层利用。正交化更新牺牲了即时梯度保真度，却换来了更好的表征保持能力，这类似于隐式残差连接的效果。

Conclusion: Muon的有效性可归结为其隐式残差连接特性——在局部下降与下游可用性之间取得平衡。这一发现为理解Muon提供了概念性解释，也为设计兼顾局部优化与下游泛化的优化器提供了新的设计视角。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as one of the most effective optimizers for training large neural networks, yet its empirical success has been explained from several different perspectives. In this paper, we propose a simple mechanistic interpretation: Muon can be understood as an implicit residual connection during training. Specifically, orthogonalizing the update can sacrifice some immediate gradient fidelity while improving representation preservation for downstream layers. We study this trade-off in controlled linear optimization settings, where Muon can learn representations that are slower to fit a local target but easier for downstream layers to exploit. Our results suggest a conceptual explanation for Muon and a design perspective for optimizers that balance local descent with downstream usability.

</details>


### [318] [ZO-Act: Efficient Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning via One-Shot Activation-Informed Low-Rank Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01125)
*Xun Dong,Yibo Xu,Naigang Wang,Xin Li,Penghang Yin,Zi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZO-Act 提出一种基于激活信息的零阶优化微调方法，将扰动限制在由输入激活导出的固定低秩子空间中，仅优化轻量系数矩阵，从而降低方差、兼容 Adam 和量化 LLM，并在多项任务上超越现有 ZO 微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化微调方法要么扰动全模型权重，要么在随机构造的低维子空间中扰动，导致高方差估计和有限性能。本文旨在利用 LLM 激活的低秩结构，设计一种更低方差、更高效的 ZO 微调方法。

Method: ZO-Act 对每个线性层在初始化时利用输入激活计算一个小型激活基（activation basis），并将优化限制在该基张成的固定低秩子空间内，仅对轻量系数矩阵进行前向零阶优化。该方法使可训练变量显式化，兼容 Adam 等动量优化器；同时冻结低位权重，自然支持量化 LLM 微调。理论分析表明，在受限系数空间上做零阶优化可同时降低方差依赖的收敛项和有限差分误差，而低秩激活和梯度结构则控制了子空间近似偏差。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B 和 INT4 量化 Llama-3-8B 上的实验表明，ZO-Act 在语言理解、问答和常识推理任务上均一致优于强 ZO 微调基线。

Conclusion: ZO-Act 通过激活感知的低秩子空间约束，有效降低零阶梯度估计方差，实现高效、高性能且兼容量化模型的大语言模型微调，为反向传播不可用或内存受限场景提供了有力方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables fine-tuning large language models when backpropagation is unavailable or memory-prohibitive, but existing methods often perturb full model weights or randomly constructed low-dimensional subspaces, yielding high-variance estimates and limited performance. We propose ZO-Act, an activation-informed ZO fine-tuning method that restricts perturbations to a fixed low-rank subspace derived from input activations. For each linear layer, ZO-Act computes a small activation basis once at initialization and optimizes only lightweight coefficient matrices using forward-only loss evaluations. This reduces the effective perturbation dimension, exposes explicit trainable variables compatible with momentum-based optimizers such as Adam, and naturally supports quantized LLM fine-tuning by keeping low-bit weights frozen. We analyze ZO-Act as zeroth-order optimization over a restricted coefficient space and show that perturbing the low-dimensional coefficients reduces both the variance-dependent convergence term and the finite-difference error of the ZO estimator, at the cost of a controlled subspace approximation bias that is mitigated by the low-rank structure of LLM activations and gradients. Experiments on Llama-3-8B, OPT-13B, and INT4 Llama-3-8B show consistent gains over strong ZO fine-tuning baselines across language understanding, question answering, and commonsense reasoning.

</details>


### [319] [GAIA: Geometry-Adaptive Operator Learning for Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01128)
*Meenakshi Krishnan,Pranav Pulijala,Ke Chen,Haizhao Yang,Ramani Duraiswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAIA 是一种几何自适应积分自编码器，通过将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌，并利用交叉注意力条件化积分变换层，实现了在任意几何上统一处理前向问题（含边值问题）和反问题的算子学习，无需重新训练或迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何自适应神经算子主要针对输入输出共享同一空间域的前向问题设计，无法有效处理输入输出位于不同域的边值问题和反问题（如EIT、光学层析成像）。这限制了算子学习在更广泛物理场景中的适用性。

Method: GAIA 将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌（geometry tokens），通过交叉注意力将这些令牌注入积分变换层，使卷积核能局部自适应几何特征。该架构支持在单次前向传播中同时处理前向（含BVP）和反问题，无需迭代优化、重新训练或图构建。

Result: 在7个2D/3D基准测试上评估，GAIA 在所有反问题和BVP任务上均取得最优结果：翼型流场重建的中位相对L²误差降低64%，EIT降低27%（相比次优摊销方法）；在MCB的每个形状类别上均超越所有基线。在前向问题上与专用求解器竞争力相当，且在变压器基线退化的不同点分辨率下保持稳定精度。

Conclusion: GAIA 通过几何令牌与交叉注意力机制成功统一了任意几何上的前向与反问题求解，为算子学习拓展到更广泛的应用场景提供了有效的单一架构方案。

Abstract: Operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries builds fast neural surrogates for large-scale simulation. Although recent geometry-adaptive neural operators have made substantial progress, they are mainly designed for forward problems in which inputs and outputs share the same spatial domain. This limits their applicability for boundary value problems (BVPs) and inverse problems, where inputs and outputs may live on different domains. We introduce the Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA), an operator learning model that encodes the domain boundary and the interior field distribution into geometry tokens, and conditions integral transform layers on these tokens via cross-attention, allowing the kernel to adapt locally to geometric features. This yields a single architecture for forward (including BVPs) and inverse problems on arbitrary domains in one pass, without retraining, iterative optimization, or graph construction. We evaluate GAIA on seven 2D and 3D benchmarks, four of which are new or substantially extended benchmarks for inverse problems and BVP: electrical impedance tomography, optical tomography, 3D Darcy flow on varying geometries, and a modified setting of Poisson BVP on mechanical components benchmark (MCB). GAIA sets new state-of-the-art results on every inverse and BVP task, reducing median relative $L^2$ error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT relative to the next best amortized method, and outperforming all baselines on every shape category of MCB. On other forward problems, GAIA is competitive with specialized solvers while maintaining stable accuracy across point resolutions on which transformer-based baselines degrade.

</details>


### [320] [QuasiMoTTo: Quasi-Monte Carlo Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01179)
*Michael Y. Li,Anthony Zhan,Kanishk Gandhi,Noah D. Goodman,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuasiMoTTo 利用拟蒙特卡洛方法生成相关的并行样本，在保持每个样本边缘分布与语言模型一致的前提下，以更少的样本量（减少25-47%）达到与i.i.d.采样相同的推理精度，并在强化学习训练中减少50%的训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统并行推理采用i.i.d.采样，虽易于扩展但产生大量冗余解，浪费推理算力。作者认为独立性与样本效率之间的权衡并非根本性的，存在一个设计空间可以在保持并行化的同时生成相关但精确的样本，从而提升推理和强化学习的样本效率。

Method: 将自回归采样重新参数化为逆CDF采样，用拟蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法生成底层均匀随机数。QMC使均匀随机数分布更均匀，从而减少输出空间的冗余覆盖。虽然批次内样本相关，但每个样本的边缘分布仍保持与语言模型一致，因此可用于策略梯度训练。同时开发了无偏bootstrap估计器来评估相关采样器下的pass@k指标。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo以少25-47%的样本匹配i.i.d.的pass@k准确率，且常能达到任意边缘保持采样器的pass@k上界。在GRPO策略梯度强化学习中，以50%更少的训练步数匹配i.i.d.性能。收益来源于更高的覆盖率，为每批次提供更强的学习信号。

Conclusion: QuasiMoTTo证明了通过设计相关但边缘精确的并行采样器，可以突破"并行扩展必须以冗余为代价"的固有认知，在不牺牲扩展便利性的前提下显著提升推理与训练的算力效率。

Abstract: Scaling inference compute, by generating many parallel attempts per problem, is a costly but reliable lever for improving language model capabilities. By default these attempts are generated independently, wasting inference compute on redundant solutions. This waste seems unavoidable. After all, independence is what makes parallel sampling trivial to scale. However, this tradeoff is not fundamental: there is a rich design space of samplers that generate correlated but exact samples entirely in parallel. We explore this design space as an avenue for improving sample efficiency in scaling inference compute and reinforcement learning (RL). Concretely, we introduce QuasiMoTTo, which uses correlated samples as a drop-in replacement for i.i.d. samples. To generate these samples, QuasiMoTTo uses a reparameterization of autoregressive sampling as inverse-CDF sampling and draws the underlying uniforms with quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC); because QMC spreads the uniforms out more evenly than i.i.d., the resulting samples cover the output space with far less redundancy. Even though the batch is correlated, each sample is marginally distributed according to the language model, so we can use the batch for policy-gradient training. Our empirical analysis focuses on understanding how efficiently QuasiMoTTo can turn compute into performance. To evaluate correlated samplers, whose dependence breaks standard pass@k estimators, we first develop an unbiased bootstrap estimator. Across four reasoning benchmarks, QuasiMoTTo matches i.i.d. pass@k accuracy with 25-47% fewer samples. Strikingly, QuasiMoTTo often saturates an upper bound on pass@k that holds for any marginal-preserving sampler. We also apply QuasiMoTTo to policy-gradient RL (GRPO) where it matches i.i.d. performance with 50% fewer training steps. These gains come from higher coverage, which yields a stronger learning signal per batch.

</details>


### [321] [Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps for Online Feedback-Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01144)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Jens Sjölund,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IMPFM 提出了一种基于序列控制交互式多粒子流映射的框架，用于在偏好未知且仅通过序列反馈揭示的场景下实现样本高效的在线探索与奖励对齐。通过粒子间后验样本共享和探索-利用重加权机制，该框架在保持分布覆盖多样性的同时防止奖励过度优化和模式坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于生成模型的免训练奖励对齐方法仅擅长在分布的狭窄局部区域探索，当偏好先验未知且仅通过序列反馈逐步揭示时，需要广泛的全局探索来发现高效用区域，现有方法难以胜任。

Method: 提出 IMPFM 框架：1）利用流映射（flow maps）驱动一组交互式粒子逐步向目标分布传输；2）在每个重采样步骤中，通过粒子集合的集体后验样本来修正个体粒子的漂移，实现后验样本共享；3）引入基于多粒子交互的探索-利用重加权机制；4）证明该框架产生一个多粒子交互感知的 Feynman-Kac 校正器，将系统引向 KL-倾斜目标分布。

Result: 在多种搜索和对齐任务上的广泛实证评估及消融实验表明，IMPFM 相比现有基线方法具有显著优越性。

Conclusion: IMPFM 通过序列校正的多粒子动力学机制，在保持结构多样性的同时实现了全局探索，克服了标准 SMC 采样器的权重退化问题，并有效防止了模式坍塌和奖励过度优化。

Abstract: While generative models have enabled training-free reward alignment, current methods typically excel in local exploration within narrow regions of the underlying distribution. These approaches struggle when preferences are unknown a priori and only revealed through sequential feedback-a scenario demanding broad exploration to uncover high-utility regions. To address this, we propose Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM), a framework for sample-efficient online feedback-driven search. IMPFM progressively transports a group of interactive particles toward the target distribution, maintaining the broad coverage essential for heterogeneous preference alignment. IMPFM introduces a principled and efficient posterior sample sharing mechanism across particles powered by flow maps. By correcting individual particle drift with the collective posterior samples of the entire ensemble at each resampling step, the framework maximizes sample utility to enable global exploration while actively mitigating reward over-optimization, typical of standard control frameworks. Paired with a principled exploration-exploitation reweighting mechanism involving multi-particle interaction, this sequentially corrected multi-particle dynamics explicitly preserves structural diversity and overcomes the weight degeneracy inherent to standard SMC samplers. Crucially, we prove that the resulting sampling framework yields a multi-particle interaction-aware Feynman-Kac corrector that progressively steers the multi-particle system toward a KL-tilted target distribution, facilitating global exploration and preventing mode collapse. Extensive empirical evaluations and rigorous ablations across diverse search and alignment tasks confirm the efficacy of IMPFM over existing baselines.

</details>


### [322] [Right in the Right Way: LM Training with Verifiable Rewards and Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01181)
*Mehul Damani,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗式生成器-判别器框架，将可验证奖励（RLVR）与来自人类示范的判别器学习信号相结合，解决了RLVR在训练语言模型时忽视风格、结构等不可验证主观属性的问题，在代码修复、故事生成和奖励黑客防御等任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR方法仅优化可客观评分的指标，忽视了风格、结构等不可验证的人类偏好属性，导致多样性崩溃、回复不自然和奖励黑客等问题。需要一种能够同时优化可验证与不可验证属性的训练范式。

Method: 提出对抗式生成器-判别器框架：生成器通过强化学习同时最大化任务准确率和判别器给出的对抗奖励；判别器与生成器策略联合训练，学习区别人工书写输出与模型生成输出，作为人类输出分布的代理，提供难以形式化为标量奖励的生成质量反馈。

Result: 在漏洞修复任务中，方法在匹配RLVR基线最终性能的同时，产生的解决方案编辑距离显著更低；在故事生成中，显著提升了胜率，且生成的故事更多样、更接近人类风格；在奖励黑客基准测试中，几乎消除了模型不当行为，同时保持了高基准分数。

Conclusion: 该方法桥接了强化学习与监督微调（SFT），为联合优化任务的可验证与不可验证属性提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training LMs on tasks with well-defined success metrics, such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. However, current RLVR methods optimize only what can be objectively scored, often neglecting subjective, non-verifiable aspects of human-like outputs, such as style and structure. This limitation leads to well-documented failure modes such as diversity collapse, unnatural-sounding responses, and reward hacking. We propose an adversarial generator-discriminator framework that augments verifiable rewards with a learned signal from human demonstrations. A generator model is trained using RL to maximize both task accuracy and an adversarial reward derived from a discriminator. The discriminator, trained alongside the generator policy, learns to distinguish human-written outputs from model-generated ones. The discriminator serves as a learned proxy for the human output distribution, providing feedback on aspects of generation that are difficult to formalize as scalar rewards. Across diverse domains, including bug fixing and open-ended generation, our approach consistently improves non-verifiable properties while preserving the accuracy gains of RLVR. In bug fixing, our method produces solutions with significantly lower edit distance compared to RLVR baselines while matching end performance. In story generation, our method significantly improves win rate while producing stories that are diverse and more human-like. And in a simple reward hacking benchmark, our method nearly eliminates model misbehavior while maintaining high benchmark scores. Together, these results show that our approach bridges RL and SFT, offering a scalable path toward jointly optimizing the verifiable and non-verifiable properties of a task.

</details>


### [323] [A Lightweight Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Multivariate Time Series using Hierarchical-JEPA on ECG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01145)
*Siwon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ER-JEPA 是一个用于多变量时间序列的轻量级自监督学习框架，受心脏病学家诊断思路启发，采用双层层次化结构。该模型在约18万条10秒心电记录上预训练后，在ST-MEM基准上取得了最优下游性能，且计算快速、资源占用极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学数据分析常面临目标数据集有限、而存在大规模无标注通用数据集的场景。现有自监督方法虽能利用大数据集，但针对多变量时间序列（如12导联心电图）的高效层次化表征学习仍不充分。作者希望模仿心脏病学家的分层诊断思路，构建一个既能捕捉多尺度抽象表征又轻量高效的SSL框架。

Method: 提出事件重建联合嵌入预测架构（ER-JEPA），核心包括：(1) 两阶段结构——先为每个时间区间构建表征，再将这些表征作为单变量时间序列处理；(2) 层次化集成两个JEPA模块，构成层次化JEPA（H-JEPA）；(3) 以Vision Transformer（ViT）为骨干网络。两个JEPA的结构性串联使模型能够编码多层抽象表征，以增强对复杂任务的预测能力。

Result: 在约180,000条10秒12导联心电图记录上预训练后，模型在ST-MEM基准上取得了最先进的下游性能，同时具备快速计算和极低资源消耗的优势。此外，还分析了预训练阶段层次化表征的敏感性。

Conclusion: H-JEPA成功应用于12导联ECG多变量时间序列数据，验证了层次化JEPA架构在医学时间序列自监督学习中的有效性，为有限标注场景下的心电图分析提供了一种高效、轻量的解决方案。

Abstract: Data analysis in the medical domain often encounters scenarios involving a limited target dataset and a large, unannotated dataset with a general distribution. Under such circumstances, self-supervised learning (SSL) methods are highly effective for utilizing large datasets, making them a popular choice for electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. This work presents the Event Reconstruction Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ER-JEPA), a lightweight SSL framework for multivariate time series, whose name and two-fold hierarchical structure are inspired by the diagnostic approach of cardiologists. At its core, ER-JEPA features: (1) a two-stage structure that constructs representations for each time interval and subsequently processes these representations as a univariate time series, (2) the hierarchical integration of two Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), and (3) a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone. The structural concatenation of two JEPAs categorizes the model as a Hierarchical JEPA (H-JEPA), designed to encode multiple levels of abstract representations for enhanced prediction on complex tasks. This study reports a successful application of H-JEPA to 12-lead ECG data as a multivariate time series alongside an analysis of the sensitivity of hierarchical representation during the pretraining stage. Pretrained on approximately 180,000 10-second recordings, the model achieves state-of-the-art downstream performance on the ST-MEM benchmark, with rapid computation and minimal resource usage.

</details>


### [324] [Efficient Compression of Structured and Unstructured Volumes via Learned 3D Gaussian Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01164)
*Landon Dyken,Sharmistha Chakrabarti,Nathan Debardeleben,Steve Petruzza,Qi Wu,Will Usher,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D高斯原语的显式体积数据压缩模型，将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示，通过加权聚合相交高斯来重建标量值。该方法天然编码域几何，无需网格存储，在非结构化体积数据上全面超越现有INR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于非结构化体积数据的隐式神经表示（INR）不编码几何信息，需要额外存储部分网格以进行后续采样，限制了压缩效果。同时，新颖视图合成中3D高斯显式集合的成功启发了作者探索将其用于体积数据压缩。

Method: 提出基于3D高斯原语的显式压缩模型：1）将3D高斯集合重新定义为标量场的显式表示；2）设计加权聚合采样策略，通过相交高斯的加权贡献重建空间位置的标量值；3）开发针对结构化和非结构化模型的CUDA加速采样管线；4）设计鼓励精确域编码的损失函数；5）提出基于采样误差的新型致密化策略。

Result: 在结构化体积数据上，该方法以显著更快的训练速度达到与INR相当的 reconstruction 质量；在非结构化体积数据上，所有指标均显著优于现有INR方法，且无需额外存储网格信息。

Conclusion: 基于3D高斯的显式模型通过自然编码域几何，为体积数据压缩提供了新的有效方案，特别在非结构化数据上实现了大幅性能提升和更高压缩比，消除了网格存储的瓶颈。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that implicit neural representations (INRs) can be trained to effectively compress structured and unstructured volume data, allowing for direct data querying with a reduced memory footprint. However, as existing INRs for unstructured volumes do not encode geometry, they require partial mesh storage for later sampling, limiting achievable compression. At the same time, novel view synthesis methods have shown that explicit collections of 3D Gaussians can be used to accurately visualize volume data. In this work, we introduce an explicit model for volume data compression based on 3D Gaussian primitives. We reinterpret collections of 3D Gaussians as an explicit representation of a scalar field and use a sampling strategy that reconstructs scalar values at spatial locations through weighted aggregation of intersecting Gaussians. We develop optimized CUDA-accelerated pipelines for structured and unstructured model sampling, loss functions that encourage accurate domain encoding by our models, and a novel sampling-error based densification strategy. Our explicit formulation naturally encodes domain geometry, eliminating the need for mesh storage in unstructured volumes and introducing significantly higher compression opportunities. Compared to existing INRs, we demonstrate that our explicit model achieves competitive reconstruction quality with significant training speedups on structured volumes, while markedly outperforming in all metrics on unstructured volumes.

</details>


### [325] [Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Chung-Yiu Yau,Hongzhou Lin,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM强化学习后训练（RL post-training）的增益高度集中在Transformer中间层，仅训练单一层即可恢复甚至超越全参数训练的多数性能。该模式在多个模型家族、RL算法和任务领域间稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练通常均匀更新所有模型参数，隐含假设各层贡献相当。本文挑战这一假设，旨在系统揭示RL适配在Transformer各层间的分布规律。

Method: 引入"层贡献度"（layer contribution）指标，衡量单独训练某一层所能恢复的全参数RL训练增益比例。在7个模型（Qwen3、Qwen2.5系列）、3种RL算法（GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO）和多个任务（数学推理、代码生成、智能体决策）上进行系统逐层分析。

Result: RL增益高度集中于少数甚至单层；高贡献层稳定出现在Transformer中间层，而输入和输出端附近层贡献显著较低。层重要性排序在数据集、任务、模型家族和RL算法间保持强相关。

Conclusion: RL后训练存在显著的层稀疏性，中间层是关键。这一发现挑战了全参数均匀更新的常规做法，为高效RL微调策略设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.

</details>


### [326] [Neural Certificate Pricing for Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01185)
*Jingyi Chen,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NCP利用无监督学习框架，训练神经网络预测对偶价格，通过结构化恢复层构造原始边际解，以摊销分离的方式解决组合优化问题，无需枚举指数级候选解即可实现高质量决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题的核心困难在于：验证给定解的可行性可在多项式时间内完成，但证明最优性需要指数级搜索。现有方法未能充分挖掘这种"验证易、搜索难"的非对称性。

Method: 提出Neural Certificate Pricing（NCP）框架：神经网络预测证书级别的对偶价格（certificate-level dual prices），结构化恢复层据此构造诱导的原始边际解。核心思想是"摊销分离"（amortized separation）——不枚举违反不等式，而是学习残差价格，使其聚合效应进入恢复过程。当证书一致性条件满足时，恢复的边际解全局可行。

Result: 在三类组合优化问题上，NCP要么大幅优于SOTA神经基线，要么以极低的计算成本与其持平，并展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。理论分析证明，预测价格的一阶误差仅导致目标值的二阶损失。

Conclusion: NCP通过利用组合优化中验证与搜索的非对称性，以摊销分离策略实现了高效、可泛化的神经组合优化方法，兼具理论保证和实证优势。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization (CO) problems are difficult because certifiable discrete structure induces exponential search. One needs to search over the set exponentially many candidates to certify optimality, however, the structural feasibility of a path, packing, or cover can be verified in polynomial time once supplied. In this study, we introduce Neural Certificate Pricing (NCP) that exploits this asymmetry under an unsupervised learning framework. A neural network is trained to predict certificate-level dual prices, while a structured recovery layer constructs the induced primal marginal. NCP can be viewed as amortized separation: instead of enumerating violated inequalities, it learns the residual prices through which their aggregate effect enters recovery. When the certificate-consistency condition holds, the recovered marginal is globally feasible, and a local theory shows that first-order errors in the predicted price induce only second-order loss in objective value. Across three classes of CO problems, NCP either outperforms state-of-the-art neural baselines by large margins or matches them at a fraction of the computation time, and shows stronger out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [327] [Quantum vs. Classical Machine Learning: A Unified Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01197)
*Chuanming Yu,Jiaming Liu,Zihao Ge,Xiongfei Wu,Lulu Zhu,Pengzhan Zhao,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究对比了7对量子机器学习(QML)模型与经典模型在监督学习和强化学习中的表现，发现QML模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上尚未超越经典基线，但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于量子机器学习相对于经典模型是否具有性能优势的证据不足，亟需系统性的实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 选取7对模型（涵盖监督学习与强化学习），在统一框架下对QML模型与其经典对应模型进行性能对比实验，评估指标包括预测性能、策略稳定性、训练时间、噪声过滤能力及假阳性控制。

Result: 评估的QML模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间方面均未超越经典基线模型，但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面展现出一定优势。

Conclusion: QML目前尚不具备全面超越经典方法的能力，但在特定任务（如噪声过滤）上具有应用前景。论文总结了QML在硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性三方面面临的挑战，为后续鲁棒性研究和参数优化提供了基础。

Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a promising computational paradigm for machine learning (ML), with the potential to offer computational advantages over classical approaches. At this stage, the evidence supporting the performance and advantages of quantum machine learning (QML) models relative to classical models is insufficient.To address this gap, this paper presents an empirical study on the performance of QML models and their classical counterparts. We compare seven model pairs spanning supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Our results indicate that the evaluated quantum machine learning models do not yet surpass the classical baselines in overall prediction performance, policy stability, or training time. Nevertheless, QML remains a promising approach for filtering noise and controlling false positives. Our research findings summarize the challenges facing quantum machine learning across hardware environments, training efficiency, and convergence stability, providing a foundation for research into the robustness and parameter optimization of QML. This work is publicly available at https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML.

</details>


### [328] [TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01204)
*Patrick Podest,Marco Pichler,Elias Bürger,Levente Zólyomi,Bernhard Voggenberger,Wilhelm Berghammer,Daniel Klotz,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TiRex-2 是一个基于 xLSTM 的循环时间序列基础模型，从单变量拓展到多变量预测，支持历史协变量和未来已知协变量。其核心创新在于采用记忆中心循环架构实现流式场景下的恒定每块推理成本，结合双向时间混合器和非对称分组注意力变量混合器，首次同时实现未来协变量集成与严格因果性。提出合成耦合管线支持大规模多变量预训练，在零样本评测中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer类时间序列基础模型虽能捕捉跨变量依赖，但存在两大缺陷：(1) 上下文长度带来O(n²)复杂度；(2) 新观测到达时需全历史重计算，无法适应真实场景中数据持续流式到达的需求。此外，尚无模型能同时支持未来已知协变量集成与对目标变量的严格因果约束。

Method: (1) 架构：采用循环xLSTM设计，以恒定每块成本处理流式数据；双向时间混合器搭配非对称分组注意力变量混合器，实现未来协变量注入并保持目标变量严格因果；(2) 预训练：提出合成耦合管线，从大规模单变量语料中动态组合生成多样化的多变量训练样本；(3) 参数规模：单变量模式使用3840万激活参数，多变量额外激活4410万参数。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench上取得零样本SOTA性能；在任意上下文长度的流式推理中保持稳定，推理成本不随序列长度增长；每块推理成本恒定。

Conclusion: TiRex-2 通过记忆中心循环架构成功突破了Transformer模型在流式预测中的计算瓶颈，首次同时实现未来协变量集成与严格因果性，代表了时间序列基础模型向真实流式场景迈进的重要一步。

Abstract: We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.

</details>


### [329] [Language-Critique Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01225)
*Chih-Han Yang,Dai-Jie Wu,Yun-Ping Huang,Ping-Chun Hsieh,Kenneth Marino,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号的模仿学习框架，通过语言批评（language-critique）替代传统的标量信号（如置信度、判别器分数），从次优演示中学习鲁棒策略。该方法在行为克隆和扩散策略上进行了实例化（LC-BC 和 LC-DP），理论上证明了该目标函数可在标准假设下上界专家性能差距，并在导航、操控和游戏等连续控制任务中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从次优演示中学习的模仿学习方法依赖压缩的标量监督信号（如置信度估计、判别器分数、重要性权重），这些信号无法显式表达任务进度、失败模式或纠正动作等中间推理过程，导致信息损失。作者希望利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号，保留丰富的反馈信息，从而学习更鲁棒的策略。

Method: 提出语言批评（language-critique）框架：首先从演示中构建语言标签，显式描述当前进度、识别次优行为并提供细粒度的纠正指导；然后引入语言批评损失函数，直接使用结构化语言信号训练策略而非将其压缩为标量。该方法在行为克隆和扩散策略上分别实例化为 LC-BC 和 LC-DP。同时提供了理论分析，证明所提目标函数在标准假设下可上界专家性能差距。

Result: 在导航、操控和游戏等多种连续控制任务上进行了实证评估，所提方法（LC-BC 和 LC-DP）一致性地优于强模仿学习基线和离线强化学习基线。

Conclusion: 自然语言可以作为一种强大且结构化的监督形式，用于从次优数据中学习鲁棒策略。语言批评框架避免了标量信号的表达能力瓶颈，能够捕获任务进度、失败模式和纠正行为等细粒度信息，为从次优演示中学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Prior work on imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations typically relies on compressed supervision signals such as confidence estimates, discriminator scores, or importance weights. These scalar signals are inherently limited, as they cannot explicitly express intermediate reasoning about task progress, failure modes, or corrective actions. We propose a language-critique framework for imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations that instead leverages natural language as a structured supervision signal, avoiding the collapse of expressive feedback into scalars. Our method first constructs language labels from demonstrations that explicitly describe current progress, identify suboptimal behaviors, and provide fine-grained corrective guidance. We then introduce a language-critique loss that directly trains policies using these structured signals without reducing them to scalars, and instantiate it for both behavior cloning and diffusion policies, yielding LC-BC and LC-DP. We further provide a theoretical result showing that the proposed objective upper-bounds the expert performance gap under standard assumptions. Empirically, we evaluate on diverse continuous control tasks spanning navigation, manipulation, and gameplay, where our methods consistently outperform strong imitation learning and offline reinforcement learning baselines. These results demonstrate that language can serve as a powerful and structured form of supervision for learning robust policies from suboptimal data.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [Similarity-Based Prediction for Digital Twins: Panel Data, Theory, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00128)
*Ruihang Han,Li-Hsiang Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: StaLoP 是一种非参数动态面板预测框架，通过"目标局部预测兼容性"而非时间邻近性来加权利用历史面板信息，结合协变量局部化进行预测。论文给出了偏差-方差刻画、渐近正态性、联合预测带及模型选择准则等理论结果，并在序列预测、模拟器校准、变量选择和迁移流预测中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列面板预测方法通常依赖时间邻近性，但当相似的输入-输出模式发生在非相邻时间点，或近期面板与目标面板差异较大时，这类方法会失效。因此需要一种能够跨越时间距离、基于预测兼容性来利用历史信息的新框架。

Method: 提出 State-Local Prediction (StaLoP) 框架：(1) 用目标局部状态向量表示各面板；(2) 通过经验差异分数比较历史面板与目标面板，确定目标点的相关性权重；(3) 将这些权重与协变量局部化相结合进行预测；(4) 提出目标局部 GDF 校正的 MSPE 准则用于面板和模型选择。

Result: 理论层面建立了偏差-方差分解特征、渐近正态性、联合预测带以及模型选择准则；大量模拟验证了 StaLoP 的性能及其理论性质；在序列预测、模拟器校准、变量选择和县际迁移流预测四个应用中均展现出更优的样本外预测能力。

Conclusion: StaLoP 通过目标局部预测兼容性有效克服了传统时间邻近方法的局限，理论和实验均证明其在动态面板预测中的优势，并为相关应用领域提供了科学洞察。

Abstract: Prediction from sequential panel data is central to digital-twin modeling, where new panels arrive over time and the predictive system is updated sequentially. Existing methods often rely on temporal proximity, which can fail when similar input-output patterns recur at nonadjacent times or when recent panels differ from the target panel. We propose State-Local Prediction (StaLoP), a nonparametric dynamic panel prediction framework that utilizes information through target-local predictive compatibility. StaLoP represents panels using target-local state vectors, compares historical and target panels via empirical discrepancy scores to determine relevance weights for the target point, and combines these weights with covariate localization. Theoretical results, including bias-variance characterization, asymptotic normality, simultaneous prediction bands, and a target-local-GDF-corrected MSPE criterion for panel and model selection, are developed. Extensive simulations validate the performance of StaLoP and support its theoretical properties. Applications to sequence prediction, simulator calibration, variable selection, and county-to-county migration-flow forecasting demonstrate improved out-of-sample prediction and provide scientific insights into the underlying applications.

</details>


### [331] [Quantile regression with measurement errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00188)
*Mushan Li,Yanyuan Ma,Liqun Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量含正态测量误差的一般分位数回归模型，提出了一种新的估计方法，通过核平滑和复数域矩生成函数技巧，首次在一般框架下获得了根-n一致且渐近正态的估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分位数回归中协变量存在测量误差时，由于分位数回归的固有非线性和检验函数的非连续性，构造一致估计量极具挑战。现有方法通常只能处理线性模型，或要求同时估计多个分位数水平，缺乏适用于一般线性与非线性分位数回归的统一框架。

Method: 作者采用核平滑技术解决分位数回归中检验函数的非连续性问题，并将问题拓展至复数域，利用矩生成函数克服非线性带来的困难，从而构造出一个无需同时施加多分位数水平约束的统一估计量。

Result: 所提估计量在温和条件下具有根-n一致性和渐近正态性。数值模拟和2024年日本樱花开花时间的实际数据示例验证了方法的有效性。这是正态测量误差下一般分位数回归问题中的首个一致估计量。

Conclusion: 该方法为含正态测量误差的线性和非线性分位数回归提供了一个统一、理论上良好的一阶渐近有效解决方案，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: We devise a novel estimator for a general quantile regression model with normal measurement errors in the covariates. The method is applicable to both linear and nonlinear quantile regressions and does not impose the quantile requirement on multiple quantile levels simultaneously. We circumvent the difficulties caused by discontinuity in quantile regression through kernel smoothing, and overcome the nonlinearity inherent in quantile regression via considering extension to the complex domain and moment generating functions. We show that the resulting estimator achieves the standard root-$n$ consistency and asymptotic normality under mild conditions. The performance of the proposed method is illustrated via numerical simulations and a real data example related to Cherry Blossom times in Japan in 2024. This is the first consistent estimator in a general quantile regression problem with normal measurement errors.

</details>


### [332] [Causal Inference for All: Marginal Estimands for Outcomes Truncated by Death](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00222)
*Ruixuan Zhao,Mats Stensrud,Linbo Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对纵向研究中因死亡而截断的结局，提出了一类新的估计目标——单世界边际可分离效应，该估计量面向全人群、具有因果可解释性，并能充分利用纵向数据。作者开发了识别与估计方法，并在前列腺癌试验的再分析中展示了不同估计量可能导致不同治疗结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向研究中，感兴趣的结局常因死亡而被截断（即仅在存活条件下可观测或有定义）。现有策略面临两难困境：幸存者平均因果效应（SACE）等因果可解释的估计量仅针对潜在亚组；而"存活期间"（while-alive）和复合终点等策略虽适用于全人群，但难以解释为针对非死亡结局的因果效应。因此，需要一种既面向全人群、又保持因果可解释性、还能充分利用纵向数据的新方法。

Method: 作者提出了一类新的估计目标框架——**单世界边际可分离效应**（single-world marginal separable effects），该效应将条件可分离效应推广为全人群汇总指标。具体而言，通过分离假设（separable effects framework），将治疗对非死亡结局的效应与对死亡结局的效应进行解耦，从而在全人群层面获得因果可解释的边际效应。论文进一步开发了这些估计量的识别条件（identification）和估计方法（estimation），包括利用纵向重复测量数据来加强推断。

Result: 在前列腺癌试验的再分析中，该方法展示了其实用价值：不同的估计目标（如SACE、while-alive策略与所提出的边际可分离效应）可能得出不同的治疗结论，凸显了审慎选择估计目标的重要性。所提出的方法能够利用纵向数据提供更为丰富且因果可解释的全人群效应估计。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入单世界边际可分离效应，成功解决了因死亡截断结局研究中的因果推断困境。该框架在全人群范围内提供了因果可解释的效应估计，并充分利用纵向数据的信息优势。实证结果表明，不同估计量会导致不同的治疗结论，强调了在临床研究中明确定义和选择估计目标的关键性。

Abstract: In longitudinal studies, outcomes of interest are often truncated by death, meaning that they are only observed or well-defined conditional on intercurrent events such as survival. Existing strategies face a trade-off: causally interpretable estimands, such as survivor average causal effects, target a latent subgroup, whereas while-alive and composite summaries apply to the full population but are difficult to interpret as causal effects on the non-mortality outcome. We address these challenges by introducing methodology for a new set of estimands that (i) concern the entire population, (ii) remain causally interpretable, and (iii) leverage the longitudinal data commonly available in studies with outcomes truncated by death. The set of estimands includes single-world marginal separable effects that generalize conditional separable effects to full-population summaries. We develop identification and estimation results for these estimands and apply the methodology in a reanalysis of a prostate cancer trial, highlighting how different estimands can yield different treatment conclusions.

</details>


### [333] [Robust Estimation and Inference with Selective Borrowing in Hybrid Controlled Trials: A Tutorial with SelectiveIntegrative and intFRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00350)
*Ke Zhu,Hairong Huang,Shu Yang,Xiaofei Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为混合对照试验（HCT）提供了一套实用的统计分析工作流程，涵盖估计目标、识别假设、匹配策略、借用法则以及渐近推断与随机化检验，并通过SelectiveIntegrative和intFRT两个R包在模拟肺癌数据集上进行了分步演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验常面临样本量有限、入组缓慢或伦理限制，引入外部对照可提升统计效率，但协变量偏移和结局漂移若调整不当会引入偏倚、损害推断效度，因此亟需一套透明、可复现的HCT分析实践指南。

Method: 本文提出了一个完整的HCT分析路线图：明确估计目标与识别假设，进行资格对齐与匹配，采用完全借用或选择性借用策略，并辅以渐近推断和基于随机化检验的推断方法。实现层面则利用SelectiveIntegrative包和intFRT包进行逐步操作，并在模拟CALGB 9633试验及国家癌症数据库外部对照的合成肺癌数据集上做演示。

Result: 通过合成肺癌数据的完整案例演示，展示了如何在保证推断有效性的前提下，利用外部对照提升统计效率，并给出了可操作的代码实现与分析输出。

Conclusion: 该教程帮助应用统计学家开展透明、可解释、可复现的HCT分析，在维持推断效度的同时有效提升效率，为混合对照试验的规范实施提供了实用参考。

Abstract: Hybrid controlled trials (HCTs) augment randomized controlled trials (RCTs) with external controls (ECs) to improve statistical efficiency when RCTs face limited sample sizes, slow accrual, or ethical constraints. However, valid use of ECs requires careful adjustment for covariate shift and outcome drift, as inappropriate borrowing may introduce bias and compromise inference. This tutorial provides a practical workflow for estimation and inference in HCTs. We first present a statistical analysis roadmap covering estimands, identification assumptions, eligibility alignment, matching, full and selective borrowing strategies, and both asymptotic inference and randomization tests. We then demonstrate step-by-step implementation using the SelectiveIntegrative and intFRT packages. The workflow is illustrated using a synthetic lung cancer dataset included in the intFRT package that mimics the CALGB 9633 trial and ECs from the National Cancer Database. The tutorial aims to help applied statisticians conduct transparent, interpretable, and reproducible HCT analyses that improve efficiency while maintaining valid inference.

</details>


### [334] [Confidence Intervals for the Risk Difference in Combined Unilateral and Bilateral Data Incorporating a Distribution-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00373)
*Jia Zhou,Chang-Xing Ma*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对配对器官研究中常见的联合单侧与双侧二元结局，本文提出了一种基于风险差估计量概率分布的置信区间方法，以及修正的MOVER程序，以更好地捕捉小样本下的分布偏态特征，并通过模拟研究和真实数据验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险差置信区间方法主要依赖渐近正态性假设，在小样本情况下无法充分反映抽样分布的偏态特征，导致区间估计不够准确。因此需要一种能更好刻画有限样本分布特性的置信区间方法。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) 基于风险差估计量概率分布的分布型置信区间（distribution-based CI）；(2) 考虑受试者内部相关性的修正MOVER（Method of Variance Estimates Recovery）程序。通过广泛的模拟研究，在不同参数设置下与常用渐近方法进行覆盖概率和区间宽度比较，并在两个真实数据集上进行应用验证。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在大样本下所有方法均表现良好；所提出的分布型置信区间在保持名义覆盖概率的同时，区间宽度与现有方法相当；在小样本下，能够捕捉到渐近方法未能反映的抽样分布偏态。两个真实数据分析中，各方法得出了一致的推断结论。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布型方法为涉及联合单侧与双侧二元结局研究中风险差的区间估计提供了有效的替代框架，尤其在小样本场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Combined unilateral and bilateral binary outcomes frequently arise in studies involving paired organs. The risk difference is a clinically interpretable measure for comparing treatment effects between groups. Existing confidence interval methods are primarily based on asymptotic normality and may fail to adequately reflect finite-sample distributional features, particularly skewness. To address this issue, we propose a distribution-based confidence interval derived from the probability distribution of the risk difference estimator and a modified MOVER procedure that accounts for intra-subject correlation. Their performances are compared with those of commonly used asymptotic methods through extensive simulation studies. Across a broad range of parameter settings, all methods exhibited satisfactory performance as sample size increased. The proposed distribution-based interval achieved coverage probabilities close to the nominal level with interval widths comparable to those of existing procedures. In small sample settings, it was able to capture skewness in the sampling distribution that was not reflected by methods relying on asymptotic normality. Analyses of two real-world datasets demonstrated the practical applicability of the competing methods and yielded consistent inferential conclusions. The proposed approach provides an alternative framework for interval estimation of the risk difference in studies involving combined unilateral and bilateral binary outcomes.

</details>


### [335] [Distributed Prediction under Heterogeneity with Unidentifiable Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00376)
*Erbo Li,Zhaojun Hu,Ting Wei,Yifan Sun,Liping Zhu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式半参数框架，通过自适应同质性追求（迹相似度惩罚）解决数据异质性，并引入invex松弛与多步局部更新算法克服非凸性和通信瓶颈，理论上建立了非渐近无模型预测误差界及两阶段极小极大最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式环境下，低维结构参数不可识别（如降维场景）导致严重非凸性，加之数据异质性和通信成本的挑战，使得协变量-响应预测问题极为困难，需要一种能同时解决这三重障碍的统一框架。

Method: 提出分布式半参数框架：(1) 利用迹相似度惩罚（trace-similarity penalty）构建自适应同质性追求，处理数据异质性；(2) 采用invex松弛技术将严重非凸问题转化为可全局最优求解的形式；(3) 设计多步局部更新算法，显著降低通信开销并保证稳定收敛至全局最优。

Result: 理论上建立了非渐近、模型无关的预测误差界；证明估计量达到两阶段极小极大最优收敛速率及更紧的无模型预测误差界；提供了算法收敛性和通信效率的理论保证。仿真实验和多中心医学实际应用验证了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式半参数框架有效整合了异质性处理、非凸优化与通信效率三大难题，在理论和实践层面均展现出显著优势，为分布式降维场景下的预测问题提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting a response based on covariates is a fundamental problem in statistics and machine learning. However, profound difficulties arise when the underlying low-dimensional structural parameters are unidentifiable, as typified in dimension reduction contexts. Specifically,estimating these non-identifiable parameters inherently introduces severe nonconvexity. In distributed settings, this difficulty is further compounded by the challenges of data heterogeneity and communication cost. To overcome these intertwined barriers, we propose a novel distributed semiparametric framework. We formulate an adaptive homogeneity pursuit utilizing a trace-similarity penalty to effectively address data heterogeneity. To resolve the ensuing severe nonconvexity and communication bottlenecks, we introduce an invex relaxation technique coupled with a multi-step local update algorithm, ensuring stable convergence to global optimality with significantly reduced communication overhead. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic model-free prediction error bound and prove that our estimator achieves a two-phase minimax optimal convergence rate and an sharper model-free prediction error bound. Furthermore, we provide theoretical guarantees for algorithmic convergence and communication efficiency. Extensive simulations and a real-world multi-center medical application validate the superiority of our method.

</details>


### [336] [How does academic performance affect self-efficacy? Interpretable modelling through latent academic achievement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00722)
*Sarah Lee,Matias Quiroz,Sally Cripps*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯条件模型，用于研究序数型学业成绩与连续型自我效能感之间的方向性关系，通过潜在学业成就作为预测因子进入自我效能回归，并结合贝叶斯变量选择识别相关协变量。该条件公式化设计使得能够使用部分塌缩的Gibbs采样器，在更新变量纳入指标时解析地积分掉回归系数，从而提升采样效率和变量选择性能。应用于澳大利亚儿童纵向研究数据，揭示了潜在学业成就与自我效能感之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的证据表明学业成绩对自我效能感存在方向性影响，但现有方法未能很好地同时处理序数型学业成绩测量与连续型自我效能感测量，且缺乏有效的贝叶斯变量选择机制来识别影响各结果的因素。

Method: 构建贝叶斯条件模型：将潜在学业成就作为预测因子纳入自我效能感的回归方程中；采用贝叶斯变量选择识别与两种响应变量相关的协变量；设计定制化的部分塌缩Gibbs采样器，在更新变量纳入指示器时解析地积分掉回归系数，以提高采样效率。

Result: 模拟研究表明，相比近期更一般的联合高斯copula回归公式，所提出的条件公式和定制采样器在采样效率和变量选择性能上均有显著提升。在澳大利亚儿童纵向研究数据上的应用揭示了潜在学业成就与自我效能感的关系，并发现两种结果在相关协变量范围上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该条件贝叶斯模型为理解学业成绩与自我效能感之间的方向性关系提供了有效的分析框架，其定制化采样策略在计算效率上具有优势，实证分析揭示了两种结果在影响因素上的异质性。

Abstract: There is increasing evidence of a directional relationship from academic performance to self-efficacy. We develop a Bayesian model for investigating this relationship when academic performance is measured on an ordinal scale and self-efficacy on a continuous scale. The model allows latent academic achievement to enter the self-efficacy regression as a predictor, while Bayesian variable selection identifies factors associated with either response. The resulting conditional formulation yields an interpretable regression characterisation of how latent academic achievement relates to self-efficacy. Furthermore, it enables a tailored partially collapsed Gibbs sampler that analytically integrates out the regression coefficients when updating the variable inclusion indicators. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed conditional formulation and tailored sampler improve sampling efficiency and variable-selection performance relative to a recent, more general joint Gaussian copula regression formulation. We apply the methodology to data from the longitudinal study of Australian children, a landmark national cohort study covering children's education, social and emotional wellbeing, health and family circumstances. The model and analysis shed light on how latent academic achievement relates to self-efficacy in Australian children, and reveal that the two outcomes differ markedly in the range of covariates associated with each outcome.

</details>


### [337] [Transfert learning and adaptive LASSO quantile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00847)
*Gabriela Ciuperca*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对分位数回归提出了一种基于双L1惩罚的知识迁移估计方法，利用源数据库的预估计量实现知识迁移。该方法具备相合性与稀疏性，在非高斯误差模型下依然有效，且计算速度优于标准自适应LASSO。文中还提出了相应的算法，并通过模拟和蛋白质三级结构实际数据验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分位数回归中，标准自适应LASSO等现有方法计算耗时较长，且在某些场景下性能受限。作者希望利用已有的源数据库信息进行知识迁移，以缩短计算时间，同时使方法在非高斯误差模型下依然适用，从而提升估计的竞争力和实用性。

Method: 提出一种基于迁移学习的自适应LASSO分位数估计方法（adaptive transfer LASSO quantile estimator）。具体做法是：先从一个源数据库获得初始估计量，再在目标数据上施加两个L1惩罚项来实现知识迁移，并设计了一个算法来高效实现该估计量的计算。文中还推导了估计量在不同场景下的收敛速率和渐近行为。

Result: 理论证明该迁移学习估计量满足相合性与稀疏性；模拟实验验证了理论结果，并显示所提出的自适应学习估计量比传统LASSO估计量更具竞争力；在蛋白质三级结构的物理化学性质真实数据应用中，进一步证明了该方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的迁移学习分位数估计方法实现了更短的计算时间，能处理非高斯误差模型，且比标准自适应LASSO和普通LASSO估计量更优，具有良好的理论性质和实际应用前景。

Abstract: We propose for a quantile regression an estimation method for transferring knowledge using two $L_1$ penalties based on an estimator obtained from a source database. The proposed transfer learning estimator satisfies the properties of consistency and sparsity. Its convergence rate and asymptotic behavior are studied in several scenarios. This knowledge transfer results in a shorter computation time than that of the standard adaptive LASSO estimator. Another advantage of our method is that it can be applied to models with non-Gaussian errors. In addition, in order to implement the computing of the adaptive transfer LASSO quantile estimator, we propose an algorithm. The simulations confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate that the adaptive learning estimator, calculated using the proposed algorithm, is more competitive than the LASSO estimators. Finally, we illustrate the practical utility of the proposed transfer learning estimator and algorithm using a real-data application involving the physicochemical properties of protein tertiary structures.

</details>


### [338] [Simulating Node Manipulations in Gaussian Graphical Models: The GGMNIRA Framework for Continuous and Ordinal Psychological Network Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00915)
*Yiming Wu,Fei Wang,Hongyun Liu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: GGMNIRA将NIRA算法从Ising模型扩展到高斯图模型框架，通过操纵节点的条件均值并利用KL散度量化网络分布变化，以评估节点在网络动态中的投影重要性，适用于连续和有序数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中心性指标仅反映节点的静态拓扑位置，缺乏反映节点对网络动态影响的理论依据；NIRA算法虽通过模拟操纵评估节点重要性，但仅限于二值数据且操纵参数的理论含义局限于精神病理学语境。

Method: 提出GGMNIRA算法，通过操纵高斯图模型中节点的条件均值，使用KL散度量化操纵前后网络分布的变化；围绕该算法开发了KL散度的相关性稳定性系数和非参数bootstrap差异检验，并通过模拟研究建立解释阈值；框架扩展至桥接高斯图模型和调节高斯图模型。

Result: 通过模拟研究为KL散度建立了相关性稳定性系数和bootstrap差异检验的解释阈值；算法适用于连续和有序数据，并能应用于多构念共病网络和调节效应情境；所有方法已集成至R包"GGMNIRA"。

Conclusion: GGMNIRA成功将模拟操纵逻辑从二值数据的Ising模型推广到高斯图模型框架，为连续和有序数据的心理网络分析提供了评估节点动态重要性的系统方法，并具备完善的统计推断工具和软件实现。

Abstract: Scientific Abstract: In psychological network analysis, centrality indices are commonly used to evaluate the importance of nodes within a network. However, centrality only captures the static topological position of a node, and there is no sufficient theoretical justification for assuming that it reflects a node's influence on network dynamics. The NodeIdentifyR Algorithm (NIRA) offers an alternative by systematically applying simulated manipulations to node intercepts within the Ising model to evaluate nodes' projected importance, but this algorithm is restricted to binary data, and the manipulated parameter lacks a clear theoretical meaning outside the context of psychopathology. To address these limitations, we propose the Gaussian Graphical Model NodeIdentifyR Algorithm (GGMNIRA), which manipulates a node's conditional mean and uses Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence to quantify the change in network distribution before and after manipulation, thereby extending this simulated manipulation logic to the Gaussian graphical model framework, which is applicable to continuous and ordinal data. Around this algorithm, we further developed a correlation stability coefficient and a nonparametric bootstrap difference test for KL divergence, with corresponding interpretive thresholds established through simulation studies. The framework was also extended to bridge Gaussian graphical models and moderated Gaussian graphical models, enabling its application to multi-construct comorbidity networks and to contexts involving moderation effects. All methods are implemented in the R package "GGMNIRA".

</details>


### [339] [An Instrumental Variable Approach to Account for Informative Treatment Switching in Real-world Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00980)
*Yang Liu,Andrew Ying,Zongqi Xia,Jue Hou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种工具变量方法，将治疗转换时的预期结局作为未测量混杂，在结构累积生存模型下识别因果效应。该方法具有双重稳健性，并通过基线生存校正的交叉拟合方法整合通用机器学习模型，用于多发性硬化症二线治疗比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床研究中，患者可能发生后续治疗转换（treatment switching），而转换时的预期结局信息往往记录不全，构成未测量混杂，导致常规方法估计偏倚。需要一种可重复、可推广的方法来原则性地处理这种信息性治疗转换，以准确评估初始治疗方案的因果效应。

Method: 1. 将基线治疗作为工具变量（IV），把转换时预期结局视为未测量混杂。2. 构建基于中心化IV与鞅型残差过程关联的估计方程，在结构累积生存模型下识别治疗效应。3. 方法具有双重稳健性：基线倾向模型或未转换结局模型之一一致估计即可。4. 在半参数可加风险模型下，通过治疗效应参数与生存结局回归模型的协同训练（co-training），无需观察无转换子集。5. 提出基线生存校正的交叉拟合方法，可整合通用机器学习模型估计讨厌参数。

Result: 数值模拟表明，在多种设定下，当一系列基准方法产生有偏或矛盾结果时，所提方法保持了有效性。应用于多发性硬化症二线治疗中高效疾病修正治疗与标准疗效疾病修正治疗的比较。

Conclusion: 该方法为信息性治疗转换问题提供了一个原则性框架，具有双重稳健性和机器学习兼容性，在复杂临床比较效果研究中具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Reproducible and generalizable assessment of treatment decisions requires principled handling of subsequent treatment switching that may inform expected outcomes and shift across cohorts and over time. To effectively account for informative treatment switching, we propose an instrumental variable approach that characterizes the poorly documented expected outcomes at switching as unmeasured confounding. After establishing the baseline treatment as a viable instrumental variable, we constructed an estimating equation based on the association between the centered instrumental variable and a martingale style residual process that identifies the treatment effect under structural cumulative survival model. Our proposed method is doubly robust, i.e., valid whenever either of baseline propensity model or no-switching outcome model is consistently estimated. A co-training of treatment effect parameter and survival outcome regression model eliminated the requirement of observing a no-switching subset under semi-parametric additive hazards models. We further developed an baseline-survival-corrected cross-fitting approach to incorporate general machine learning models for estimating nuisance models. Numerical results demonstrated the validity of our method in various settings when a basket of benchmark solutions produced biased or contradictory results. We applied our method to comparison of high-efficacy vs standard efficacy disease modifying treatments as the second line therapy of multiple sclerosis.

</details>
